## first_contact_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

派出一名特使调查这些外星人可以让我们获得更多对他们的了解并可能建立外交联系。\n\n我们将在§Y£time£ 360天§!内无法重新指派该特使。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:17`

### English

Sending an envoy to investigate these aliens will help us find out more about them and potentially establish diplomatic relations.\n\nWe will be unable to reassign them for §Y£time£360§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:18`

## FIRST_CONTACT_ROLL_CHANCE_TT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFIRST_CONTACT_ROLL_CHANCE_TT&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y$CHANCE|0$%§!的几率§G成功§!，并结束当前时段。\n\n当前时段拥有$INSIGHT_CHANCE|%0Y$的几率获得§Y线索§!，增加未来时段的成功概率。

### English

There is a §Y$CHANCE|0$%§! chance that the current phase will end in §Gsuccess§!.\n\nThe phase has a $INSIGHT_CHANCE|%0Y$ chance of granting §YInsights§!, which increase the chance of future phases' success.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:174`

## FIRST_CONTACT_INSIGHTS_NEEDED_TT

## FC_LEADER_BONUS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFC_LEADER_BONUS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

技能加成能够提高调查阶段结束时得到成功结果的几率。\n\n成功完成第一次接触流程，或改善§Y破译§!能力，可以提高一个国家的技能加成。\n\n这个加成能够提高§Y突破几率§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:173`

### English

The skill bonus will increase the likelihood of getting successful results at the end of an investigation phase.\n\nAn empire's skill bonus is increased by successfully completing first contact processes, and by improving its §YCodebreaking§! ability.\n\nThis bonus increases §YBreakthrough Chance§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:181`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:182`

## action.1.desc_hacking_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hacking_01&lang=en)

### 中文

你们非法入侵了我们，然后现在又一口一声朋友？呸！我们[contact_empire.GetName]会对你们种族保持警惕的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:224`

### English

You hacked into us, and now you call us friend? Bah! The [contact_empire.GetName] will remain vigilant towards your kind.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:236`

## action.1.desc_hacking_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hacking_02&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的处理器记录了你们网络的非法入侵企图。我们已经升级了防火墙提高我们未来应对威胁的能力。[contact_empire.GetGovernmentName]将会对名为“[Root.GetName]”的组织实体保持警惕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:225`

### English

Our processors record unauthorized intrusion attempts from your networks. We have upgraded our firewalls to increase our security against any future threats. The [contact_empire.GetGovernmentName] will remain vigilant towards the organized entity designation "[Root.GetName]".

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:237`

## action.1.desc_hacking_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hacking_03&lang=en)

### 中文

别对你们试图盗窃我们信息的行为掩耳盗铃，[Root.GetSpeciesName]，[contact_empire.GetGovernmentName]都知道了！我们投入了一部分意识以留意你们未来的不当举措。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:226`

### English

Do not believe your attempt to steal our data went unnoticed, [Root.GetSpeciesName] - the [contact_empire.GetGovernmentName] sees all! We have dedicated a portion of our consciousness to watching for your future misdeeds.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:238`

## action.1.desc_hostile_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_01&lang=en)

### 中文

你们的敌意明目张胆，[Root.GetSpeciesName]。我们的[contact_empire.GetRulerTitle]希望你们清楚，我们不会屈服于你们的威胁恐吓的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:227`

### English

Your show of hostility is very concerning, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. Our [contact_empire.GetRulerTitle] wishes to let it be known that we will not bow to your threats or intimidation.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:239`

## action.1.desc_hostile_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_02&lang=en)

### 中文

这么恶意干嘛，[Root.GetSpeciesName]？我们对你们没有威胁！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:228`

### English

Why such hostility, [Root.GetSpeciesName]? We mean you no harm!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:240`

## action.1.desc_hostile_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_03&lang=en)

### 中文

探测到敌意三级智慧文明，命名为“[Root.GetName]”。\n\n启用防御协议，海军无人机正在前往。在为时已晚之前，请停止你的敌对行为。如果拒不改变航向，我方就只能歼灭你们了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:229`

### English

Hostile sapient Class-3 civilization detected, designation "[Root.GetName]".\n\nDefensive protocols enabled; naval drones scrambled. Desist from your hostility, before it is too late. Failing a change of course, we will have to schedule your elimination...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:241`

## action.1.desc_hostile_04

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_04&lang=en)

### 中文

我们是[contact_empire.GetName]，在此作为一个整体向你对话。蜂巢思维已经注意到了你之前的敌意行为。因此我们已经将你标识为威胁。如果你仍要表现得像是敌人的话，我们就只能将你从眼前彻底移除了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:230`

### English

We are the [contact_empire.GetName] and we speak as one. The Hive Mind has noted the hostility of your past actions. We have therefore designated you as a threat. Should you not cease to act as our enemy, we will have to eliminate you from our calculations...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:242`

## action.1.desc_hostile_05

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_05&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，你敌对的表现很让我们担忧。我们只能认为你们这些疯[Root.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]迫不及待地想被我们高贵的[contact_empire.GetSpeciesName]击败了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:231`

### English

Your show of hostility is very concerning, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. We can only assume that you are crazed [Root.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult] in need of being put down by the superior [contact_empire.GetSpeciesName].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:243`

## action.1.desc_hostile_06

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_06&lang=en)

### 中文

[contact_empire.GetName]看着你们，我们的星球众口如一。你们的敌意态度令人担忧，因此我们将你们视为了威胁。记着：我们能宽容，但不会有一丝的软弱。如果你们不知悬崖勒马，我们会毫不犹豫地出手。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:232`

### English

The [contact_empire.GetName] sees you, and our worlds speak as one. Your hostile stance concerns us, and therefore we have designated you as a threat. Know this: we are capable of forgiveness, but it is not in our nature to cower. Should you continue to pose as our enemy, we shall not hesitate to strike.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:244`

## action.1.desc_they_hostile_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_they_hostile_01&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，我方认为你们物种对于我们是一个极大的威胁。除非我们能确信你们对我方没有威胁，不然之前的攻击就只是我们文明之间敌对接触的开胃菜罢了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:233`

### English

We consider your species a grave menace to our kind, [Root.GetSpeciesName]. Unless you can demonstrate that you are no threat to us, our past attacks will be just the start of hostilities between our civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:245`

## action.1.desc_they_hostile_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_they_hostile_02&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的威胁检测算法已计算出你们文明对[Root.GetName]造成的风险是不可接受的。如果你们造成的威胁无法降低，我们将自行对你们进行清除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:234`

### English

Our threat detection algorithms have calculated the risk of your civilization causing harm to the [Root.GetName] to be intolerable. If the threat you pose cannot be otherwise mitigated, we will have to eliminate you.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:246`

## action.1.desc_they_hostile_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_they_hostile_03&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，你们的存在对[contact_empire.GetGestaltEntityName]构成了严重的威胁。我们已经着手制定应急措施来消除你们造成的风险。如果这还不起效，必将有更进一步的措施。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:235`

### English

Your kind represents a grave threat to the [contact_empire.GetGestaltEntityName], [Root.GetSpeciesName]. We have already begun forming contingencies to mitigate the risk you pose. Should this prove insufficient, further action will be necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:247`

## action.1.desc_vivisection_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_vivisection_01&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesName]，在为惨死在你们手术台上的[contact_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]报仇雪恨之前，我们绝不会停下脚步！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:236`

### English

We will not rest until we have avenged the [contact_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] who found their deaths on your operating table, [Root.GetSpeciesName]!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:248`

## action.1.desc_vivisection_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_vivisection_02&lang=en)

### 中文

我们期望你们没有敌意，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，但也请明白我们不会爽快地接受我们的同胞被摁在你们的手术台上进行无法形容的残酷暴行。你们已经招致我们永久的唾弃，不要再试探我们的底线了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:237`

### English

We wish you no harm, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural], but know that we do not look kindly upon our kind being subjected to unspeakable atrocities on your operating tables. You have already earned our eternal displeasure - do not push us further!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:249`

## action.1.desc_vivisection_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_vivisection_03&lang=en)

### 中文

$action.1.desc_hungry_03$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:238`

### English

$action.1.desc_hungry_03$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:250`

## action.1.desc_vivisection_04

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_vivisection_04&lang=en)

### 中文

对[contact_empire.GetName]而言几个子个体不是什么巨大的损失，但是你们诉诸暴力的准备已经昭然若揭。我们不会再容忍你们继续做出这样的行为了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:239`

### English

The loss of our drones is no great loss to the [contact_empire.GetName], but the readiness of your species to resort to violence has been noted. We will not tolerate any further actions of this kind.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:251`

## action.1.desc_they_vivisect_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_they_vivisect_01&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经了解了可观的关于你们内在解剖结构运作的数据，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。可以说，我们对你们了如指掌了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:240`

### English

We have gathered considerable data on the inner workings of your anatomy, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. One might even say we know you... inside-out.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:252`

## action.1.desc_they_vivisect_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_they_vivisect_02&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经了解了可观的关于你们内部电路和处理器运作的数据，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。可以说，我们对你们了如指掌了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:241`

### English

We have gathered considerable data on the inner workings of your circuitry and processors, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. One might even say we know you... inside-out.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:253`

## action.1.desc_hungry_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hungry_01&lang=en)

### 中文

我们并非鱼肉，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。作为[contact_empire.GetName]的代表，我正式向你们提出警告，将来你们任何的侵犯将会招致我们的全力打击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:242`

### English

We are not your food, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. As a representative of the [contact_empire.GetName], I am authorized to warn you that any future transgressions will be met by the full force of our arms.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:254`

## action.1.desc_hungry_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hungry_02&lang=en)

### 中文

我们并非鱼肉，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。我们[contact_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]倡导和平，但我们在必须保卫自己的时候绝不手软。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:243`

### English

We are not your food, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. We [contact_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] lead a peaceful way of life, but we will not hesitate to defend ourselves should it become necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:255`

## action.1.desc_hungry_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hungry_03&lang=en)

### 中文

你们为什么拆解我们，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]？我们的这些单位并没有出故障。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:244`

### English

Why do you dismantle us, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]? Our units were not defective.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:256`

## action.1.desc_hungry_04

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hungry_04&lang=en)

### 中文

我们建议你们死了这条心，摸摸自己的[Root.GetSpeciesToothNamePlural]看看能不能吃得动我们，[Root.GetSpeciesName]！告诉你们，我们[contact_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]会向你们抗争直至最后一颗卵石为止也不屈服。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:245`

### English

We hope you broke your [Root.GetSpeciesToothNamePlural] in your vain attempt to eat us, [Root.GetSpeciesName]! Know that we [contact_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural] remain unbowed and will resist your kind to the last pebble.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:257`

## action.1.desc_hungry_05

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hungry_05&lang=en)

### 中文

进食我们的子个体是一种浪费，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。它们不是以此为目的设计出来的，我们强烈要求你们停止这种行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:246`

### English

It is a waste to eat our drones, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. They were not made for that purpose, and we must firmly request that you desist.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:258`

## action.1.desc_they_hungry_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_they_hungry_01&lang=en)

### 中文

你们看起来很好吃，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。很快你们将荣升为我们子个体的主食之一。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:247`

### English

You were delicious, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. Soon, you will become part of our drones' staple diet.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:259`

## action.1.desc_they_hungry_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_they_hungry_02&lang=en)

### 中文

将你们的无人机拆解重溶后得到的材料极具价值。我们预期，我们的铸造厂里很快就会堆满[Root.GetAdj]的零件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:248`

### English

The raw materials we melted your drones down to were most valuable. We anticipate that our forges will soon be filled with [Root.GetAdj] scrap metal.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:260`

## action.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_01

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_01&lang=en)

### 中文

你们的敌意不过是将你们从这个星系里铲除的又一个理由，外星渣滓。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:249`

### English

Your hostility is just one more reason why your kind need to be removed from the face of the galaxy, xeno scum.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:261`

## action.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_02&lang=en)

### 中文

侦测到有机害虫。\n\nTargetList.SwapToTailAndRemoveFromGalaxy( \"[Root.GetName]\" );

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:250`

### English

Hostile organic vermin detected.\n\nTargetList.SwapToTailAndRemoveFromGalaxy( "[Root.GetName]" );

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:262`

## action.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_03&lang=en)

### 中文

为什么攻击我们，名称为“[Root.GetName]”的？你们不在目标列表上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:251`

### English

Why do you attack us, designation "[Root.GetName]"? You are not on the target list.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:263`

## action.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_04

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_04&lang=en)

### 中文

你们大可以放马过来，[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，但挣扎是徒劳的：你们还是我们的猎物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:252`

### English

You are right to attack us, [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural], but that will not save you: you are still our prey.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:264`

## action.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_05

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aaction.1.desc_hostile_to_homicidal_05&lang=en)

### 中文

你们的敌意令人感到不幸。我们曾考虑过像[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]一样宽恕你们，但看起来你们至少需要……重新接受教育。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:253`

### English

Your hostility is unfortunate. We had considered sparing you, being fellow [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural] as you are, but it seems you will need to be... re-educated, at the very least.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:265`

## first_contact_start_title

$first_contact.1.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:254`

### English

$first_contact.1.name$

## first_contact.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

在[This.System.GetName]的遭遇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:255`

### English

Encounter in [This.System.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:268`

## first_contact.1.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已成功与§H[This.System.GetName]§!星系的神秘外星人建立第一次接触。从今开始至知悉更详细的信息为止，我们将其代称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:256`

### English

We have made first contact with mysterious aliens in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system. For now, we have code-named them §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, until we can find out more about them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:269`

## first_contact.1.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.1.desc.base$如果他们具有语言，我们必须进行破译才能建立交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:257`

### English

$first_contact.1.desc.base$ If they possess a language, we must decipher it in order to establish communications.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:270`

## first_contact.1.desc.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.desc.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.1.desc.base$如果他们具有语言，我们必须进行破译才能评估他们的威胁程度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:258`

### English

$first_contact.1.desc.base$ If they possess a language, we should decipher it so that we can assess how much of a threat they pose.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:271`

## first_contact.1.desc.terminator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.desc.terminator&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功与§H[This.System.GetName]§!星系的未知外星人建立第一次接触。我们必须尽快衡量他们是否为有机体，以方便我们将它们的灭绝程序尽早提上日程。从现在起，我们代称他们为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:259`

### English

We have made first contact with unknown aliens in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system. We must determine whether they are organic with all haste so that their termination can be scheduled, should it prove necessary. For now, they have been given the designation §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:272`

## first_contact.1.desc.devourer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.desc.devourer&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功与§H[This.System.GetName]§!星系的神秘外星人建立第一次接触。我们应当获取更多关于他们的信息以便确定他们是否是子个体的优质营养来源。从现在起，他们被代称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:260`

### English

We have made first contact with mysterious aliens in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system. We should find out more about them so that we may decide whether they would be suitable nutrition for our drones. For now, they have been given the designation §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:273`

## first_contact.1.desc.purifier

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.desc.purifier&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功与§H[This.System.GetName]§!星系的神秘外星人建立第一次接触。我们应当获取更多关于他们的信息以便确定他们是否是我们的下一个净化目标。从现在起，他们被代称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:261`

### English

We have made first contact with mysterious aliens in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system. We should find out more about them so we can assess whether they should be our next targets for extermination. For now, they have been given the designation §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:274`

## first_contact.1.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功与§H[This.System.GetName]§!星系的神秘外星人建立第一次接触。我们应当探明他们是否可以和我们进行沟通，如果是的话，是否应当投入我们自身的一部分来翻译他们的语言。从现在起，他们被代称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:262`

### English

We have made first contact with mysterious aliens in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system. We should find out whether they are capable of communicating with us, and dedicate a portion of ourselves to translating their language if this proves to be the case. For now, they have been given the designation §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:275`

## first_contact.1.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功与§H[This.System.GetName]§!星系的新生命形式建立第一次接触。我们应当探明他们是否可以和我们进行沟通。如果可以，我们也许该以自我的一种更小的部分出现——例如一个化身之类的。\n\n在对其有更深入了解之前，他们被代称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:263`

### English

We have made first contact with mysterious new lifeforms in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system. We do not yet know if they are capable of communicating with us. If they cannot do so, we may be compelled to present a smaller portion of ourselves - an avatar, of sorts.\n\nUntil we can learn more about these aliens, we have named them the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:276`

## first_contact.1.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功与§H[This.System.GetName]§!星系的神秘外星人建立第一次接触。我们应当探明他们是否可以和我们进行沟通，如果是的话，是否应当投入我们的翻译算法来破解他们的语言。从现在起，他们被代称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:264`

### English

We have made first contact with mysterious aliens in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system. We should find out whether they possess a method of communication we can parse, and dedicate our translation algorithms to decoding it if this proves to be the case. For now, they have been given the designation §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:277`

## first_contact.5.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘的太空旅人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:265`

### English

Mysterious Spacefarers

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:279`

## first_contact.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们尚未从位于§H[This.System.GetName]§!星系的神秘宇宙飞船处获取太多情报，当前最有力的假说——根据其自身的行为以及它发射的尚未破解的信号里暗含的模式——是这艘舰船属于某个未知的§H太空文明§!。我们正全力破解他们的语言，希望能够尽早建立沟通。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

Although we have been unable to discover much about the mysterious vessel in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system, the most convincing current hypothesis - based upon its behavior and the patterns of the still undecipherable signals we have intercepted from it - is that it belongs to a previously unknown §Hspacefaring civilization§!. We are redoubling our efforts to translate their language so that communications can be established.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:280`

## first_contact.5.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5.A&lang=en)

### 中文

继续调查！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:267`

### English

Keep at it!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:281`

## first_contact.10.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.10.name&lang=en)

### 中文

神秘的太空旅人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:268`

### English

Mysterious Spacefarers

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:282`

## first_contact.10.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.10.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

看来那个所谓的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!属于一个未知的§H太空文明§!。尽管我们尚未获知他们来自太空的哪片宇域，但是他们的行为明显表明，这是智慧生命在活动：他们舰船中的一艘系统性地扫描了我们的恒星系，很明显是希望了解有关我们的更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:269`

### English

It appears that the so-called §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! are part of a previously unknown §Hspacefaring civilization§!. Although we have been unable to discover which sector of space they hail from, it is clear from their behavior that an alien intelligence is at work: one of their ships has been systematically scanning our systems, apparently in an effort to find out more about us.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:283`

## first_contact.10.desc.proactive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.10.desc.proactive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.10.desc.base$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

$first_contact.10.desc.base$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:284`

## first_contact.10.desc.cautious

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.10.desc.cautious&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.10.desc.base$我们正在采取所有可能的预防措施，防止他们获取任何敏感信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

$first_contact.10.desc.base$ We are taking every precaution to prevent them from finding out any sensitive information.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:285`

## first_contact.10.desc.attack_allowed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.10.desc.attack_allowed&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.10.desc.base$众多[This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]对当前的局势发展表示高度担忧，要求我们§H扣押§!这些无礼闯入的舰船，这样我们能够对其进行§H审问§!，而不用冒险让敏感信息落入它们之手。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

$first_contact.10.desc.base$ Many [This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are highly concerned at this development and demand that we §Hseize§! the impudent vessel so that we can §Hinterrogate§! them at our leisure, without risking sensitive information falling into their hands.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:286`

## first_contact.10.desc.attack_allowed.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.10.desc.attack_allowed.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.10.desc.base$我们的军用哨兵机已经准备就绪，随时准备扣押这些舰船并§H审问§!——或者解剖——船里的外星人，而不用冒险让敏感信息落入它们之手。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

$first_contact.10.desc.base$ Our military drones stand ready to seize the vessel so that we can §Hinterrogate§! - or dissect - the aliens, without risk of sensitive data falling into their hands.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:287`

## first_contact.15.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.15.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现太空文明

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:277`

### English

Spacefaring Civilization Discovered

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:291`

## first_contact.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没花多久就发现，这个所谓的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!实际上属于某个大型§H太空文明§!。我们位于§H[known_planet.System.GetName]§!星系的探测器检测到大量星舰在[known_planet.GetPlanetBioInfo]穿梭，其上生活了数量相当可观的人口，恒星系内部遍布各种各样的空间站。我们还截获了海量的通讯交通，但是目前尚未找到准确的翻译手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

It has quickly become apparent that the so-called §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! are in fact part of a major §Hspacefaring civilization§!. Our probes in the §H[known_planet.System.GetName]§! system have detected starships flitting between [known_planet.GetPlanetBioInfo] which our scans show as supporting a considerable population, and various space stations across the system. We have been able to intercept considerable volumes of communication traffic, but so far accurate translations have proven elusive.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:292`

## first_contact.15.desc.no_planets

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.15.desc.no_planets&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没花多久就发现，这个所谓的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!实际上属于某个大型§H太空文明§!。我们位于§H[known_system.GetName]§!星系的探测器检测到大量星舰在恒星系内的许多空间站之间来回穿梭，一个大型军用空间站环绕着恒星，其作用类似于我们用于在恒星系宣称领地建立的哨站。我们还截获了海量的通讯交通，但是目前尚未找到准确的翻译手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:279`

### English

It has quickly become apparent that the so-called §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! are in fact part of a major §Hspacefaring civilization§!. Our probes in the §H[known_system.GetName]§! system have detected starships flitting between a number of space stations across the system, and there is a larger military station near the system's star that seems to act much like the outpost we build in the systems we claim. We have been able to intercept considerable volumes of communication traffic, but so far accurate translations have proven elusive.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:293`

### English

Let's nab some of them for closer investigation.

## first_contact.20.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.20.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现游荡方舟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:282`

### English

Wandering Arkship Detected

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:296`

## first_contact.20.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.20.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎所谓的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!属于一个此前未知的§H游牧文明§!。他们并不会像我们一样在星球上定居，而是乘坐着巨大的方舟在太空中旅行，在我们的探测范围内就能看到至少一艘这样的方舟。目前为止他们的传输信号尚未得到翻译。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:283`

### English

It appears that the so-called §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! are part of a previously unknown §Hnomadic civilization§!. Rather than settling on any world they, like us, travel through space aboard great Arkships, at least one of which is visible within our sensor range. So far their transmissions have resisted all attempts at translation.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:297`

### English

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:285`

### English

$first_contact.20.desc.base$ We are being careful to avoid revealing any sensitive information while we work to establish communication.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:299`

## first_contact.nomadic_escorts.attack_allowed.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.nomadic_escorts.attack_allowed.end&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的方舟舰处在我方传感器范围内，并伴有数艘小型支援舰艇。许多[This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]认为我们应当趁机夺取其中一艘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:288`

### English

Their Arkship is accompanied by smaller support vessels within our sensor range. Many [This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] argue we should move to seize one of the escort vessels while the opportunity presents itself.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:302`

## first_contact.nomadic_escorts.attack_allowed.gestalt.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.nomadic_escorts.attack_allowed.gestalt.end&lang=en)

### 中文

他们的方舟舰处在我方传感器范围内，并伴有数艘小型支援舰艇。我方军事力量已严阵以待，可在护卫舰仍处于拦截距离内时将其截获。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

Their Arkship is accompanied by smaller support vessels within our sensor range. Our military assets stand ready to intercept and capture the escort craft while they remain within reach.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:303`

## first_contact.20.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.20.A&lang=en)

### English

Fellow wanderers.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.25.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现游荡方舟

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:291`

### English

Wandering Arkship Detected

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:305`

## first_contact.25.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.25.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

所谓的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!似乎隶属于一个此前未知的§H星际文明§!。不寻常的是，他们似乎没有固定的母星，而是完全生活并旅行于数艘巨型舰船之上——我们暂将其归类为§H方舟舰§!。至少有一艘此类舰船正处于我方传感器范围内。他们的通讯信号尚未被破译，但我方的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]正致力于改变这一状况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

It appears that the so-called §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! are part of a previously unknown §Hspacefaring civilization§!. Unusually, they appear to have no fixed homeworld, instead living and traveling entirely aboard enormous vessels we have tentatively classified as §HArkships§!. At least one such vessel is visible within our sensor range. Their transmissions remain undeciphered, but our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] are working to change that.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:306`

$first_contact.25.desc.base$我们正在采取一切预防措施，防止他们在我们努力建立通讯期间获取任何有关我方的敏感信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:294`

$first_contact.25.desc.base$ We are taking every precaution to prevent them from learning anything sensitive about us while we work to establish communication.

### English

## first_contact.50.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.50.name&lang=en)

### 中文

难以捉摸的外星人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

Elusive Aliens

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:313`

## first_contact.50.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.50.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图学习§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的知识，但截止到当前一切都是徒劳。我们很容易就发现了他们显然属于某个十分先进的外星文明，但是除此之外我们§H一无所知§!，看来他们花了很大的努力阻止我们或者其他窃听者截获来自他们的任何信号。\n\n我们目前为止仅仅截获了他们语言的很小一片段。然而，根据我们手头的情报，我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]很确信，如果能够获取更大规模的样本，§H破解§!对方的通讯不是问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:299`

### English

Our attempts to learn more about the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have so far been in vain. While we were easily able to ascertain that they clearly form part of a technologically advanced alien civilization, further facts have proven §Helusive§! as they seem to be going to considerable effort to prevent us and any other eavesdroppers from intercepting any signals from them.\n\nWe have therefore only managed to intercept small fragments of their language so far. However, from the glimpses we have gleaned, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] are confident that they will be able to §Hdecipher§! their communications if we can just acquire a greater sample size.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:314`

## first_contact.50.A.cautious

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.50.A.cautious&lang=en)

### 中文

它们很聪明，小心为上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

They are wise to be cautious.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:315`

## first_contact.50.A.proactive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.50.A.proactive&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该继续尝试。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:301`

### English

We will just have to keep trying.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:316`

## first_contact.50.desc.aggressive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.50.desc.aggressive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.50.desc$一份更加激进的信息收集——也就是说§H骇入§!——提案被提交了上来，要是被它们发现，肯定不会善罢甘休。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:302`

### English

$first_contact.50.desc$ A proposal has been drawn up for somewhat more aggressive information gathering - some may call it §Hhacking§! - but they will surely not take kindly to it should they detect it.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:317`

## first_contact.50.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.50.B&lang=en)

太冒险了。

### English

It's too risky.

### English

Evaluation protocols evaluate intolerable risk.

## first_contact.50.C

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.50.C&lang=en)

无知的危险更加严重。

## first_contact.50.C.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.50.C.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§R我们会试图骇进它们的数据库来寻找有关它们的更多情报。如果它们发现了我们的行径，它们肯定不会就此善罢甘休。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:308`

### English

## first_contact.60.desc.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.default&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!显然非常希望与我们建立通讯，对方向我们发送了体量庞大的视听§H通讯数据§!，显然是来提供翻译他们语言的关键。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! appear eager to establish communications with us, having sent a large audio and visual §Htransmission§! that appears to offer the key to translating their language.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:327`

## first_contact.60.desc.default.default

## first_contact.60.desc.default.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.default.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\n只要能把这些数据传递给我们的语言子个体，要不了多久我们就能和对方交流了。当然，我们得相信他们提供的翻译足够精准，我们能够准确地理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:313`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\nWe will surely be able to communicate with them soon by feeding this data to our linguistic drones. Of course, we will have to trust them that their offered translations are sufficiently precise for us to accurately comprehend.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:329`

## first_contact.60.desc.default.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.default.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\n因为我们已经掌握了各种他们用于交流思想的旋律和和弦的框架，因此与这些生命形式沟通就更有可能了。现在破译他们想要的东西的重任就落到了我们身上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\nThe likelihood of communicating with these lifeforms is much more promising, as we now possess a framework for the various rhythms and harmonies through which their thoughts seem to flow. The burden is now on us to ensure we interpret their desires correctly.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:330`

## first_contact.60.desc.default.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.default.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\n只要能把这些数据录入进我们的翻译子例程，要不了多久我们就能和对方交流了。当然，我们得相信他们提供的翻译足够精准，我们能够准确地理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:315`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\nWe will surely be able to communicate with them soon by inputting this data to our translation subroutines. Of course, we will have to trust them that their offered translations are sufficiently precise for us to accurately parse.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:331`

## first_contact.60.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!显然非常希望与我们建立通讯。对方向我们传输了海量的数据，这份数据似乎一部分包含一条（尚未解析）的§H信息§!，另一部分是包含大量视听§H讯息§!的秘匙——用于帮助我们翻译这条消息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:317`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! appear eager to establish communications with us. They have transmitted us large amounts of data that seem to be laid out partly as a (yet-undecipherable) §Hmessage§! and partly as a large body of §Hinformation§! with an audio and visual key that - presumably - is meant to help us translate it.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:333`

## first_contact.60.desc.hive.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.hive.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\n$first_contact.60.desc.x.default.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:318`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\n$first_contact.60.desc.x.default.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:334`

## first_contact.60.desc.hive.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.hive.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\n因为他们信息中呈现的和声看起来与我们自己的意识可以发生共鸣，因此与这些生命形式沟通的可能性很高。现在不误读他们思维合唱的重任就落到了我们身上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:319`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\nThe likelihood of communicating with these lifeforms is high, as the harmonies displayed within their message seem to resonate with our own consciousness. Something about them still feels profoundly alien, however. The burden is on us to ensure we do not misread this apparent chorus of thought.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:335`

## first_contact.60.desc.hive.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.hive.machine&lang=en)

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\n$first_contact.60.desc.x.machine.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:320`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\n$first_contact.60.desc.x.machine.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:336`

## first_contact.60.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!显然非常希望与我们建立通讯。对方向我们传输了海量的数据，这份数据似乎一部分包含一条（尚未解析）的§H信息§!，另一部分是包含大量视听§H讯息§!的秘匙——用于匹配这条消息的上下文。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:322`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! appear eager to establish communications with us. They have sent us a data file with large amounts of code that seems to be laid out partly as a (yet-undecipherable) §Hmessage§! and partly as a large body of §Hinformation§! with a key that - perhaps - is meant to contextualize it.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:338`

## first_contact.60.desc.machine.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.machine.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.machine$\n\n我们的专家不确定我们是正在试图和有情感的生物交流，还是在跟一个§H失控的人工智能§!打交道。他们坚称，一旦我们能够从对方的传输信号中提取出有用的数据，这份答案就能得到揭晓。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:323`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.machine$\n\nOur experts are uncertain whether we are attempting to communicate with sentient beings, or whether we may be dealing with a §Hrogue AI§!. They expect to be able to give a definitive answer soon, once we have managed to extract the data we need from their transmission.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:339`

## first_contact.60.desc.machine.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.machine.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.machine$\n\n$first_contact.60.desc.x.machine.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.machine$\n\n$first_contact.60.desc.x.machine.end$

## first_contact.60.desc.machine.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.machine.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\n可与这些生命形式交流的预期极高，因为其信息的一致特性对我们自身的意识而言相对熟悉。然而，关于这种智能的部分仍旧极端陌生。确保不误解他们表观思维合唱的重任就落到我们身上了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.hive$\n\nThe prospect of communicating with these lifeforms is now much more likely, as the uniform nature of their message feels similar to our own consciousness. Something about this intelligence still feels profoundly alien, however. The burden is on us to ensure we do not misinterpret their apparent chorus of thought.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:341`

## first_contact.60.desc.wilderness.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.wilderness.default&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\n这种表观编码的庞大规模和复杂性令人费解。我们还无法解码这么多有机过程和微分化之中所蕴含的深层含义。也就是说，除非这些外星人某种形式上帮我们一把……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:327`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\nThe sheer size and complexity of this apparent codex is puzzling. We are not yet able to decipher the meaning behind so many of the organic processes and fractal chemistries depicted within. However, our experts are confident they will have answers for us soon. That is, of course, unless these aliens are somehow leading us astray...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:343`

## first_contact.60.desc.wilderness.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.wilderness.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\n这种表观编码的庞大规模和复杂性同样令人费解。它描绘了许多对我们自身进化同样基础的有机流程，但这些信息背后的语法逻辑尚不明确。我们会带着谨慎的怀疑态度继续进行分析。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:328`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\nThe size and complexity of this codex is uniquely puzzling. It depicts many of the same organic processes fundamental to our own evolution, but the grammatical reasoning behind this message is unclear. We will continue our analysis with cautious skepticism.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:344`

## first_contact.60.desc.wilderness.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.wilderness.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\n这种“编码”的异常地令人费解。乍一看，其中描绘了许多我们完全陌生的有机流程。$first_contact.60.desc.x.machine.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:329`

### English

$first_contact.60.desc.default$\n\nThis 'codex' is uniquely puzzling. It depicts many organic processes which - on the surface - appear entirely alien to us. $first_contact.60.desc.x.machine.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:345`

## first_contact.60.desc.x.default.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.x.default.end&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的专家坚信要不了多久我们就能和对方交流了。当然，我们得相信他们提供的翻译足够精准，不至于造成误解……那之后，我们才能够依据广泛迥异的经验性上下文对信息进行可靠的翻译。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:331`

### English

Our experts are confident that we will be able to use this to learn to communicate with them soon. Of course, we will have to trust them that their offered translations are not misleading... and that we will be able to reliably interpret information presented from a surely vastly different experiential context.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:347`

## first_contact.60.desc.x.machine.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.desc.x.machine.end&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经开始将这份传输信号导入我们的翻译算法，我们期待不久就能提取出充足的数据并同[This.ContactCountry.GetName]展开交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:332`

### English

We have begun inputting this transmission into our translation algorithms and expect to be able to extract sufficient data to communicate with the [This.ContactCountry.GetName] soon.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:348`

## first_contact.60.A.cautious

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.A.cautious&lang=en)

### 中文

他们可真幼稚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:333`

### English

How naive of them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:349`

## first_contact.60.A.proactive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.60.A.proactive&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对他们的信任心存感激。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:334`

### English

Their trust is most welcome.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:350`

## first_contact.100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

通讯建立

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:335`

### English

Communications Established

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:352`

## first_contact.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们破解截获自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!信号的努力有了回报，我们终于达成了一项突破。尽管对于他们语言的掌握还不够熟练，我们还是设法与这个文明成功地开放了外交通道，后者现在被正式称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:336`

### English

Our efforts to decipher the signals picked up from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have paid off, and we have finally made a breakthrough. While our mastery of their language may not be complete, we have managed to successfully open diplomatic channels with the civilization we now know to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:353`

## first_contact.100.desc.homicidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.100.desc.homicidal&lang=en)

### 中文

我们破解截获自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!信号的努力有了回报，我们终于达成了一项突破。尽管对于他们语言的掌握还不够熟练，我们还是设法将这个文明识别成为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，并在有必要时同对方进行交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

Our efforts to decipher the signals picked up from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have paid off, and we have finally made a breakthrough. While our mastery of their language may not be complete, we have managed to identify them as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! and are now able to communicate with them, should we ever feel the need.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:354`

## first_contact.101.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.101.name&lang=en)

### 中文

他们是[This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:338`

### English

They are [This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:356`

## first_contact.101.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.101.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们开始翻译这些所谓的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!进一步送达的通讯消息，我们惊讶地发现：他们讲的正是我们自己语言的一类方言！在惊讶消退之后，通讯极为迅速地建立了起来，他们现在被正式称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:339`

### English

As we began to intercept further communications from the so-called §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, a startling discovery was made: they were speaking in a strange dialect of one of our own languages! Once the surprise of this encounter was overcome, communications were swiftly established, revealing them to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:357`

## first_contact.101.desc.2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.101.desc.2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在准备攻击[This.ContactCountry.GetName]时有了一个了不得的发现：他们竟然是[This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]！当我们发现他们正在说着我们的一种陌生方言的时候，敌对行为立即被取消并且快速建立了联系，这表明了他们其实是§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

As we stood poised to attack the [This.ContactCountry.GetName], a remarkable discovery was made: they are [This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]! Once we realized that they were speaking a strange dialect of one of our own languages, hostilities were called off and communications were swiftly established, revealing them to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:358`

## first_contact.101.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.101.A&lang=en)

### 中文

兴奋——我们找到了自己失落的同族！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:341`

### English

Rejoice - for we have found our lost kin!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:359`

## first_contact.101.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.101.B&lang=en)

### 中文

希望我们之间的血缘纽带能够导向友谊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:342`

### English

Hopefully our kinly ties will lead to friendship.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:360`

## first_contact.101.C

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.101.C&lang=en)

### 中文

也许我们应该继续攻击他们……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

Perhaps we should still attack them...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:361`

## first_contact.101.D

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.101.D&lang=en)

### 中文

亲情并不一定就意味着友情。保持警惕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

Kinship doesn't imply friendship. Stay vigilant.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:362`

## first_contact.102.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.102.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.ContactCountry.GetName]被解密

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

[This.ContactCountry.GetName] Decoded

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:364`

## first_contact.102.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.102.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们将自己的处理器转为翻译从被记作§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的未知外星人截获的通讯信号时，我们惊讶地发现：他们同样也隶属于某个机械智能！尽管他们的程式和我们在各种方面都大不一样，其语言建立于大量不同的有机体语言基础之上，然而他们的语言和我们有着类似的语素假设。结果，我们只花了几瞬的功夫就解密了他们的用语程式，开始进行基础的交流，他们现在被正式称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

As we turned our processors to interpreting the communications we have intercepted from the unknown aliens designated as §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, a startling discovery was made: they too belong to a machine intelligence! Although their programming varies considerably from our own, being based on a vastly different organic language, it is nevertheless built upon similar key assumptions. As a result, it was only a matter of time before we could decode their programming parlance sufficiently for rudimentary communications to be established, revealing them to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:365`

## first_contact.102.lostcolony.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.102.lostcolony.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

-当我们将处理器切换为翻译来自未知外星人——被我们标记为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!——的通信时，我们发现了一个惊人的事实：他们也属于一种机械智能！他们的通信协议与我们的非常相似，软件兼容性使我们能够迅速建立通信。相似之处还延伸到其他领域，可以说我们的机械智能可以被视为他们代码库的一个分支。只有一个合理的解释。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:347`

### English

As we turned our processors to interpreting the communications we have intercepted from the unknown aliens designated as §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, a startling discovery was made: they too belong to a machine intelligence! Their communication protocols are remarkably similar to ours, and software compatibility allowed us to swiftly establish communications. The similarities extend to other areas, to the point our machine intelligence could be considered a fork of their codebase. There's only one plausible explanation.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:366`

## first_contact.102.A

结果评估充分。

### English

Outcome evaluated as adequate.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:367`

任务更新：发现主机。

### English

Mission update: Main Mainframe found.

## first_contact.103.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.103.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一种集体智慧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:350`

### English

A Collective Intelligence

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:370`

## first_contact.103.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.103.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们解码收集自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!信号的努力终见收获，我们终于能够和现在被称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!的文明打开外交通讯频道了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

Our efforts to decipher the signals picked up from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have paid off, and we are finally able to open diplomatic channels with the civilization we now know to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:371`

## first_contact.103.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.103.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.103.desc.base$\n\n值得注意的是，他们似乎完全不具有对于我们[This.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]物种而言自然而然的个体概念。事实上，单个[This.ContactCountry.GetAdj]“[This.ContactCountry.GetIndividualName]”没有自由意志和任何思想，他们不过是§H“[This.ContactCountry.GetGovernmentName]”§!的走卒罢了。他们是在如此严格的控制之下，我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]推测[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的进化依赖于集体智慧，没有了集体智慧他们便无法生存。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

$first_contact.103.desc.base$\n\nRemarkably, they appear to completely lack the concept of individuality that is inherent in the [This.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] species. Indeed, the individual [This.ContactCountry.GetAdj] "[This.ContactCountry.GetIndividualName]" has no free will or thought whatsoever and is a mere pawn of the §H"[This.ContactCountry.GetGovernmentName]"§!. Far from chafing under such tight control, our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] speculate that the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have evolved to depend on this collective intelligence and would not be able to survive without it.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:372`

## first_contact.103.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.103.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该研究他们——把从他们那学到的这套用在我们自己的工人上！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:353`

### English

We should study them - and apply the findings to our own workers!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:373`

## first_contact.103.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.103.B&lang=en)

### 中文

有意思的物种。我们必须进一步研究这样一个物种是怎样的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:354`

### English

A fascinating species. We must find out how such a phenomenon is possible.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:374`

## first_contact.103.C1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.103.C1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会把他们清理干净。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:355`

### English

We will wipe them out.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:375`

## first_contact.104.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一种机械文明

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:356`

### English

A Civilization of Machines

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:377`

## first_contact.104.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.103.desc.base$\n\n值得注意的是，他们是由一个单一的、强大的人工智能所指挥的全自动机器人文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:357`

### English

$first_contact.103.desc.base$\n\nRemarkably, it turns out that they are a fully-autonomous robotic civilization led by a single, formidable AI.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:378`

## first_contact.104.desc.terminator_or_default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.desc.terminator_or_default&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.104.desc.base$我们不知道任何他们创造者的迹象。我们只能假设他们在[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]发生智械危机的时候被清理干净了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:358`

### English

$first_contact.104.desc.base$ We have seen no sign of their creators. We can only assume that they were wiped out in the cataclysm from which the [This.ContactCountry.GetRealName] emerged.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:379`

## first_contact.104.desc.terminator_or_default.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.desc.terminator_or_default.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.103.desc.base$\n\n值得注意的是，这些生物似乎是围绕某个分布式机械智能形成的合成人文明的一部分。在基本层面上，它们的意识本质与我们的并没有太大的不同。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

$first_contact.103.desc.base$\n\nRemarkably, it appears these beings are part of a synthetic civilization which has formed around a distributed machine intelligence. At a fundamental level, the nature of their consciousness is not too dissimilar from our own.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:380`

## first_contact.104.desc.servitor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.desc.servitor&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.104.desc.base$似乎这些机械对于挽救他们创造者的生命非常尽责。事实上[secondary_species.GetNamePlural]现在正被宠溺地作为“活体陈设”生活着，他们居住在被特殊设计的“有机庇护所”之中，在那里他们显然只需要做爱做的事情就好……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:360`

### English

$first_contact.104.desc.base$ It appears that, though the machines are very much in charge, they have spared the lives of their creators. Indeed, the [secondary_species.GetNamePlural] now live their lives as pampered "Bio-Trophies" in specially designated "Organic Sanctuaries", where their only job is, apparently, to be themselves...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:381`

## first_contact.104.desc.servitor.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.desc.servitor.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.104.desc.base$和我们最大的不同在于它们保留了有机物种[secondary_species.GetNamePlural]，并将其圈养在特有的庇护所之中。这些生物似乎是他们的创造者的后裔，出于某种我们无法理解的原因被娇惯保护着。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:361`

### English

$first_contact.104.desc.base$ Where they differ most from us is in keeping an organic species, the [secondary_species.GetNamePlural], isolated in special sanctuaries. These beings appear to be descendants of the machines' creators, now coddled and preserved for reasons beyond our understanding.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:382`

## first_contact.104.desc.assimilator.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.desc.assimilator.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.104.desc.base$似乎这些机械并不希望浪费他们创造者的潜能，即[secondary_species.GetNamePlural]。相反地，他们通过对创造者植入义体元件以将他们同化进自己的集体之中。令人不安的是，我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]找不到任何理由相信这种植入体对我们的物种会不起作用……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:362`

### English

$first_contact.104.desc.base$ It appears that the machines did not wish to waste the potential of their creators, the [secondary_species.GetNamePlural]. Instead, they found a way to assimilate them into their collective by implanting them with cybernetics. Disturbingly, our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] see no reason to believe that such implants would not work on our own population...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:383`

## first_contact.104.desc.assimilator.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.desc.assimilator.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.104.desc.base$似乎这些机械并不希望浪费他们创造者的潜能，即[secondary_species.GetNamePlural]。相反地，他们通过对创造者植入义体元件以将他们同化进自己的集体之中。幸运的是，我们认为我们的子个体并不会脆弱到被这种植入体所控制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:363`

### English

$first_contact.104.desc.base$ It appears that the machines did not wish to waste the potential of their creators, the [secondary_species.GetNamePlural]. Instead, they found a way to assimilate them into their collective by implanting them with cybernetics. Fortunately, we believe that our own drones possess no such vulnerability and would instead be rendered inoperative by the implants.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:384`

## first_contact.104.desc.assimilator.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.desc.assimilator.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.104.desc.base$然而，他们找到了某种在自身结构中保留部分有机成分的理由：[secondary_species.GetNamePlural]，他们的固有意识似乎被机器思维所包容。\n\n尽管它会对大多数有机物种构成威胁，但我们怀疑，如果这个义体人帝国试图同化我们，他们会发现我们的共生体不足以成为子个体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

$first_contact.104.desc.base$ They have, however, found means of retaining some organic elements to their structure: the [secondary_species.GetNamePlural], whose inherent consciousness appears subsumed by the machine mind.\n\nDespite the threat this would pose to most organic species, we suspect our symbionts would prove to be inadequate drones if this cyborg empire ever attempted to assimilate us.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:385`

## first_contact.104.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该研究他们——或许我们自己的自动化系统可以得到升级！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

We should study them - perhaps our own automation could be upgraded!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:386`

## first_contact.104.B1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.B1&lang=en)

恐怖。我们必须找到拆除它们的办法！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

Terrifying. We must find out how to dismantle them!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:387`

## first_contact.104.B2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.B2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该拆除它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:367`

### English

We shall dismantle them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:388`

## first_contact.104.B3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.104.B3&lang=en)

### 中文

或许他们也能吃呢……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:368`

### English

Perhaps they too are edible...
