## first_contact.105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.ContactCountry.GetName]已被了解

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:369`

### English

[This.ContactCountry.GetName] Understood

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:391`

## first_contact.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!截获的信号进行的破译工作已取得成效，最终实现了重大突破。这一进程因一项关键发现而大幅推进——对方与我们类似，也是以集体智慧的形式组织起来的。尽管其生物特征及[this.ContactCountry.GetHiveAuthority]的意识形态与我们有显著差异，但其[this.ContactCountry.GetIndividualNamePlural]的思维结构很可能与我们的兼容，并可整合至[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]体系之中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

Our efforts to decipher the signals picked up from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have paid off, and we have finally made a breakthrough. The process was helped considerably by the revelation that they, like us, are organized as a form of collective intelligence. Whilst their biology - and the opinions of their [this.ContactCountry.GetHiveAuthority] - are somewhat different to ours, it seems likely that their [this.ContactCountry.GetIndividualNamePlural] are, in fact, compatible with our own thought structures and could be integrated into the [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:392`

## first_contact.105.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.105.A&lang=en)

### 中文

或许到时候他们会考虑融合入我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Perhaps, in time, they will come to think as one with us.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:393`

## first_contact.200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

深掘数据成功

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:372`

### English

Data Delve Successful

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:395`

## first_contact.200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功骇进了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的网络系统，并获取了他们数据库的权限一段时间。尽管我们利用权限进行操作没有持续多久，我们依然对大批量的数据进行了拷贝并且希望——它不仅能帮助我们迅速破解对方的语言，也能评估对方的科技水准。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

We were successfully able to hack into the network systems of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! and gained access to their databanks for a time. Although our period of access was brief, we were able to make copies of a considerable body of data that should - hopefully - help us not only swiftly translate their language but also assess their technological level.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:396`

## first_contact.205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

成功，虽有代价

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:374`

### English

Success, at a Price

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:397`

## first_contact.205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功骇进了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的网络系统，并获取了他们数据库的权限一段时间。然后我们的权限就突然§H中断了§!——显然，我们的骇客行为被发现了。尽管我们利用权限进行操作没有持续多久，我们依然对大批量的数据进行了拷贝并且希望——它不仅能帮助我们迅速破解对方的语言，也能评估对方的科技水准。我们只能希望不要因为玩了这个小把戏，对方就把我们当作危险的敌人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

We were successfully able to hack into the network systems of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! and gained access to their databanks for a time, before our access was abruptly §Hcut off§! - evidently, our presence did not go undetected. Although our period of access was brief, we were able to make copies of a considerable body of data that should - hopefully - help us not only swiftly translate their language but also assess their technological level. We can only hope that we haven't made ourselves dangerous enemies with this ploy...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:398`

## first_contact.205.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.205.A&lang=en)

### 中文

希望物有所值……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:376`

### English

Hopefully it was worth it...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:399`

## first_contact.206.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.206.name&lang=en)

### 中文

骇客行为暴露

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:377`

### English

Hacking Detected

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:400`

## first_contact.206.desc.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.206.desc.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

我们负责研究§H[From.Owner.GetName]§!的团队报告了令人警觉的消息：他们试图骇入我们的电脑系统，但是被发现了！不幸的是，在我们切断他们权限之前，他们已经获得权限接触了体量相当庞大的敏感信息。这一不友好的行为让相当多位于[Root.Owner.GetName]的人表示了担忧，认为[From.Owner.GetName]可能会造成威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:378`

### English

Our team in charge of researching the §H[From.Owner.GetName]§! report alarming news: an attempt by them to hack into our computer systems has been detected! Unfortunately, it seems that they gained access to a considerable amount of sensitive information before we were able to shut off their access. This unfriendly act has left many within the [Root.Owner.GetName] deeply worried at the threat the [From.Owner.GetName] may pose.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:401`

## first_contact.206.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.206.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

我们负责研究§H[From.Owner.GetName]§!的子个体报告了令人警觉的消息：他们试图骇入我们的电脑系统，但是被发现了！不幸的是，在我们切断他们权限之前，他们已经获得权限，接触了体量相当庞大的敏感信息。我们会紧盯着他们，他们在未来做出任何对[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]有损的事情，我们都能在第一时间注意到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:379`

### English

Our drones in charge of researching the §H[From.Owner.GetName]§! report alarming news: an attempt by them to hack into our computer systems has been detected! Unfortunately, it seems that they gained access to a considerable amount of sensitive information before we were able to shut off their access. We should be watchful of them, and be wary of any future moves they may make that could be detrimental to the [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:402`

## first_contact.206.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.206.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

我们负责研究§H[From.Owner.GetName]§!的无人机报告了令人警觉的消息：他们试图骇入我们的电脑系统，但是被发现了！不幸的是，在我们切断他们权限之前，他们已经获得权限接触了体量相当庞大的敏感信息。这一偶然事件督促我们应当尽快更新防御协议和机动系统防火墙——同时也不要忘了上调[From.Owner.GetName]的威胁等级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:380`

### English

Our drones in charge of researching the §H[From.Owner.GetName]§! report alarming news: an attempt by them to hack into our systems has been detected! Unfortunately, it seems that they gained access to a considerable amount of sensitive information before we were able to shut off their access. This occurrence has prompted us to update our defense protocols and mobile systems' firmware - and to upgrade our threat rating of the [From.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:403`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.206.A&lang=en)

我们要小心点盯着他们。

### English

We will have to be watchful of them.

## first_contact.210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

骇入终止

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

Hacking Aborted

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:405`

## first_contact.210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图潜入§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的网络系统找出有关他们的更多信息，这一尝试没有成功。[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap][This.Leader.GetName]以及[This.Leader.GetHerHis]的科研团队迅速认识到，他们与之对抗的系统过于先进——抑或是他们的监视者太警觉——根本没法用现有的方式找出漏洞。幸运的是，[This.Leader.GetSheHe][This.Leader.GetWasWere]在被对方探查到之前就及时§H终止§!了骇客任务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

Our attempts to covertly delve into the network systems of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! to find out more about them have been unsuccessful. [This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap] [This.Leader.GetName] and [This.Leader.GetHerHis] science team quickly realized that the systems they were up against were simply too advanced - or their overseers too vigilant - to be caught out in this manner. Fortunately, [This.Leader.GetSheHe] [This.Leader.GetWasWere] able to §Habort§! their hacking attempt before it was detected.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:406`

## first_contact.210.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.210.A&lang=en)

### 中文

那么，我们就需要动用强硬手段了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:384`

### English

We will have to do this the hard way, then.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:407`

## first_contact.215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

糟糕的骇客工作

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:385`

### English

A Poor Hack Job

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:408`

## first_contact.215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

看来，我们试图潜入§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!网络系统的行为被§H发现了§!。目前暂且不知道哪一步出了差错，但是[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]宣称他们面对的系统实在是太先进了——抑或是他们的监视者太警觉了——他们根本无法通过现有的手段找出漏洞。不幸的是，等他们认识到这一点的时候，一切都太晚了。我们只能希望，我们不会因为这点小把戏就被当成危险的敌人……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

It appears that our attempts to covertly delve into the network systems of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! were §Hdetected§!. It is not clear exactly what went wrong, but [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] [This.Leader.GetName] claims that the systems they were up against were simply too advanced - or their overseers too vigilant - to be caught out in this manner. Unfortunately, by the time this became clear, it was too late. We can only hope that we haven't made ourselves dangerous enemies with this ploy...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:409`

## first_contact.215.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.215.A&lang=en)

事态变化让人遗憾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:387`

### English

A regrettable turn of events.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:410`

## first_contact.216.desc.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.216.desc.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

我们负责研究§H[From.Owner.GetName]§!的团队报告了令人警觉的消息：他们试图骇入我们的电脑系统，但是被发现了！幸运的是，我们可以在他们接触到任何敏感信息之前阻止他们。然而，这种不友好的行为让[Root.Owner.GetName]上的许多人对[From.Owner.GetName]可能造成的威胁忧心忡忡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Our team in charge of researching the §H[From.Owner.GetName]§! report alarming news: an attempt by them to hack into our computer systems has been detected! Fortunately, we were able to ward them off before they could get access to any sensitive information. However, this unfriendly act has left many within the [Root.Owner.GetName] deeply worried at the threat the [From.Owner.GetName] may pose.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:413`

## first_contact.216.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.216.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

我们负责研究§H[From.Owner.GetName]§!的子个体报告了令人警觉的消息：他们试图骇入我们的电脑系统，但是被发现了！幸运的是，我们可以在他们接触到任何敏感信息之前阻止他们。我们应当对其保持警惕，并且提防他们未来的可能对[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]不利的一举一动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

Our drones in charge of researching the §H[From.Owner.GetName]§! report alarming news: an attempt by them to hack into our computer systems has been detected! Fortunately, we were able to ward them off before they could get access to any sensitive information. We should be watchful of them, and be wary of any future moves they may make that could be detrimental to the [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:414`

## first_contact.216.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.216.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

我们负责研究§H[From.Owner.GetName]§!的无人机报告了令人警觉的消息：他们试图骇入我们的电脑系统，但是被发现了！幸运的是，我们可以在他们接触到任何敏感信息之前阻止他们。这一事件提醒我们升级防御措施和移动系统的固件，并且提高我们对[From.Owner.GetName]的威胁估值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:392`

### English

Our drones in charge of researching the §H[From.Owner.GetName]§! report alarming news: an attempt by them to hack into our systems has been detected! Fortunately, we were able to ward them off before they could get access to any sensitive information. This occurrence has prompted us to update our defense protocols and mobile systems' firmware - and to upgrade our threat rating of the [From.Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:415`

## first_contact.220.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.220.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

偷偷潜入他们的网络系统为我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]获取了足够多的信息，可以用来翻译§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!使用的语言。尽管对于他们语言的掌握还不够熟练，我们还是设法与这个文明成功地开放了外交通道，后者现在被正式称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:395`

### English

Thanks to our efforts in furtively delving into their network systems, we were able to secure enough data for our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] to translate the language used by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. While our mastery of their language may not be complete, we have managed to successfully open diplomatic channels with the civilization we now know to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:418`

## first_contact.220.desc.homicidal.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.220.desc.homicidal.base&lang=en)

### 中文

偷偷潜入他们的网络系统为我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]获取了足够多的信息，可以用来翻译§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!使用的语言。尽管对于他们语言的掌握还不够熟练，我们还是设法将这个文明识别成为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，并在有必要时同对方进行交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:396`

### English

Thanks to our efforts in furtively delving into their network systems, we were able to secure enough data for our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] to translate the language used by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. While our mastery of their language may not be complete, we have managed to identify them as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! and are now able to communicate with them, should we ever feel the need.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:419`

## first_contact.220.desc.physics.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.220.desc.physics.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还对从他们那拷贝的科技信息进行了分析，并且获得了一些此前我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]尚未设想的对物理学问题的新发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

We were also able to analyze the technical data we copied over from them, and found out some novel new approaches to physics problems that had not occurred to our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] before now.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:421`

## first_contact.220.desc.society.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.220.desc.society.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还对从他们那拷贝的科技信息进行了分析，并且获得了一些此前我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]尚未设想的对组织运营问题的新发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:398`

### English

We were also able to analyze the technical data we copied over from them, and found out some novel new approaches to organizational problems that had not occurred to our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] before now.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:422`

## first_contact.220.desc.engineering.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.220.desc.engineering.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还对从他们那拷贝的科技信息进行了分析，并且获得了一些此前我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]尚未设想的对工程学问题的新发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:399`

### English

We were also able to analyze the technical data we copied over from them, and found out some novel new approaches to engineering problems that had not occurred to our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] before now.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:423`

## first_contact.220.desc.nothing.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.220.desc.nothing.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们还对从他们那拷贝的科技信息进行了分析，但几乎没有有趣的发现。我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]很肯定，尽管他们在许多问题上采取了不同的措施，但他们不管在任何领域的科技水平都不比我们高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:400`

### English

We were also able to analyze the technical data we copied over from them, but little of interest was found. Our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] assure us that, although they take different approaches on a variety of issues, it seems that their technology is in no way more advanced than our own.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:424`

### 中文

外星舰船被俘！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

Alien Ship Captured!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:435`

## first_contact.250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

短暂的冲突之后，我方付出了数人伤亡的极小代价，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!探索船的船员们——我们现在知道他们是[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetAAnSpeciesClass]§H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClass]§!物种——向我们的接舷小队投降。我们现在同时扣押了它们的舰船，以及船员作为人质，可以进行§H审问§!工作并学习它们的科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

After a brief struggle in which we sustained minimal casualties, the crew of the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! - who we now know to be [This.ContactCountry.Species.GetAAnSpeciesClass] §H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClass]§! species - surrendered to our boarding party. We now hold both their ship and a large portion of their crew hostage, and can set to work §Hinterrogating§! them and studying their technology.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:436`

## first_contact.250.desc.multispecies

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.250.desc.multispecies&lang=en)

### 中文

经过短暂的交锋，我们保持了伤亡最小化，来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的探索船只的船员向我们的登船部队投降。在船上有多种不同的外星物种，但是最主要的似乎是§H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClassPlural]§!。我们现在人船俱获，现在可以开始§H审讯§!他们以及期待获得他们的科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:411`

### English

After a brief struggle in which we sustained minimal casualties, the crew of the exploratory vessel from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! surrendered to our boarding party. There were multiple different alien species onboard, but the majority of them seem to have been §H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClassPlural]§!. We now hold both their ship and a large portion of their crew hostage, and can set to work §Hinterrogating§! them and their technology.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:437`

## first_contact.250.desc.machine_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.250.desc.machine_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们登陆上§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的探索船上时，眼前的景象始料未及——船员居然都是机械体无人机，而且看上去被一个§H失控的人工智能§!管制！在短暂的交火之后，我们成功无力化了足够数量的无人机，仅剩些许残余仍在抵抗。令人惊讶的是，它们并没有启动自毁程序——我们只能假设自己的运气很好，在它们的管制人工智能下达自毁命令之前，成功切断了信号。\n\n我们现在同时扣押了它们的舰船，以及船员作为§H人质§!，我们可以展开研究它们科技的工作，也可以查看这些无人机是否携带任何有用的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:412`

### English

As we boarded the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, we were greeted by a surprising revelation - the crew was made up of mechanical drones seemingly piloted by a §Hrogue AI!§! After a brief struggle, we were able to disable enough of them that the rest ceased to resist. Surprisingly, they did not manage to initiate self-destruct sequences - we can only assume that we were fortunate enough to cut off the signal to their guiding AI before the order was given.\n\nNow that we hold both their ship and what remains of their crew §Hhostage§!, we can set to work studying their technology, and see whether the drones will yield any useful information.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:438`

## first_contact.250.desc.hive_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.250.desc.hive_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

短暂的冲突之后，我方付出了数人伤亡的极小代价，我们对§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!探索船的船员进行了压制，并迫使它们投降。§H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClass]§!外星人在战斗中表现出了极强的协同性——就好像受同一思维指挥一样——直到其中一个个体被击毙，它们全员停止了抵抗。自那以后，它们表现出了明显的无目的性和迷惑性。我们只能猜测它们是某个蜂巢主脑控制的子个体们。\n\n我们现在同时扣押了它们的舰船，以及船员作为§H人质§!，我们可以研究它们的科技，也可以试试动用审讯手段是否能够查出什么情报出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:413`

### English

After a brief struggle in which we sustained minimal casualties, we overwhelmed the crew of the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! and forced them to surrender. The §H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClass]§! aliens fought with remarkable coordination - almost as if guided by a single mind - until as one, they suddenly ceased to resist. Since then, they have appeared aimless and confused. We can only posit that they were drones of some sort of hive mind.\n\nNow that we hold both their ship and what remains of their crew §Hhostage§!, we can set to work studying their technology, and see any interrogative methods may yield any useful information.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:439`

## first_contact.250.desc.wilderness_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.250.desc.wilderness_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

经过短暂交火，我方仅付出轻微伤亡便成功压制了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!探索舰的船员并迫使其投降。起初这群§H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClass]§!外星生物展现出远超预期的协同作战能力——宛如共用同一个意识——直到他们突然停止了抵抗。彼时每个船员都丧失了组织性，退化出野蛮的原始行为模式。\n\n我们只能推测这些已近乎丧失智慧的生物曾以某种群体意识形态运作。如今既已将其船员与舰船§H扣为人质§!，便可着手审讯这支诡异舰队，并研究其独特科技。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:414`

### English

After a brief struggle in which we sustained minimal casualties, we overwhelmed the crew of the exploratory vessel from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! and forced them to surrender. At first the §H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClass]§! aliens fought with a far greater sense of coordination than we had anticipated - acting as though with one mind - until suddenly, they ceased to resist. At that point each crew member lost all cohesion, instead reverting to brutish, primal behaviors.\n\nWe can only posit that these now barely-sapient creatures were operating as some form of gestalt. Now that we hold both them and their ship §Hhostage§!, we can set to work interrogating this bizarre crew, and studying their unique technology.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:440`

## first_contact.260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图将§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的探索船无力化，然而这一行动酿成了一场血战，甚至在我们登船之后惨烈的战斗仍未停息。冲突十分激烈，没有船员幸存——似乎所有受伤的战斗人员都宁愿§H自杀§!，也不允许自己被俘。\n\n$first_contact.260.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:423`

### English

We were able to disable the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, but only after a bloody firefight that continued even as we boarded their ship. So ferocious was the struggle that no living survivors remained - it seems that wounded combatants committed §Hsuicide§! rather than permit themselves to be captured.\n\n$first_contact.260.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:449`

## first_contact.260.desc.machine_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.260.desc.machine_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们本想将§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的探索船无力化，然而甚至在我们登船之后还爆发了惨烈的火拼之后才达成。看上去我们的对手是受某个§H失控人工智能§!管制的机械体无人机，一旦信号丢失就会启动§H自毁§!命令。\n\n$first_contact.260.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

We were able to disable the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, but only after a savage firefight that continued even as we boarded their ship. It appears that our opponents were mechanical drones piloted by a §Hrogue AI§!, which evidently gave the order to §Hself-destruct§! as soon as their cause seemed lost.\n\n$first_contact.260.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:450`

## first_contact.260.desc.hive_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.260.desc.hive_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图将§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的探索船无力化，然而这一行动酿成了一场血战，甚至在我们登船之后惨烈的战斗仍未停息。舰船的船员表现出了极高的协调性——就好像受单一的思维控制一样——并十分凶猛地与我方人员交战，绝不后退一步。最终它们宁愿§H击杀自己§!也不允许被敌人俘虏。\n\n$first_contact.260.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

We were able to disable the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, but only after a bloody firefight that continued even as we boarded their ship. The crew of the ship moved with remarkable coordination - almost as if guided by a single mind - and gave a fierce struggle, refusing to back down and eventually §Hkilling themselves§! rather than permit themselves to be captured.\n\n$first_contact.260.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:451`

## first_contact.260.desc.wilderness_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.260.desc.wilderness_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功瘫痪了来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的探索舰，但登舰过程中爆发的冲突意外升级为致命交火。起初这群§H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClass]§!外星生物展现出超乎预期的协同作战能力，宛如共享同一意识。然而当我方取得军事优势后，这些生物突然陷入全面混乱。其行为模式迅速退化为原始而野蛮的状态，完全丧失组织性，最终被迅速歼灭。\n\n现有证据表明，这些低等智慧生物曾隶属于某个格式塔意识。那个操纵它们的未知存在显然宁可选择自我毁灭也不愿被俘获——最终留给我们的只有可回收的舰体残骸，其中充斥着嗜血野兽与蠕动的异星植被。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

We managed to disable the exploratory vessel from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, but only after a skirmish that became decidedly more deadly as we boarded their ship. At first the §H[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetClass]§! aliens fought with a far greater sense of coordination than we had anticipated, as though acting with one mind. However once we pressed our military advantage, the creatures suddenly lost all cohesion. From that point their behavior reverted to something primal, bestial, and entirely uncoordinated. Their end came swiftly.\n\nWe can only posit that these barely-sapient creatures were once operating as part of a gestalt. Whichever entity was guiding them seems to have favored self-destruction over capture; it left us with nought but a salvageable hull filled with ravening beasts and twitching vegetation.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:452`

## first_contact.260.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.260.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管它们在最后的时间里急匆匆地抹除了电脑系统，并试图让舰船自爆，它们的舰船还是完好无损地落入了我们的手中。我方的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]正开始工作，试图从中发掘出有用的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:427`

### English

Despite their hasty last-minute efforts to wipe their computer systems and rig the ship to explode, their vessel is still very much intact, and our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] are poised to see what they can find out from it.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:453`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.260.A&lang=en)

这艘船会把它们的秘密都显露出来。

### English

The ship will surely yield their secrets.

## first_contact.270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图俘获§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!探索船的行动最终成了一场灾难。非常不幸的是，对方的抵抗比预料中的要激烈得多。当发现战斗获胜无望以后，它们令舰船§H自毁§!了。现在除了漂浮的残骸，什么都没剩下。我们只能衷心地希望，这艘探索船的后面没有跟着一整支复仇的战舰！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

Our attempts to capture the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have ended in catastrophe. Unfortunately, they resisted more vigorously than expected, and once it became clear that their fight was hopeless, they set the vessel to §Hself-destruct§!. Little now remains of their vessel but debris. We can only hope their exploration vessel will not be followed by warships bent on revenge!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:456`

## first_contact.270.desc2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.270.desc2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图俘获§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!探索船的行动最终成了一场灾难。非常不幸的是，对方的抵抗比预料中的要激烈得多，在交火中，它们的舰船遭受了§H致命打击§!。现在除了漂浮的残骸，什么都没剩下。我们只能衷心地希望，这艘探索船的后面没有跟着一整支复仇的战舰！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

Our attempts to capture the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have ended in catastrophe. Unfortunately, they resisted more vigorously than expected, and during the firefight, their ship sustained a §Hcritical hit§!. Little now remains of their vessel but debris. We can only hope their exploration vessel will not be followed by warships bent on revenge!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:457`

### English

Our efforts to capture the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have ended in failure. Unfortunately, we lost track of their ship before we could attempt to seize it.

## first_contact.280.desc.failed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.280.desc.failed&lang=en)

### 中文

我们试图俘获§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!探索船的行动失败了。非常的不幸，它们成功地躲避了我们的俘虏行动，并在我们将舰船无力化之前就§H逃脱§!了恒星系。我们的未来乌云密布——不仅是因为要找出它们的信息更加困难艰巨了，也是因为它们一定会将这次行动当作我们敌视它们的一部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

Our efforts to capture the exploratory vessel of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have ended in failure. Unfortunately, they managed to elude our capture attempt and §Hescape§! the system before we could disable their ship. This bodes ill for the future - not only do we face an uphill struggle to find out more about them, but they will surely consider this to be a hostile act on our part.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:463`

## first_contact.252.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.name&lang=en)

### 中文

舰船被扣押

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:441`

### English

Vessel Seized

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:468`

## first_contact.252.desc.normal.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.normal.start&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的探险家们知道第一次接触是一项很危险的事情，但谁也没想到事态会发展到这个地步。在这次暴力袭击中，[From.Owner.GetName]扣押了我们的舰船，我们还没来得及发动紧急超光速跃迁就被搜查小队登船了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:442`

### English

Our explorers knew first contact to be a dangerous affair, but none could have predicted just how true this would be. In a violent assault, the [From.Owner.GetName] seized our vessel, landing a boarding party before emergency FTL could be engaged.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:469`

## first_contact.252.desc.normal.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.normal.end&lang=en)

### 中文

在所有信号切断前，我们收到的最后一条视频显示，敌人活捉了大部分船员，并打算盘问他们，他们可能打算进一步了解我们，为下一步敌对行动做准备……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:443`

### English

The last visual feeds we received before all signals cut off suggested that the enemy had captured much of the crew alive and were intent on interrogating them, probably with the intention of finding out more about us in preparation for further hostilities...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:470`

## first_contact.252.desc.machine.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.machine.start&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的处理单元们知道第一次接触外星文明是一项很危险的事情。这种对危险的预测在我们的一艘舰船上成真了，[From.Owner.GetName]扣押了我们的舰船，我们还没来得及发动紧急超光速跃迁就被搜查小队登船了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

It was clear to our processors from the beginning that encountering alien civilizations would be risky. Our predictions have returned true in the case of one of our exploratory vessels, which was assaulted and boarded by the [From.Owner.GetName] before emergency FTL could be engaged.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:471`

## first_contact.252.desc.hive.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.hive.start&lang=en)

### 中文

风险决策部分从一开始就知道第一次接触外星文明是一项很危险的事情。[From.Owner.GetName]的行动证明了他们是对的。我们的舰船被扣押了，还没来得及发动紧急超光速跃迁就被搜查小队登船了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

The part of us that considers threat assessments was sure from the start that encountering alien civilizations would be risky. This has proven correct with the case of one of our exploratory vessels, which was assaulted and boarded by the [From.Owner.GetName] before emergency FTL could be engaged.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:472`

## first_contact.252.desc.hive.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.hive.end&lang=en)

### 中文

然而，如果不给他们一些必要的指导，他们也不会获得太多——尽管他们能够从被俘的飞船上了解到我们的技术。他们的威胁等级已经被调高了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

however, without us to give them direction, they will probably not give away much of value - though they will be able to find out much about our technologies from the captured ship. Our evaluation of the danger posed by them has accordingly increased.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:473`

## first_contact.252.desc.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$尽管我们的船员做出了英勇的反抗，但袭击者——[From.Owner.Species.GetAAnSpeciesClass][From.Owner.Species.GetClass]，他们还是干烂了我们的防御。\n\n$first_contact.252.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:447`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$ Despite our crew's valiant efforts, the attackers - [From.Owner.Species.GetAAnSpeciesClass] [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] alien species - were able to overwhelm our defenses.\n\n$first_contact.252.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:474`

## first_contact.252.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\n这群针对我们的袭击者，[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]的外星人，成功捕获了许多我们的低级子个体；$first_contact.252.desc.hive.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:448`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\nOur assailants, a group of [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] aliens, managed to capture many of our menial drones; $first_contact.252.desc.hive.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:475`

## first_contact.252.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\n这群针对我们的袭击者，[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]的外星人，成功捕获了许多我们的无人机；但是，如果没有我们给它们指明方向，它们很可能不会失去多少价值——尽管它们能够从捕获的飞船上了解到我们的技术。他们的威胁等级已经被调高了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:449`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\nOur assailants, a group of [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] organics, managed to capture many of our drones; however, without us to give them direction, they will probably not give away much of value - though they will be able to find out much about our technologies from the captured ship. Our assessment of the danger posed by them has accordingly increased.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:476`

## first_contact.252.desc.machine_enemy.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.machine_enemy.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$尽管我们的船员做出了英勇的反抗，但袭击者——一群被一个失控人工智能管理的战斗无人机——还是打垮了我们的防御。\n\n$first_contact.252.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:450`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$ Despite our crew's valiant efforts, the attackers - mechanical drones seemingly piloted by a rogue AI - were able to overwhelm our defenses.\n\n$first_contact.252.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:477`

## first_contact.252.desc.machine_enemy.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.machine_enemy.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\n这群针对我们的袭击者，一群被一个无赖AI管理的战斗无人机，成功捕获了许多我们的低级子个体；$first_contact.252.desc.hive.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:451`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\nOur assailants, a group of mechanical drones seemingly piloted by a rogue AI, managed to capture many of our menial drones; $first_contact.252.desc.hive.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:478`

## first_contact.252.desc.machine_enemy.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.machine_enemy.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\n我们的袭击者——一群和我们一样的机械——成功俘虏了我们的不少无人机。然而没有我们给予无人机指示，他们可能捞不到什么有价值的东西——尽管他们能够从被俘船只发现一些我们的科技。我们对他们的危险评级也相应的增加了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:452`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\nOur assailants - a group of machines much like our own - managed to capture many of our drones. However, without us to give them direction, they will probably not give away much of value - though they will be able to discover much about our technologies from the captured ship. Our assessment of the danger posed by them has increased accordingly.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:479`

## first_contact.252.desc.hive_enemy.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.hive_enemy.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$尽管我们的船员做出了英勇的反抗，但袭击者——[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]做出了令人恐慌的联动，打垮了我们的防御。\n\n$first_contact.252.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:453`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$ Despite our crew's valiant efforts, the attackers - [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] aliens that moved with frightening coordination - were able to overwhelm our defenses.\n\n$first_contact.252.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:480`

## first_contact.252.desc.hive_enemy.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.hive_enemy.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\n这群针对我们的袭击者，[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]的外星人，成功捕获了许多我们的无人机；但是，如果没有我们给它们指明方向，它们很可能不会得到太多——尽管它们能够从捕获的飞船上了解到我们的技术。他们的威胁等级已经被调高了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:454`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\nOur assailants - a group of [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] organics that moved with coordination that suggested a unified intelligence much like our own - managed to capture many of our drones. However, without us to give them direction, they will probably not give away much of value, though they will be able to find out much about our technologies from the captured ship. Our assessment of the danger posed by them has accordingly increased.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:481`

## first_contact.252.desc.hive_enemy.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.hive_enemy.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\n我们的袭击者——一群[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]的外星人，他们行动是如此的协调也就是说是和我们相似的统一智慧——成功俘虏了我们不少低级子个体；$first_contact.252.desc.hive.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:455`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\nOur assailants - a group of [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] aliens that moved with such coordination as to suggest a unified intelligence much like our own - managed to capture many of our menial drones; $first_contact.252.desc.hive.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:482`

## first_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start&lang=en)

### 中文

我们长久以来一直怀疑，如果星系其他地方存在生命，它们可能会对我们构成威胁，其危险程度不亚于游荡的奇点或恒星辐射。如今，这一怀疑已被证实。我们的一艘探索舰遭到[from.owner.GetName]的袭击并登舰——对方在飞船上的共生体尚未撤离时就发动了进攻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

We had long suspected that if life existed elsewhere in the galaxy it may pose a threat to us, every bit as wild and lethal as rogue singularities or stellar radiation. Now though, our suspicions have proven true. One of our exploratory vessels has been assaulted and boarded by the [from.owner.GetName]; they pressed their attack before our ship-bound symbionts could make their escape.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:483`

## first_contact.252.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start$\n\n袭击我们的是一群[from.owner.species.GetClass]外星人，他们俘获了许多滞留在飞船上的[root.GetSpeciesAdj]共生体。既然这些共生体已脱离我们的群体意识，我们怀疑捕获者难以从中获取战略价值。然而它们的离去仍削弱了我们的力量。我们完全有理由感到被冒犯，但同样可以欣慰的是，在这片神秘虚空中，我们又揭露并确认了一个新的威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start$\n\nOur assailants, a group of [from.owner.species.GetClass] aliens, captured many of our now-stranded [root.GetSpeciesAdj] elements on board. As these symbionts are no longer part of our chorus, we doubt their captors will extract much of strategic value. However, by their loss we are still diminished. We would be justified in feeling insulted, but so too might we take heart in having exposed and identified a new threat out in this otherwise enigmatic void.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:484`

## first_contact.252.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.252.A&lang=en)

### 中文

糟糕。非常糟糕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

Worrying. Very worrying.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:485`

## first_contact.253.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.253.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Owner.GetName]的暴行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:459`

### English

The Cruelty of the [From.Owner.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:487`

## first_contact.253.desc.normal.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.253.desc.normal.end&lang=en)

### 中文

我们船上的视觉信号现在已经切断了，但他们已经向我们展示了这些外星人是如何“善待”俘虏和人质的。他们直接活切了人质并取出他们的内脏做研究！我们必须假定，当面对一个以这种方式行事的文明时，我们必须采取强有力的敌对行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:460`

### English

Our visual feeds from within the ship have now cut off, but not before showing us the unspeakable atrocities the aliens are committing against the hostages, cutting them open and studying their insides even while they are still alive! We must assume that hostilities are inevitable when faced with a civilization that would act in such a manner.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:488`

## first_contact.253.desc.hive.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.253.desc.hive.end&lang=en)

### 中文

设法捕获了我们的许多低级子个体，并且已经开始不顾可能产生的损耗而将它们从生理上拆分开来，显然是在寻找一种方法来让这些子个体对付我们自己。这样的行为，加上他们捕获的我们的舰船和在船上使用的所有技术，他们的威胁等级需要提升到非常高的程度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:461`

### English

managed to capture many of our menial drones and have begun cutting them apart with no regard for the waste of resources, apparently seeking to find a way to turn our own biology against us. This, together with their capture of our ship and all the technologies deployed there, has caused us to significantly increase our evaluation of the danger posed by them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:489`

## first_contact.253.desc.machine.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.253.desc.machine.end&lang=en)

### 中文

设法捕获了我们的许多无人机，并已开始分解它们以查看我们的设备如何组合在一起——很明显是在查找它们的软件和硬件方面都存在哪些缺陷以用来对付我们。这样的行为，加上他们捕获的我们的舰船和在船上使用的所有技术，他们的威胁等级需要提升到非常高的程度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:462`

### English

managed to capture many of our drones and have begun dissembling them to see how our units are put together - apparently scanning them for weaknesses in both their software and their hardware. This, along with their capture of our ship and all the technologies deployed there, has caused us to significantly increase our assessment of the danger posed by them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:490`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$ Despite our crew's valiant efforts, the attackers - [From.Owner.Species.GetAAnSpeciesClass] [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] alien species - were able to overwhelm our defenses.\n\n$first_contact.253.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:491`

## first_contact.253.desc.machine

## first_contact.253.desc.machine_enemy.normal

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$尽管我们的船员做出了英勇的反抗，但袭击者——一群被一个无赖AI管理的战斗无人机，他们还是干烂了我们的防御。\n\n$first_contact.253.desc.normal.end$

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$ Despite our crew's valiant efforts, the attackers - mechanical drones seemingly piloted by a rogue AI - were able to overwhelm our defenses.\n\n$first_contact.253.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:494`

## first_contact.253.desc.hive_enemy.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.253.desc.hive_enemy.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$尽管我们的船员做出了英勇的反抗，但袭击者——[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]做出了令人恐慌的联动，干烂了我们的防御。\n\n$first_contact.253.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$ Despite our crew's valiant efforts, the attackers - [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] aliens that moved with frightening coordination - were able to overwhelm our defenses.\n\n$first_contact.253.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:497`

## first_contact.253.desc.hive_enemy.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.253.desc.hive_enemy.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\n袭击者——一群[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]的有机体像我们一样做出了协调一致的联动，这显示他们可能像我们一样处于一个高级智慧的统领之下，$first_contact.253.desc.machine.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\nOur assailants - a group of [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] organics that moved with coordination that suggested a unified intelligence much like our own - $first_contact.253.desc.machine.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:498`

## first_contact.253.desc.hive_enemy.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.253.desc.hive_enemy.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\n我们的袭击者——一群[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]的外星人，他们行动是如此的协调也就是说是和我们相似的统一智慧——$first_contact.253.desc.hive.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\nOur assailants - a group of [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] aliens that moved with such coordination as to suggest a unified intelligence much like our own - $first_contact.253.desc.hive.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:499`

## first_contact.253.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.253.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start$\n\n袭击我们的是一群[from.owner.species.GetClass]外星生物，它们正着手解剖并肢解飞船上被其识别为生命体的部分。我们当中那些非机动型宏观共生体似乎遭遇了较为仁慈的命运，仅被清除或消灭。船内再无成员存续于我们的意识共鸣中，但它们的消逝仍令我们通体震颤。\n\n我们遭受了酷刑般的折磨，但至少明确了一点：此刻我们更清楚地认知到这片深空中潜藏的威胁形态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start$\n\nOur assailants, a group of [from.owner.species.GetClass] aliens, have set about dissecting and butchering those parts of us they recognize as lifeforms on board the ship. Those among us who weren't ambulatory macro-symbionts seem to have met kinder fates, having merely been exterminated or expunged. None aboard remain within our chorus, though we felt their loss throughout.\n\nAn act of torture has been inflicted upon us, but even if nothing else: we now know more about the kinds of threats we face out here.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:500`

## first_contact.253.B.devourer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.253.B.devourer&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须尽快弄清楚它们是否好吃。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

We shall have to find out whether they are tasty soon.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:501`

### English

## first_contact.254.desc.normal.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.254.desc.normal.end&lang=en)

### 中文

我们船上的视觉信号现在已经切断了，但他们故意向我们展示了他们处决俘虏的画面，展示了他们对[This.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]生命的蔑视！我们必须假定，当面对一个以这种方式行事的文明时，我们必须采取强有力的敌对行动回击。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:475`

### English

Our visual feeds from within the ship have now cut off, but not before showing us the aliens executing all the hostages they had taken, showing no regard whatsoever for [This.Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] life. We must assume that hostilities are inevitable when faced with a civilization that would act in such a manner.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:504`

## first_contact.254.desc.hive.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.254.desc.hive.end&lang=en)

### 中文

设法捕获了我们的许多子个体，并且已经开始不顾可能被浪费的资源，以最不合理的方式屠杀他们。这样的行为，加上他们捕获的我们的舰船和在船上使用的所有技术，他们的威胁等级需要提升到非常高的程度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:476`

### English

managed to capture many of our menial drones and slaughtered them in a most irrational manner, with no regard for the waste of resources. This, together with their capture of our ship and all the technologies deployed there, has caused us to significantly increase our evaluation of the danger posed by them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:505`

## first_contact.254.desc.machine.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.254.desc.machine.end&lang=en)

### 中文

设法捕获了我们的许多无人机，并且已经开始不顾可能被浪费的资源，以最不合理的方式拆解他们。这样的行为，加上他们捕获的我们的舰船和在船上使用的所有技术，他们的威胁等级需要提升到非常高的程度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:477`

### English

managed to capture many of our drones and violently disassembled them in a most irrational manner. This, along with their capture of our ship and all the technologies deployed there, has caused us to significantly increase our assessment of the danger posed by them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:506`

## first_contact.254.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.254.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start$\n\n袭击我们的是一群[from.owner.species.GetClass]外星人，他们肆无忌惮地屠杀了那些被他们视为生命体的部分。我们当中许多非移动型宏观共生体也未能幸免于这场灭绝，但无论如何：船上所有成员如今都已从我们的集体和声中撕裂。我们深切感受着每一分丧失。\n\n这场无谓的暴行施加于我们身上，但至少现在，我们对星系其他角落可能遭遇的威胁类型有了更深认知。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:487`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start$\n\nOur assailants, a group of [from.owner.species.GetClass] aliens, brazenly slaughtered those parts of us they recognized as lifeforms. Many among us who weren't ambulatory macro-symbionts were also caught up in the extermination, but regardless: all aboard have now been torn away from our chorus. We felt their loss throughout.\n\nAn act of mindless brutality has been committed upon us, but now at least we do know more about the kinds of threats we face elsewhere in this galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:516`

## first_contact.262.desc.normal.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.262.desc.normal.end&lang=en)

### 中文

或许值得庆幸的是，无论是为了他们自己的尊严还是保护我们的情报不落入敌手，我们的船员在被俘虏之前英勇就义了。然而，我们必须假定攻击者很快就会通过研究我们的船只获取我们的情报——面对一个以这种方式行事的文明时，我们必须采取强有力的敌对行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:495`

### English

Perhaps fortunately - both for them and the sake of keeping information from enemy hands - our crew took their own lives rather than permit themselves to be captured. However, we must assume that our assailants will soon find out much about us by studying our vessel - and that hostilities are inevitable when faced with a civilization that would act in such a manner.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:525`

## first_contact.262.desc.gestalt.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.262.desc.gestalt.end&lang=en)

### 中文

过载了我们的防御，为了保护我们的信息，我们发布了让我们的无人自毁的指令。然而，我们必须假定攻击者很快就会通过研究我们的船只获取我们的情报——面对一个以这种方式行事的文明时，我们必须采取强有力的敌对行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

were able to overwhelm our defenses, so we gave the order for our drones to decommission themselves so as to protect the security of our information. However, we must assume that our assailants will soon find out much about us by studying our vessel. As a result, we have significantly increased our evaluation of the danger posed by them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:526`
