## first_contact.262.desc.hive_enemy.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.262.desc.hive_enemy.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$尽管我们的船员做出了英勇的反抗，但袭击者——[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]做出了令人恐慌的联动，干烂了我们的防御。\n\n$first_contact.262.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.normal.start$ Despite our crew's valiant efforts, the attackers - [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] aliens that moved with frightening coordination - were able to overwhelm our defenses.\n\n$first_contact.262.desc.normal.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:533`

## first_contact.262.desc.hive_enemy.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.262.desc.hive_enemy.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\n袭击者——一群[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]的有机体像我们一样做出了协调一致的联动，这显示他们可能像我们一样处于一个高级智慧的统领之下，$first_contact.262.desc.gestalt.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.machine.start$\n\nOur assailants - a group of [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] organics that moved with coordination that suggested a unified intelligence much like our own - $first_contact.262.desc.gestalt.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:534`

## first_contact.262.desc.hive_enemy.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.262.desc.hive_enemy.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\n我们的袭击者——一群[From.Owner.Species.GetClass]的外星人，他们行动是如此的协调也就是说是和我们相似的统一智慧——$first_contact.262.desc.gestalt.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.hive.start$\n\nOur assailants - a group of [From.Owner.Species.GetClass] aliens that moved with such coordination as to suggest a unified intelligence much like our own - $first_contact.262.desc.gestalt.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:535`

## first_contact.262.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.262.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start$\n\n我们的袭击者——一群[from.owner.species.GetClass]外星人——很快击溃了我们的防御，他们优先攻击了那些被其识别为生命体的部分。出于自保，我们被迫抛弃了舰上所有成员，将其隔绝于我们的意识共鸣之外。尽管来犯者如此猖獗，他们终将一无所获。\n\n这是一次赤裸裸的侵略行径，但至少我们现在更清楚地认识到，在这片星海中还将面临何种威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

$first_contact.252.desc.wilderness.start$\n\nOur assailants, a group of [from.owner.species.GetClass] aliens, soon overwhelmed our defenses, having prioritized those parts of us they actually recognized as lifeforms. As an act of self-preservation we were forced to abandon all those aboard, isolating them from our chorus. For all their aggression, our attackers shall learn little from us.\n\nAn act of naked transgression has been directed toward us, but now at least we do know more about the kinds of threats we face elsewhere in this galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:536`

## first_contact.272.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.272.name&lang=en)

### 中文

暴力性第一次接触

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:507`

### English

Violence at First Contact

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:538`

## first_contact.272.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.272.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们派去调查[From.Owner.GetName]的一艘飞船被对方的武装力量的暴力袭击所摧毁。现在还不清楚他们究竟是想截获我们的船只，还是只是想简单地给我们一个警告让我们离开他们的领土。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:508`

### English

A vessel we had sent to investigate the [From.Owner.GetName] was destroyed in a violent assault by their forces. It is unclear whether they sought to capture our vessel, or whether they simply sought to give us the message to keep away from their territory.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:539`

## first_contact.272.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.272.B&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会复仇的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:509`

### English

They will be avenged!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:540`

## first_contact.282.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.282.name&lang=en)

### 中文

死里逃生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

A Close Shave

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:541`

## first_contact.282.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.282.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[ship_to_seize.GetName]§!，我们派去调查§H[From.Owner.GetName]§!的飞船，勉强躲过了对方武装力量的恶意攻击。他们似乎想要截获我们的船只并审讯船员，很有还带着对我们开展进一步攻击行动为自己牟利的目的。不过谢天谢地，在一切失去控制之前，[ship_to_seize.GetName]设法发动了紧急超光速跃迁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

The §H[ship_to_seize.GetName]§!, our vessel we had sent to investigate the §H[From.Owner.GetName]§!, narrowly escaped a vicious attack by their forces. It seems they had thought to seize our ship to interrogate our crew, probably with the aim of swinging further hostilities against us in their favor. Thankfully, the [ship_to_seize.GetName] managed to engage emergency FTL before it was too late.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:542`

## first_contact.282.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.282.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[ship_to_seize.GetName]§!，我们派去调查§H[From.Owner.GetName]§!的飞船，勉强躲过了对方武装力量的恶意攻击。他们似乎是想截获我们的船只并逆向科技，很有可能想着对我们展开进一步攻击为自己牟利。不过谢天谢地，在一切失去控制前，[ship_to_seize.GetName]设法发动了紧急超光速跃迁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

The §H[ship_to_seize.GetName]§!, our vessel we had sent to investigate the §H[From.Owner.GetName]§!, narrowly escaped a vicious attack by their forces. It seems they had thought to seize our ship and steal our technologies, probably with the aim of swinging further hostilities against us in their favor. Thankfully, the [ship_to_seize.GetName] managed to engage emergency FTL before it was too late.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:543`

## first_contact_fleets_mia_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_fleets_mia_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

为了保证安全，我方其他位于他们领土中的舰队也发动了紧急超光速跃迁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

Our other fleets in what appears to be their territory have sought safety by engaging emergency FTL.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:544`

## first_contact.285.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.285.name&lang=en)

### 中文

审讯完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

Interrogations Complete

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:546`

## first_contact.285.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.285.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!外星人的研究。最初这些人质不太愿意与我们合作，但随着时间推移他们在无意中不断地给了我们有关他们语言的线索，一直到我们能够与他们进行基础的语言交流。从那时起，我们就能迅速拼凑有关他们的细节，一旦有一位或是更多的人质愿意合作，我们就能进入他们的电脑系统。\n\n得益于从他们身上收集到的信息，我们能够确认他们来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，并就其活动和军事实力收集到了相当多的情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:515`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! aliens we recently captured. The hostages were initially unwilling to cooperate with us, but over time they inadvertently gave us clues to their language, until we were able to communicate with them on a basic level. From that point, we were rapidly able to piece together further details about them, eventually gaining access to their computer systems once one of the more cooperative hostages cracked.\n\nThanks to the information we have gleaned from them, we are able to identify them as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! and have amassed considerable intelligence on their activities and capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:547`

## first_contact.285.desc.weak

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.285.desc.weak&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!外星人的研究。这些人质显然意志薄弱，没什么阻止我们对其调查的想法。事实上，他们十分配合。我们很快就能翻译他们的语言并进入对方的电脑系统。\n\n因此，我们得以确定他们来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，并就其活动和军事实力收集到了相当多的情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! aliens we recently captured. The hostages were remarkably weak-willed and showed no particular desire to keep us from finding out about them; indeed, they proved extremely cooperative. We were soon able to translate their language and access their computer systems.\n\nAs a result, we have been able to identify them as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! and have amassed considerable intelligence on their activities and capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:548`

## first_contact.285.desc.strong

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.285.desc.strong&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!外星人的研究。这些人质表现得极其不愿意配合我们，甚至我们审讯者使用了以往最具有说服力的方法也无济于事。然而，随着时间的推移，他们在无意中不断地泄露出有关他们语言的各种线索，最终使得我们有能力与他们开展基础性的交流。从那时起，我们就可以迅速拼凑出有关他们的细节，此外骇入对方部分基础电脑系统的行动也取得了进展。尽管如此，我们还是怀疑他们对我方隐瞒了大量细节。\n\n得益于我们从他们身上获取的信息，我们得以确定他们来自于§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，并就其活动和军事力量获取了大量情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! aliens we recently captured. The hostages proved remarkably unwilling to cooperate with us, resisting even the most persuasive methods of our interrogators. However, over time, they inadvertently gave us clues to their language, eventually allowing us to communicate with them on a basic level. From that point, we were rapidly able to piece together further details about them - as well as gain access to some of their basic computer systems - although we suspect that they were able to keep many details back.\n\nThanks to the information we were able to glean from them, we are able to identify them as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! and have amassed considerable intelligence on their activities and capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:549`

## first_contact.285.desc.machine_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.285.desc.machine_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对于此前我们截获的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!机械无人机的调查行动。我们一切断它们与上级人工智能的链接之后，这些无人机就失去了代理，并且被发现几乎完全失去了作用，因为其功能已经迅速退化。\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! machines we recently captured. Once cut off from their parent AI, the drones lacked agency, and we were able to find out little of use from them as their functionality rapidly degraded.\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:550`

## first_contact.285.desc.hive_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.285.desc.hive_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对于此前捕获的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!外星人的研究。看上去它们确实是由§H蜂巢思维§!所链接起来的，单独的子个体是缺少自我意识和代理权的。将其从主体意识剥离后，这些子个体似乎漫无目的，然后一个接一个地死去。除了将其作为生物标本外也没什么其他地作用了。\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:519`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! aliens we recently captured. It would appear that they are indeed organized as a §Hhive mind§!, with individual drones lacking autonomy or agency. Cut off from the body, the drones seemed aimless and died off one by one, not yielding much in the way of useful information except as unwitting biological specimens.\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:551`

## first_contact.285.desc.wilderness_victim.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.285.desc.wilderness_victim.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经完成了对近期捕获的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!外星生物的研究。这些为数众多的生命体以某种蜂巢思维的方式组织起来，但并没有一个中央的管理实体。相反，这些几乎不具备智慧的个体似乎与它们的整个生物群落形成了一种§H共生格式塔§!结构。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! aliens we recently captured. This myriad assortment of lifeforms are organized as a sort of hive mind, albeit without a central, governing entity. Instead these barely-sapient individuals seem to form a §Hmutualistic gestalt§! with their entire biome.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:552`

## first_contact.285.desc.wilderness_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.285.desc.wilderness_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.285.desc.wilderness_victim.intro$ 由于彼此隔绝且与母星失去联系，这些共生体退化至原始状态并迅速消亡。除了作为解剖对象外，它们没有为我们提供多少有用的信息。\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:521`

### English

$first_contact.285.desc.wilderness_victim.intro$ Since being isolated from each other as well as their homeworld, these symbionts reverted to a primal state and perished fairly rapidly. They have yielded little useful information to us, save as subjects for autopsy.\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:553`

## first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end&lang=en)

### 中文

幸运的是，我们想办法破解了他们的电脑系统的秘钥，[This.Owner.GetLinguists]马上就能翻译他们用来通讯的信号。现在，我们已经能够确认§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!是§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，。我们现在积累了大量有关他们活动和能力的情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

Fortunately, we were able to crack the codes to their computer systems, and our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] were soon able to translate the signals they use for communication. As a result, we can now identify the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! and have amassed considerable intelligence on their activities and capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:554`

## first_contact.290.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外星研究完成

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

Alien Study Complete

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:555`

## first_contact.290.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!外星人的研究。这些人质相当不愿意同我们合作，不过由于我们将其开膛破肚，他们的反抗毫无意义。我们现在对他们的[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesOrganNamePlural]、[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]和[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]的内部功能以及其他身体部分有了更多的了解，我们的基因学家[This.Owner.GetResearchers]获得了相当有价值的§H异种生物学数据§!。\n\n由于我们残忍的行为带来的恐怖，一部分外星人——现在我们知道他们是§H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!——开始在我们翻译他们语言并接入电脑系统时表现出了合作的态度。作为结果，我们显然拥有了数量相当可观的情报，如果有需求，我们可以部署这些情报对付他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! aliens we recently captured. The hostages proved unwilling to cooperate with us, but this became a moot point as we began to cut them open. We now know much about the internal functions of their [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesOrganNamePlural], [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural], [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural] and other body parts, giving our genetics [This.Owner.GetResearchers] valuable §Hxenobiological data§!.\n\nDriven to terror by the cruelty of our actions, a handful of the aliens - who we now know to be §H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§! - cooperated with us as we sought to translate their language and access their computer systems. As a result, we now have considerable intelligence we are able to deploy against them, should the need arise.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:556`

## first_contact.290.desc.wilderness_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.desc.wilderness_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.285.desc.wilderness_victim.intro$\n\n与家园以及彼此隔离后，这些共生体退化到了原始状态并迅速消亡，这使得尸检变得相当简单。$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.main$\n\n$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

$first_contact.285.desc.wilderness_victim.intro$\n\nSince being isolated from each other as well as their homeworld, these symbionts reverted to a primal state and soon perished, making autopsy rather trivial. $first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.main$\n\n$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:557`

## first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经完成了对最近捕获的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!外星人的研究。结果表明，它们确实以§H蜂巢思维§!的方式组织起来，子个体缺乏自主性或能动性。虽然事实证明对它们进行审讯没有任何作用，但这已变得无关紧要，因为我们已经开始解剖它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! aliens we recently captured. It would appear that they are indeed organized as a §Hhive mind§!, with individual drones lacking autonomy or agency. Although they proved unuseful subjects for interrogation, this became a moot point as we began to cut them open.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:558`

## first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.main

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.main&lang=en)

### 中文

现在我们已经对他们[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesOrganNamePlural]、[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]、[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]和其他身体部位的内部功能有了很多了解，这为我们[This.Owner.GetResearchers]的遗传学家提供了宝贵的§H外星生物学数据§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:527`

### English

We now know much about the internal functions of their [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesOrganNamePlural], [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural], [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural] and other body parts, giving our genetics [This.Owner.GetResearchers] valuable §Hxenobiological data§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:559`

## first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.end&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在知道如何同他们的母国，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，进行沟通了，并且可以部署数量相当可观的情报来对付他们——如果有必要的话。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:528`

### English

We now know how to communicate with their parent empire, the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!, if needed - and have considerable intelligence to deploy against them should it prove necessary!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:560`

## first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.desc.hive_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.intro$$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.main$\n\n虽然花费的时间比我们预期的稍长，但凭借我们所掌握的生物学知识以及在捕获的飞船上发现的各种线索，我们最终破解了他们的语言和计算机代码。$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:529`

### English

$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.intro$ $first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.main$\n\nThough it took a bit longer than we might have hoped, the biological knowledge we had gained and the various clues scattered about the captured vessel allowed us to crack their language - and their computer codes. $first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:561`

## first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.no_vessel

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.no_vessel&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.intro$$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.main$\n\n虽然花费的时间比我们预期的稍长，但我们获得的生物学知识以及无人机被劫持时收集到的各种线索，最终让我们破解了它们的语言和计算机代码。$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:530`

### English

$first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.intro$ $first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.main$\n\nThough it took a bit longer than we might have hoped, the biological knowledge we had gained and the various clues gathered when the drones were abducted allowed us to crack their language - and their computer codes. $first_contact.290.desc.hive_victim.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:562`

## first_contact.290.desc.machine_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.desc.machine_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!机器的研究。一旦切断同人工智能主体的联系，这些无人机就失去了执行能力，无法提供与他们自己的协议相关的信息。但是一旦我们开始将他们大卸八块，我们又可以获得一定的进展了——不仅在了解他们的内部语言方面，也在找出有关他们的更多知识方面。其结论是，他们使用的处理器还是比不过我们针对他们使用的攻性数据收集技术，现在他们已经没剩下多少秘密了。\n\n我们现在可以同他们的人工智能主体——其自称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!——进行沟通，我们会静观其变。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:531`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! machines we recently captured. Once cut off from their parent AI, the drones lacked agency and gave off little useful information of their own accord, but once we began dissembling them, we were able to make more progress - both in understanding their internal functioning, and in finding out more about them. It turned out their processors were no match for the invasive data gathering techniques we used on them, with the result that little about them is now secret to us.\n\nWe can now also communicate with their parent AI - which calls itself the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! - should we see fit.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:563`

## first_contact.290.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.290.A&lang=en)

### 中文

非常令人满意的结论。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:532`

### English

A most satisfying conclusion.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.295.name&lang=en)

### 中文

研究外星材料

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:533`

### English

Alien Materials Studied

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:565`

## first_contact.295.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.295.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对于在[This.ContactCountry.GetName]中俘获的飞船的研究已经结束。没能抓到一个活口使得这次调查极具挑战。不过幸运的是，他们还是留给我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]丰富的材料用以破解它们的语言，我们现在将其识别为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，如果我们乐意的话还能和对方进行沟通。此外，我们还能够研究它们在自己舰船上使用的§H科技§!，这能帮助我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]在研究突破上找到全新的思路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

We have concluded our study of the vessel we recently captured from the [This.ContactCountry.GetName]. The absence of a living crew made our investigation challenging. Fortunately, they left behind enough material for our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] to gradually decipher their language and identify them as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!, allowing us to communicate with them should we so please. Furthermore, we were able to study their ship's §Htechnology§!, which has helped our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] uncover some novel new approaches to our own research problems.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:566`

## first_contact.295.desc.no_ship

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.295.desc.no_ship&lang=en)

### 中文

对于在[This.ContactCountry.GetName]中俘获的飞船的研究已经结束。由于没能抓到一个活口来审讯，我们的调查极具挑战。幸运的是，他们还是留给我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]丰富的材料用以破解他们的语言，我们现在将其识别为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，如果我们乐意的话还能和对方进行沟通。此外，我们还能研究他们在自己舰船上使用的§H科技§!，这能帮助我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]在研究突破上找到全新的思路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

We have concluded our study of the equipment we recently captured from the [This.ContactCountry.GetName]. Investigating it was a challenge without living specimens to interrogate. Fortunately, they left behind enough material for our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] to gradually decipher their language and identify them as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!, allowing us to communicate with them should we so please. Furthermore, we were able to study their §Htechnology§!, which has helped our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] to uncover some novel new approaches to our own research problems.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:567`

### English

## first_contact.1100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对于在§H[contact_country.GetName]§!中俘获的飞船的研究已经结束。尽管因为没有抓到活口来审讯而使调查充满挑战，但我们快速的进程可能使我们得以和[contact_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]建立通信。我们的[Root.GetResearchers]研究员可以研究他们的§H科技§!，这能帮助我们在研究突破上找到全新的思路。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

We have concluded our study of the equipment we recently captured from the §H[contact_country.GetName]§!. Although investigating it was a challenge without living specimens to interrogate, faster progress could be made once communications were opened with the [contact_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. Our [Root.GetResearchers] were able to study their §Htechnology§!, which has helped us uncover several novel new approaches to our own research problems.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:572`

## first_contact.1105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[contact_country.GetAdj]§!外星人的研究。这些人质相当不愿意同我们合作，不过由于我们已将其开膛破肚，他们的反抗毫无意义。现在我们对他们的[contact_country.GetSpeciesOrganNamePlural]、[contact_country.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]和[contact_country.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]的内部功能以及其他身体部分有了极大的了解，我们的基因学家[Root.GetResearchers]获得了相当有价值的§H异种生物学数据§!。\n\n由于我们残忍行为带来的恐怖，一部分[contact_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]开始在我们翻译他们语言并接入电脑系统时表现出了合作的态度。作为结果，我们现在拥有了数量相当可观的情报，如果有需要，我们可以利用这些情报对付他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:539`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[contact_country.GetAdj]§! aliens we recently captured. The hostages proved unwilling to cooperate with us, but this became a moot point as we began to cut them open. We now know a great deal about the internal functions of their [contact_country.GetSpeciesOrganNamePlural], [contact_country.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural], [contact_country.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural] and other body parts, giving our genetics [Root.GetResearchers] valuable §Hxenobiological data§!.\n\nDriven to terror by the cruelty of our actions, a handful of the [contact_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] cooperated with us as we sought to translate their language and access their computer systems. As a result, we now have considerable intelligence we are able to deploy against them, should the need arise.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:574`

## first_contact.1105.desc.hive_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1105.desc.hive_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[contact_country.GetAdj]§!外星人的研究。随着两国之间通讯频道的相互公开，事实已经很明显，对方的组织结构是一个§H蜂巢思维§!，子个体没有独立行动和决策的能力。尽管审讯对他们毫无用处，但是我们对其开膛破肚，使得我们对其有了诸多方面的了解。我们现在对他们的[contact_country.GetSpeciesOrganNamePlural]、[contact_country.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]和[contact_country.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]的内部机能以及其他身体部分有了更多的了解，我们的基因学家[Root.GetResearchers]获得了相当有价值的§H异种生物学数据§!。\n\n尽管花费的时间比我们原先希望的要更长，我们获得的生物学知识帮助我们破译了他们的语言——还有他们的计算机代码。若是局势需要，我们可以部署相当数量的情报来对抗他们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:540`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[contact_country.GetAdj]§! aliens we recently captured. As became apparent when they opened communications with us, they are indeed organized as a §Hhive mind§!, with individual drones lacking autonomy or agency. Although they proved unuseful subjects for interrogation, this became a moot point as we began to cut them open. We now know much about the internal functions of their [contact_country.GetSpeciesOrganNamePlural], [contact_country.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural], [contact_country.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural] and other body parts, giving our genetics [Root.GetResearchers] valuable §Hxenobiological data§!.\n\nThough it took a bit longer than we might have hoped, the biological knowledge we gained helped us to crack their language - and their computer codes. We now possess considerable intelligence to deploy against them, should it prove necessary!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:575`

## first_contact.1105.desc.machine_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1105.desc.machine_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[contact_country.GetAdj]§!机器的研究。一旦切断同人工智能主体的联系，这些无人机就失去了执行能力，无法提供与它们自己的协议相关的信息。不过当我们将它们大卸八块，我们就又获得了一定的进展——不仅是在了解它们的内部语言方面，也在找出有关它们的更多知识方面。其结论是，它们使用的处理器还是比不过我们针对它们使用的数据侵入收集技术，现在它们已经没剩下多少秘密了。\n\n我们现在掌握了数量可观的情报，它们将被投放在合适的时机，来对付它们的人工智能主体——§H[contact_country.GetName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:541`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[contact_country.GetAdj]§! machines we recently captured. Once cut off from their parent AI, the drones lacked agency and gave off little useful information of their own accord. Once we began dissembling them, we were able to make more progress - both in understanding their internal functioning, and in finding out more about them. It turned out their processors were no match for the invasive data gathering techniques we used on them, with the result that little about them is now secret to us.\n\nWe now possess considerable intelligence to deploy against their parent AI, the §H[contact_country.GetName]§!, should we see fit.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:576`

## first_contact.1110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[contact_country.GetAdj]§!外星人的研究。最初这些人质不太愿意与我们合作，但当我们同[contact_country.GetName]建立了通讯，我们就取得了很大的进展。很快，我们就“说服”了他们，让我们登录他们的电脑系统。\n\n得益于从他们身上收集到的信息，我们搜集到了关于他们国家活动和军事实力方面的大量情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[contact_country.GetAdj]§! aliens we recently captured. The hostages were initially unwilling to cooperate with us, but once communications were established with the [contact_country.GetName], we were able to make much faster progress. Before long, we were able to "persuade" them to grant us access to their computer systems.\n\nThanks to the information we have gleaned from them, we have amassed considerable intelligence on their activities and capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:578`

## first_contact.1110.desc.weak

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1110.desc.weak&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[contact_country.GetAdj]§!外星人的研究。这些人质显然意志薄弱，没什么阻止我们对其调查的想法。事实上，他们十分配合。我们很快就能说服他们让我们登入他们的电脑系统。\n\n得益于这些信息，我们搜集到了关于他们国家活动和军事实力方面的大量情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[contact_country.GetAdj]§! aliens we recently captured. The hostages were remarkably weak-willed and showed no particular desire to keep us from finding out about them; indeed, they proved extremely cooperative. Before long, we were able to persuade them to grant us access to their computer systems.\n\nThanks to the information we have gleaned from them, we have amassed considerable intelligence on their activities and capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:579`

## first_contact.1110.desc.strong

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1110.desc.strong&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[contact_country.GetAdj]§!外星人的研究。这些人质表现出了极其不配合的态度，抗拒几乎所有的审讯方式。不管怎样，当我们同[contact_country.GetName]建立了通讯，我们还是取得了一些进展——其中包括获取了他们基础电脑系统的一些权限——我们“说服”了他们，让我们登录他们的电脑系统，尽管我们猜测他们能保留许多细节。\n\n得益于这些信息，我们搜集到了关于他们国家活动和军事实力方面的大量情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[contact_country.GetAdj]§! aliens we recently captured. The hostages proved remarkably unwilling to cooperate with us, resisting even the most persuasive methods of our interrogators. However, once communications were established with the [contact_country.GetName], we were able to make some progress - including gaining access to some of their basic computer systems - although we suspect that they were able to keep many details back. Before long, we were able to "persuade" them to grant us access to their computer systems.\n\nThanks to the information we have gleaned from them, we have amassed considerable intelligence on their activities and capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:580`

## first_contact.1110.desc.machine_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1110.desc.machine_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前抓获的§H[contact_country.GetAdj]§!机器的研究。一旦切断同人工智能主体的联系，这些无人机就失去了执行能力，并且因为其功能迅速退化，被发现几乎完全失去了作用。\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[contact_country.GetAdj]§! machines we recently captured. Once cut off from their parent AI, the drones lacked agency, and we were able to find out little of use from them as their functionality rapidly degraded.\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:581`

## first_contact.1110.desc.hive_victim

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1110.desc.hive_victim&lang=en)

### 中文

我们结束了对此前捕获的§H[contact_country.GetAdj]§!外星人的研究。随着两国之间通讯频道的相互公开，事实已经很明显，对方的组织结构是一个§H蜂巢思维§!，子个体个体没有独立行动和决策的能力。将其从主巢意识剥离后，这些子个体似乎漫无目的，然后一个接一个地死去。除了将其作为生物标本外也没什么其他地作用了。\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

We have concluded our study of the §H[contact_country.GetAdj]§! aliens we recently captured. As became apparent when they opened communications with us, they are indeed organized as a §Hhive mind§!, with individual drones lacking autonomy or agency. Cut off from the body, the drones seemed aimless and died off one by one, not yielding much in the way of useful information except as unwitting biological specimens.\n\n$first_contact.285.desc.gestalt_end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:582`

## first_contact.1110.desc.gestalt_end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1110.desc.gestalt_end&lang=en)

### 中文

幸运的是，我们设法破解了他们电脑系统的秘钥，这让我们搜集到关于他们国家活动和军事实力方面的大量情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

Fortunately, we were able to crack the codes to their computer systems, allowing us to amass considerable intelligence on their activities and capabilities.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:583`

## first_contact.320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外星人已捕获

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

Aliens Captured

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:585`

## first_contact.320.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管行动没有出错，我们的特工摆脱了追兵成功逃离，但是要想§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!对我们的行动没有丝毫察觉似乎是不可能的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Though the operation went without a hitch and our operatives were able to escape without pursuit, it is probably too much to hope for that the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! remain ignorant of our actions.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:586`

## first_contact.320.desc.planet.normal.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.planet.normal.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

一支英勇的秘密特种部队带着从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!星系的殖民地上抓获的人质从§H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§!的领域内逃脱了出来。趁着夜色的掩护，他们设法降落在了星球的一个人迹罕至的地方，俘获了数名活体外星人，同时还缴获了大量技术硬件。这些对于我们破译他们的语言以及评估他们的技术等级都十分有用。\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

A brave team of covert special forces has escaped §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! territory with hostages captured from one of their colonies in the §H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§! system. They were able to land on a remote part of the world in the cover of darkness and seize several live aliens, as well as a large stash of technical hardware that is sure to help us decode their language and assess their technological level.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:587`

## first_contact.320.desc.planet.normal.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.planet.normal.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

一支英勇的秘密特种部队带着从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!星系的殖民地上抓获的人质从§H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§!的领域内逃脱了出来。趁着夜色的掩护，他们设法降落在了星球的一个人迹罕至的地方，然后却惊奇地发现这里的居民实际上是§H机械体无人机§!。不管怎样，他们在无伤条件下捕获了数台无人机，同时还缴获了大量技术硬件。这些对于我们破译他们的语言以及评估他们的技术等级都十分有用。\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

A brave team of covert special forces has escaped §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! territory with hostages captured from one of their colonies in the §H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§! system. They were able to land on a remote part of the world in the cover of darkness and were surprised to discover that the inhabitants were, in fact, §Hmechanical drones§!. Nevertheless, they seized several of them undamaged, as well as a large stash of technical hardware that is sure to help us decode their language and assess their technological level.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:588`

## first_contact.320.desc.planet.gestalt.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.planet.gestalt.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

一批主导秘密行动的无人机，由[This.Leader.GetName]管控，降落在了一个§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!位于§H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§!星系的殖民地上。他们设法降落在了行星上的一个人迹罕至的地方，然后在脱逃之前活捉了几个外星人，同时还顺手牵羊走了几个技术性硬件设备。这些对于我们破译他们的语言以及评估他们的技术等级都十分有用。\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

A detachment of covert operations drones led by [This.Leader.GetName] managed to land in a remote area of a §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! colony in the §H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§! system. They were able to seize several live aliens, as well as a large stash of technical hardware that may prove useful for decoding their communications and assessing their technological level, before making an escape.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:589`

## first_contact.320.desc.planet.hive.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.planet.hive.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

一批主导秘密行动的无人机，由[This.Leader.GetName]管控，降落在了一个§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!位于§H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§!星系的殖民地上。他们设法降落在了行星上的一个人迹罕至的地方，然而出人意料的是，这个行星上的居民实际上是机械体无人机。不管怎样，他们在脱逃之前以无伤条件抓捕了几个无人机，同时还顺手牵羊走了几个技术性硬件设备。这些对于我们破译他们的语言以及评估他们的技术等级都十分有用。\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

A detachment of covert operations drones led by [This.Leader.GetName] managed to land in a remote area of a §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! colony in the §H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§! system. Unexpectedly, it turned out that the inhabitants of the planet were, in fact, mechanical drones. Nevertheless, they seized several of them undamaged, as well as a large stash of technical hardware that may prove useful for decoding their communications and assessing their technological level, before making an escape.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:590`

## first_contact.320.desc.planet.machine.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.planet.machine.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

一支由[This.Leader.GetName]率领的秘密行动无人机分队设法降落在了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!星系§H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§!殖民地的一处人迹罕至的地带。他们很快发现这个星球上的居民实际上也是机械体无人机——或许受某个类似我方[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]的人工智能所支配。分队毫不退缩，在无伤条件下捕获了数台无人机，同时还缴获了大量技术硬件，随后成功撤离。这些或将有助于破译他们的通讯并评估其技术等级。\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

A detachment of covert operations drones led by [This.Leader.GetName] managed to land in a remote area of a §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! colony in the §H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§! system. They soon discovered that the inhabitants of the world are, in fact, mechanical drones - perhaps guided by an AI like our own [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]. Undeterred, they captured several drones undamaged, as well as a large stash of technical hardware that may prove useful for decoding their communications and assessing their technological level, before making an escape.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:591`

## first_contact.320.desc.no_planet.normal.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.no_planet.normal.normal&lang=en)

### 中文

一支英勇的秘密特种部队带着一艘他们在§H[abduction_system.GetName]§!星系拦截到的货运舰船从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的领域内逃脱了出来。舰船的船员绝大多数都保留了活口，舰船的硬件设备和电脑系统也没有遭到什么明显损坏。\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:555`

### English

A brave team of covert special forces has escaped §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! territory with a captured cargo vessel they intercepted in the §H[abduction_system.GetName]§! system. Along with the ship's crew, which was largely taken alive, much of the vessel's hardware and computer systems were also spared major damage.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:592`

## first_contact.320.desc.no_planet.normal.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.no_planet.normal.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

一支英勇的秘密特种部队带着一艘他们在§H[abduction_system.GetName]§!星系拦截到的货运舰船从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的领域内逃脱了出来。出乎我们意料的是，舰船船员均由§H机械体无人机§!构成——或许由一个失控的人工智能引导——我们没法在只造成轻微伤害的情况下对其进行制服和抓捕。好在我们的小队保证了舰船的绝大多数硬件设备和电脑系统完好无损。\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

A brave team of covert special forces has escaped §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! territory with a captured cargo vessel they intercepted in the §H[abduction_system.GetName]§! system. To our surprise, the ship was crewed exclusively by §Hmechanical drones§! - perhaps guided by a rogue AI - which we were nevertheless able to disable and capture with only minor damage. Our team also managed to leave most of the vessel's hardware and computer systems intact.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:593`

## first_contact.320.desc.no_planet.gestalt.normal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.no_planet.gestalt.normal&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:557`

### English

A detachment of covert operations drones led by [This.Leader.GetName] managed to seize a small §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! logistics vessel in the §H[abduction_system.GetName]§! system. Along with the ship's crew, which was largely taken alive, much of the vessel's hardware and computer systems were also spared major damage.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:594`

## first_contact.320.desc.no_planet.hive.machine

### 中文

一批主导秘密行动的无人机，由[This.Leader.GetName]管控，设法在§H[abduction_system.GetName]§!星系劫持了一艘§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的小型补给舰。出乎我们意料的是，舰船船员均由§H机械体无人机§!构成。我们设法在只造成轻微伤害的情况下对其进行制服和抓捕。\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

A detachment of covert operations drones led by [This.Leader.GetName] managed to seize a small §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! logistics vessel in the §H[abduction_system.GetName]§! system. Unexpectedly, it turned out that the ship was exclusively manned by §Hmechanical drones§!. Undeterred, our team disabled and captured them with only minor damage, also managing to leave most of the vessel's hardware and computer systems intact.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

## first_contact.320.desc.no_planet.machine.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.320.desc.no_planet.machine.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

一批主导秘密行动的无人机，由[This.Leader.GetName]管控，设法在§H[abduction_system.GetName]§!星系劫持了一艘§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的小型补给舰。结果我们发现，舰船船员均由机械体无人机构成——或许由一个如同我们自己的§H[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]§!一样的人工智能。我们设法在只造成轻微伤害的情况下对其进行制服和抓捕，并且保证了舰船的绝大多数硬件设备和电脑系统完好无损。\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Undeterred, our team disabled and captured them with only minor damage, also managing to leave most of the vessel's hardware and computer systems intact.\n\n$first_contact.320.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:601`

## first_contact.321.desc.mega_oops

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.321.desc.mega_oops&lang=en)

### 中文

一支有勇——但或许无谋——的秘密特殊武装小队成功从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!领地撤离并从他们§H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§!星系的殖民地中带走了若干人质。他们在黑夜的遮蔽下降落到行星偏远的角落并抓走了几只带有大型黑白斑点的哺乳生物，而它们并没有做出任何反抗。\n\n不久之后我们就意识到这些生物并不能发出任何具有[This.ContactCountry.GetName]通讯的声音特征，事实上也不能给出任何证明它们是感性生物的迹象。经过进一步调查，我们得出结论，我们意外地抓获了他们牲畜的样本！\n\n不幸的是，在这次行动之后他们的太空巡逻部队提高了警戒，说明他们已经探测到了我们的存在——使得我们任何未来的尝试计划都只能惨遭失败……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

A brave - but perhaps not very bright - team of covert special forces has escaped §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! territory with hostages captured from one of their colonies in the §H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§! system. They were able to land on a remote part of the planet in the cover of darkness and seize several specimens of a large, black and white speckled mammalian creature, which did not resist.\n\nOnly later did we realize that these creatures were not making any of the sounds characteristic of [This.ContactCountry.GetName] transmissions, nor indeed giving any indication whatsoever of being sentient. Upon further investigation, we concluded that we had accidentally captured examples of their livestock!\n\nUnfortunately, their space patrols have stepped up in vigilance since the raid, indicating that they have detected our presence - making any further attempts be doomed to failure...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:602`

## first_contact.321.A

### 中文

真尴尬。

### English

How embarrassing.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:603`

## first_contact.322.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.322.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Owner.GetName]的背叛

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

The Treachery of the [From.Owner.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:604`

## first_contact.322.desc.planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.322.desc.planet&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管有些人早在担忧未知外星人舰船闯入我们的宇域一事，[From.Owner.GetName]表现出来的威胁直到刚刚才昭然若揭。他们似乎在[abduction_planet.GetName]的一个偏远地区登陆了一支秘密行动小队，还绑架了几个活的[This.Owner.GetSpeciesName]人质，在我们能够组织起一支救援小队之前就逃遁到了深空。我们只能想象这些人质在他们的捕捉者手里会遭到何等的折磨。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

While some were worried at the incursion of unknown alien vessels into our space, it was not clear until now just how much of a threat the [From.Owner.GetName] posed. It seems they landed a covert team on a remote part of [abduction_planet.GetName] and seized living [This.Owner.GetSpeciesName] hostages, escaping into the depths of space before we could mount a rescue party. We can only imagine what tortures they will suffer at the hands of their captors.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:605`

## first_contact.322.desc.no_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.322.desc.no_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管有些人早在担忧未知外星人舰船闯入我们的宇域一事，[From.Owner.GetName]表现出来的威胁直到刚刚才昭然若揭。他们似乎派出了一支秘密行动小队拦截了我们一艘位于[abduction_system.GetName]星系的货船，扣押了这艘船，在我们能够组织起一支救援小队之前就逃遁到了深空。我们只能想象这些人质在他们的捕捉者手里会遭到何等的折磨。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

While some were worried at the incursion of unknown alien vessels into our space, it was not clear until now just how much of a threat the [From.Owner.GetName] posed. It seems they sent a covert force to intercept one of our cargo vessels in the [abduction_system.GetName] system, seizing the ship and escaping into the depths of space before we could mount a rescue party. We can only imagine what tortures the crew will suffer at the hands of their captors.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:606`

## first_contact.322.desc.planet.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.322.desc.planet.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将允许未知外星人进入我们宇域一事评估为风险——现在这项评估被证明为是正确的。似乎[From.Owner.GetName]在[abduction_planet.GetName]的一个偏远地区登陆了一支秘密行动小队，然后捕获了几个[This.Owner.GetSpeciesName]无人机，还有一些我们的硬件设备，在我们能够组织起一支小队摧毁他们之前就逃遁到了深空。我们只能希望他们从带走的东西那里找不到太多的价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

We had assessed allowing unknown aliens into our space to be a risk - an assessment which has proven correct. It seems the [From.Owner.GetName] landed a covert team on a remote part of [abduction_planet.GetName] and seized several [This.Owner.GetSpeciesName] drones, along with some of our hardware, escaping into the depths of space before we could send a party to destroy them. We can only hope they will be unable to find out much of value from what they have taken.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:607`

## first_contact.322.desc.planet.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.322.desc.planet.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

我们早已怀疑允许未知外星物种进入我方宇域会带来重大风险，如今事实证明我们的担忧不无道理。[From.Owner.GetName]显然找到了隐秘登陆[abduction_planet.GetName]地表的方法，他们隔离并劫持了数名共生体后，便遁入太空深处。其行动之诡谲，令我们完全无力阻止。\n\n对于这桩卑劣的侵犯行径，我们深感耻辱与痛惜。但值得稍感宽慰的是，这些掠夺者从窃取的样本中榨取不了多少价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

We had suspected that allowing unknown aliens into our space would carry significant risks, and we now know we were right to be concerned. It seems the [From.Owner.GetName] found a way to covertly land a team on our surface at [abduction_planet.GetName], isolating and abducting several symbionts before fleeing into the depths of space. Such was their subterfuge, we were powerless to intervene.\n\nWe feel the shame and loss of this foul transgression, however we may also take small comfort from knowing the raiders will not glean much value from what they have stolen.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:608`

## first_contact.322.desc.no_planet.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.322.desc.no_planet.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将允许未知外星人进入我们宇域一事评估为风险——现在这项评估被证明为是正确的。似乎[From.Owner.GetName]派出了一支秘密行动小队拦截了一艘我们位于[abduction_system.GetName]星系的补给舰，扣押的这艘舰船，在我们能够组织起一支小队摧毁他们之前就逃遁到了深空。我们只能希望他们从带走的东西那里找不到太多的价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:572`

### English

We had assessed allowing unknown aliens into our space to be a risk - an assessment which has proven correct. It seems the [From.Owner.GetName] sent a covert force to intercept one of our logistics vessels in the [abduction_system.GetName] system, seizing the ship and escaping into the depths of space before we could send a party to destroy them. We can only hope they will be unable to find out much of value from what they have taken.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:609`

## first_contact.330.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.330.name&lang=en)

### 中文

绑架计划失败

### English

Abduction Attempt Failed

## first_contact.330.desc.disaster

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.330.desc.disaster&lang=en)

### 中文

我们计划绑架几个人质进行审问的行动成了§H一场灾难§!！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:574`

### English

Our attempt to seize hostages for interrogation has gone §Hdisastrously§!!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:611`

## first_contact.330.desc.planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.330.desc.planet&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.330.desc.disaster$一支由[This.Leader.GetName]领导的英勇的秘密特种部队试图在§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!位于§H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§!星系的一个殖民地上登陆。然而，一旦他们着陆之后我们立马就失去了联络。在当前这个阶段，我们不能指望他们有什么逃脱追捕的希望。不幸的是，我们永远没法知道他们是被留作了活口，还是在外星人手上经受长达数小时的折磨之后被处决。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:575`

### English

$first_contact.330.desc.disaster$ A brave team of covert special forces led by [This.Leader.GetName] attempted to land on a §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! colony in the §H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§! system. However, we lost contact with them soon after they landed. At this stage, we can no longer hold out any hope that they escaped capture. Unfortunately, we may never know whether they were taken alive, or whether they were spared long hours of torture at the hands of aliens by the swift release of death.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:612`

## first_contact.330.desc.planet.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.330.desc.planet.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.330.desc.disaster$一批主导秘密行动的无人机，由[This.Leader.GetName]管控，试图降落在一个§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!位于§H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§!星系的殖民地上。然而，一旦他们着陆之后我们立马就失去了联络。在当前这个阶段，我们必须假设他们要么被俘虏要么被摧毁。不幸的是，我们永远无法直到他们在永久报废之前，敌人会从他们身上榨取何等的情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:576`

### English

$first_contact.330.desc.disaster$ A detachment of covert operations drones led by [This.Leader.GetName] attempted to land on a §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! colony in the §H[abduction_planet.System.GetName]§! system. However, we lost contact with them soon after they landed. At this stage, we must assume that they were either captured or destroyed. Unfortunately, we may never know what information the enemy might have gleaned from them before their eventual demise.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:613`

## first_contact.330.desc.no_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.330.desc.no_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.330.desc.disaster$一支由[This.Leader.GetName]带领的隶属于秘密特种部队的英勇小队尝试在§H[abduction_system.GetName]§!星系获取一个§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的货船。然而他们并没有意识到他们选择的目标比预期的具有更好的武装——也没有意识到地方周边还隐藏着数艘战舰。在仓促的撤退中，他们的船只被摧毁了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:577`

### English

$first_contact.330.desc.disaster$ A brave team of covert special forces led by [This.Leader.GetName] attempted to seize a §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! cargo vessel in the §H[abduction_system.GetName]§! system. However, they did not realize that their chosen target was better-armed than anticipated - nor that the enemy had several warships concealed nearby. Their ship was destroyed along with all hands as they sought a hasty retreat.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:614`

## first_contact.330.desc.no_planet.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.330.desc.no_planet.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.330.desc.disaster$一批主导秘密行动的无人机，由[This.Leader.GetName]管控，打算在§H[abduction_system.GetName]§!星系劫持一艘§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的补给舰。然而，他们选择的目标武装水平比先前预估的要高，他们也没有预估到周边还有几艘藏匿的战舰。无人机的舰船在仓促撤退时被摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

$first_contact.330.desc.disaster$ A detachment of covert operations drones led by [This.Leader.GetName] attempted to seize a small §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! logistics vessel in the §H[abduction_system.GetName]§! system. However, they did not realize that their chosen target was better-armed than anticipated - nor that the enemy had several warships concealed nearby. Their ship was destroyed as they sought a hasty retreat.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:615`

## first_contact.332.desc.planet.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.planet.start&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管有些人早在担忧未知外星人舰船闯入我们的宇域一事，[From.Owner.GetName]表现出来的威胁直到刚刚才昭然若揭。他们似乎在[abduction_planet.GetName]的一个偏远地区登陆了一支秘密行动小队，打算绑架了几个人质；然而，得益于我们的早期预警系统，我们能够部署自己的特种部队切断他们的逃跑路线。他们下次还想玩这种蠢把戏的时候会三思的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

While some were worried at the incursion of unknown alien vessels into our space, it was not clear until now just how much of a threat the [From.Owner.GetName] posed. It seems they landed a covert team on a remote part of [abduction_planet.GetName] to attempt to seize hostages for interrogation; however, thanks to our early warning systems, we were able to deploy our own special forces to cut off their escape. They will surely think twice before pulling a stunt like this again!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:619`

## first_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.start&lang=en)

### 中文

我们曾经评估允许未知外星舰队进入我们的太空是颇具风险的——而这已经被证明是正确的。似乎[From.Owner.GetName]在[abduction_planet.GetName]偏僻的角落已经登陆了一个特工小队。然而，得益于我们的预知威胁检测系统，我们能够部署自己的特种部队切断他们的逃跑路线。他们下次还想玩这种把戏的时候一定会三思的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

We had assessed allowing unknown aliens into our space to be a risk - an assessment which has proven correct. It seems the [From.Owner.GetName] landed a covert team on a remote part of [abduction_planet.GetName]; however, thanks to our precognitive threat detection systems, we were able to deploy our own special forces drones to cut off their escape. They will surely think twice before pulling a stunt like this again!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:620`

## first_contact.332.desc.no_planet.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.no_planet.end&lang=en)

### 中文

然而碰巧的是，我们刚好在附近有数艘隐蔽的战舰能够快速地到达现场。\n\n敌方船只在试图逃跑的时候被摧毁了，但是那里依旧可能有一些有用的信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

however, in a stroke of fortune, we had several warships concealed nearby that were able to arrive on the scene quickly.\n\nThe enemy vessel was destroyed as it sought to escape, but its remains may yet yield some useful information.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:621`

## first_contact.332.desc.planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.planet&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.332.desc.planet.start$\n\n似乎针对我们的攻击者——一群[From.Owner.GetSpeciesClass]外星人——被命令了结自己的生命而不是让他们自己被俘虏，但是这样的情况当然也给予了我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]丰富的材料以供调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

$first_contact.332.desc.planet.start$\n\nIt seems our assailants - a group of [From.Owner.GetSpeciesClass] aliens - were under orders to take their own lives rather than allow themselves to be captured, but this incident has certainly given our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] abundant material to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:622`

## first_contact.332.desc.planet.machine_enemy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.planet.machine_enemy&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.332.desc.planet.start$\n\n似乎针对我们的攻击者——一群机械无人机，也可能是受控于失控的人工智能——被命令自毁而不是让他们自己被俘虏，但是这个事故当然也提供给我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]丰富的材料以供调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

$first_contact.332.desc.planet.start$\n\nIt seems our assailants - a group of mechanical drones, perhaps guided by a rogue AI - were under orders to destroy themselves rather than allow themselves to be captured, but this incident has certainly given our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] abundant material to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:623`

## first_contact.332.desc.no_planet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.no_planet&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管有些人担心位置外星船只入侵我们的太空，直到现在我们尚不明确[From.Owner.GetName]构成了多少威胁。似乎他们派遣了一支秘密部队来拦截我们在[abduction_system.GetName]星系的一艘货船；$first_contact.332.desc.no_planet.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

While some were worried at the incursion of unknown alien vessels into our space, it was not clear until now just how much of a threat the [From.Owner.GetName] posed. It seems they sent a covert force to intercept one of our cargo vessels in the [abduction_system.GetName] system; $first_contact.332.desc.no_planet.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:624`

## first_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们曾经评估允许未知外星舰队进入我们的太空是颇具风险的——而这已经被证明是正确的。似乎[From.Owner.GetName]在[abduction_planet.GetName]偏僻的角落已经登陆了一个特工小队。然而，得益于我们的预知威胁检测系统，我们能够部署自己的特种部队切断他们的逃跑路线。他们下次还想玩这种把戏的时候一定会三思的！\n\n似乎敌方——一群[From.Owner.GetSpeciesClass]外星人——被命令自杀而不是让他们自己被俘虏，但是这个事故毫无疑问也给予了我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]丰富的材料以供调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:587`

### English

We had assessed allowing unknown aliens into our space to be a risk - an assessment which has proven correct. It seems the [From.Owner.GetName] landed a covert team on a remote part of [abduction_planet.GetName]; however, thanks to our precognitive threat detection systems, we were able to deploy our own special forces drones to cut off their escape. They will surely think twice before pulling a stunt like this again!\n\nIt seems the enemy - a group of [From.Owner.GetSpeciesClass] aliens - were given the command to take their own lives rather than allow themselves to be captured, but this incident has certainly given our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] abundant material to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:625`

## first_contact.332.desc.planet.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.planet.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

我们早就知道允许未知外星种族进入我们的宇域会带来重大风险，现在事实证明我们的担忧完全正确。[From.Owner.GetName]几乎成功将一支小队秘密降落在[abduction_planet.GetName]地表，企图绑架多名共生体并窃取我们的技术。但他们严重低估了我们高度互联的特性。当这些入侵者违反协议的行为被识破时，立即遭到了猛烈反击。\n\n这支由[From.Owner.GetSpeciesClass]外星人组成的敌袭小队在行动失败后，似乎接到了自毁指令以避免被俘。尽管如此，这次入侵事件仍为我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]提供了大量可供研究的样本材料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:588`

### English

We knew allowing unknown aliens into our space would carry significant risks, and now we know how right we were to be concerned. The [From.Owner.GetName] almost found a way to covertly land a team on our surface at [abduction_planet.GetName], intent on abducting several symbionts and seizing our technology. However, they did not properly account for our intraconnected nature. The would-be raiders met with fierce opposition as soon as we recognized their violation.\n\nIt seems the enemy - a group of [From.Owner.GetSpeciesClass] aliens - were commanded to take their own lives rather than allow themselves to be captured, but in spite of that: this incursion has given our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] abundant material with which to investigate them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:626`

## first_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.machine_enemy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.machine_enemy&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.start$\n\n似乎针对我们的攻击者——一群机械无人机，也可能是受控于失控的人工智能——被命令自毁而不是让他们自己被俘虏，但是这个事故毫无疑问也提供给我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]丰富的材料以供调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:589`

### English

$first_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.start$\n\nIt seems the enemy - a group of mechanical drones, perhaps guided by a rogue AI - were commanded to destroy themselves rather than allow themselves to be captured, but this incident has certainly given our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] abundant material to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:627`

## first_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.machine_enemy.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.machine_enemy.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.start$\n\n似乎针对我们的攻击者——一群机械体无人机，可能就像我们[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]一样的人工智能——受到命令宁愿自毁也不能被俘，不过这个事故毫无疑问给了我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]丰富的材料用于调研。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:590`

### English

$first_contact.332.desc.planet.gestalt.start$\n\nIt seems our assailants - a group of mechanical drones, perhaps guided by an AI like our own [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName] - were under orders to destroy themselves rather than allow themselves to be captured, but this incident has certainly given our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] abundant material to investigate.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:628`

## first_contact.332.desc.no_planet.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.332.desc.no_planet.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将允许未知外星人进入我们宇域一事评估为风险——现在这项评估被证明为是正确的。似乎[From.Owner.GetName]派遣了一支秘密行动部队在[abduction_system.GetName]星系拦截了我们的一艘后勤船只；$first_contact.332.desc.no_planet.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

We had assessed allowing unknown aliens into our space to be a risk - an assessment which has proven correct. It seems the [From.Owner.GetName] sent a covert force to intercept one of our logistics vessels in the [abduction_system.GetName] system; $first_contact.332.desc.no_planet.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:629`

## first_contact.350.name

谜语人

### English

Speaking in Riddles

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:633`

## first_contact.350.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.350.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们一开始在演绎推导§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!所使用的语言的基础取得了些许的成功，之后我们在建立与他们有意义的交流进程中却停滞不前。似乎他们在讲一种§H奇怪的谜语§!，通过某种精悍但不是很有意义的短语来传递意义，我们只能猜测其其重要性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:595`

### English

After some initial successes in deducing the fundamentals of the language used by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, our progress in establishing meaningful communications with them has stalled. It appears that they speak in §Hstrange riddles§!, somehow conveying meaning through short and seemingly nonsensical phrases of which we can only guess the significance.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:634`

## first_contact.350.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.350.A&lang=en)

令人疑惑。我们需要收集更大的样本量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:596`

### English

Puzzling. We will need to gather a larger sample size.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:635`

### 中文

一种隐喻的语言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:597`

### English

A Language of Metaphor

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:636`

## first_contact.355.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.355.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们进一步调查§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!——我们现在知道是§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!——我们终于知道他们使用的短语常常用典于在过去一些时间点上发生的重大的、知名的事件。这些典故的意味在他们族人内部显然是广为人知的，可以用于表达一种情感，或描述事务的状态。\n\n幸运的是，看上去这种形式的语言并不是唯一的版本，仅仅作为§H“高端腔”§!，用于官方的场合或者智者之间的交流。一种更加广泛的§H“低端腔”§!文法在非正式的场合被频繁使用，特别是在年轻人或者§H文化水平不高§!的人之间。不幸的是，同一个地位颇高的人使用“低端腔”交流是一件非常具有侮辱意味的事情，我们要是这样同他们沟通，他们准把我们当作智力等同于小孩的物种来对待……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:598`

### English

As we continued to investigate the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! - who we now know to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! - it became clear that the phrases they use are references to significant, well-known events that happened at some point in their past. The meanings of these references are apparently generally recognized among their kind, and can be a shorthand for expressing a sentiment or state of affairs.\n\nFortunately, it seems that this form of their language is not the only version of it, but merely the §H"high speech"§!, used in official settings and between intellectuals. An altogether more comprehensible §H"low speech"§! vernacular is used in more informal settings, especially among the young and those of §Hlesser intelligence§!. Unfortunately, it is considered gravely insulting to speak to one of high standing using the "low speech", so that they must treat us as the intellectual equivalents of children in order to communicate with us...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:637`

## first_contact.355.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.355.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们对其调查的深入，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!——现在我们知道叫§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!——他们使用的语句其实是某种我们未获取秘钥的编码。显然它们指的是在我们[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]之间共享的知识的事件，可能是表达情感或事态的简写。\n\n幸运的是，他们并不是总用这种§H“高级语言”§!来交流，除非是在沟通重要的事项。一种并行的更易理解的§H“低级语言”§!则被运用于非正式的场合，特别是年轻个体和§H低智力§!个体间。然而，看起来那些高等个体们对“低级语言”使用者嗤之以鼻，也就是说他们可能把我们当做智力等同于孩童一般与我们进行交流……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:599`

### English

As we continued to investigate the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! - who we now know to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! - it became clear that the phrases they use are some sort of code to which we lack the key. Apparently they refer to events that are shared knowledge among the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural], and can be a shorthand for expressing a sentiment or state of affairs.\n\nFortunately, it seems that they do not always communicate in such a manner but merely use this §H"high speech"§! when important matters are being discussed. An altogether more comprehensible §H"low speech"§! vernacular is used in more informal settings, especially among the young and those of §Hlesser intelligence§!. Unfortunately, it seems that those of high standing react with scorn to those using the "low speech", meaning that they must treat us as the intellectual equivalents of children in order to communicate with us...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:638`

Contact them in the "low speech".

## first_contact.356.option.tooltip

如果我们使用了正确的致敬，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!会对我们非常§G印象深刻§!，但是如果用错了他们会感觉有被§H冒犯§!到。\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:611`

### English

## first_contact.357.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.357.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最佳的致敬方式

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

The Finest of Greetings

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:651`

## first_contact.357.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.357.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的发言得到了立即的反应。微笑——至少我们相信是和微笑类似的表情——在§H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!间洋溢开来，他们嘴里讲了几个短语，用的是不容置喙的§H友好的§!字词。看上去我们选择致敬的方式选对了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:613`

### English

The reaction to our words was instantaneous. Smiles - or at least, what we believe to be their equivalent of smiles - broke out among the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!, who uttered several phrases of unmistakably §Hfriendly§! character. It seems we chose our form of greeting well.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:652`

## first_contact.357.A

### English
