## first_contact.358.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.358.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一个糟糕的选择

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:618`

### English

A Poor Choice

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:657`

## first_contact.358.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.358.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

话语刚一说出口，很明显我们就选错了。§H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!保持了很长一段的沉默，接着我们遭到了如连珠炮一般措辞强烈的谴责。他们的意图很明显带有§H敌意§!，即便他们想要表达的准确意味尚不明确——我们本来打算着让[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]印象深刻，结果是我们§H羞辱§!了他们。\n\n往好处看，至少我们现在能和他们沟通了，某种程度上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

As soon as the words were uttered, it was obvious that we had chosen poorly. The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§! held their silence for a long moment before we were beset by a barrage of harshly-spoken phrases. Their intent is clearly §Hhostile§!, even if their exact meaning remains unclear - it seems that in our attempt to impress the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural], we only manage to §Hinsult§! them.\n\nOn the positive side, at least we are able to communicate with them now, to some degree.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:658`

## first_contact.358.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.358.A&lang=en)

### 中文

他们会慢慢习惯的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

They will get over it.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:659`

## first_contact.365.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.365.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恐怖噪音

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:622`

### English

Monstrous Noises

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:662`

## first_contact.365.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.365.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这种噪音——听起来杂糅着恐怖的轰鸣声和金属§H刮擦§!的声音——喧嚣了数秒钟，又以一声兀然的§H嚎叫§!般的噪音结尾，让在场的所有人都打了个§H寒颤§!。\n\n正当[This.Owner.GetEnvoy]§H[This.Leader.GetName]§!觉得这可怕的声音沉寂下去以后，它们又变本加厉地开始了。[This.Leader.GetSheHe]强忍着不适关闭了接收器。[This.Leader.GetSheHeCap]转向了聚在一起的官员们，他们中的一些甚至吓得躲到了椅背后面。\n“豁，好家伙……”[This.Leader.GetSheHe]无力地说。\n\n很难想象我们从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!获取的这个物种发出的这些噪音没有敌意。海军官员已经做出来最坏的预计，认为我们很快就会与之交战，更冷静的声音则说我们需要再耐心地等待观察。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:623`

### English

The noises - a mixture of threatening booming noises and what sounds like the §Hgrinding§! of metal on metal - continued unabated for several minutes, punctuated occasionally by a §Hhowling§! noise that sent a §Hchill§! through everyone who heard it.\n\nJust as [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] §H[This.Leader.GetName]§! thought the noises had died down, they started up again, louder than before. With a wince, [This.Leader.GetSheHe] switched the receiver off. [This.Leader.GetSheHeCap] turned to the gathered crowd of officers, some of whom were cowering behind their seats.\n"Well, that was quite something..." [This.Leader.GetSheHe] said weakly.\n\nIt is hard to imagine that a species making the noises we have picked up from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! could be anything other than hostile. Naval officers already fear the worst and assume we will soon be at war with them, but calmer heads suggest we'd be better off waiting and seeing.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:663`

## first_contact.365.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.365.A&lang=en)

他们并不能决定自己发出什么样的声音。我们之后会去理解他们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:624`

### English

They cannot help how they sound. We will come to understand them later.

It is a challenge. Send threatening noises back!

### English

Is it impossible to predict how the §Y[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! will react to this.\n

人不犯我，我不犯人。

We will make no hostile moves, but we will be ready should they attack.

## first_contact.370.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.370.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.ContactCountry.GetName]的回应

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

The Response of the [This.ContactCountry.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:670`

## first_contact.370.desc1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.370.desc1&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的回应似乎让§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!有点无语。然后很快他们彻底沉默了，之后很少再听到他们的情况。我们也只能推测其含义。\n\n与此同时，我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]已经在努力翻译他们语言中我们已知的部分。只要他们一有进展，我们就有希望能理解这些东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:631`

### English

Our response seems to have left the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! quite speechless. As soon as they received it, they fell completely silent, and we have heard little from them since. We can only speculate as to the meaning of this.\n\nIn the meantime, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have been hard at work translating what we know of their language. Hopefully, once they have made sufficient progress, we will be able to make sense of this.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:671`

## first_contact.370.desc2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.370.desc2&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!通过大幅度增强其传输强度以回应了我们的挑衅。现在它们吵得[This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]已经不能忍受了，但是通过大幅调低音量，我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]可以收集大量数据，他们相信这可能是翻译外星语言的关键——假设，在噪音的某个地方，可以发现某种语言。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:632`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! responded to our challenge by significantly increasing the intensity of their transmissions. They are now too loud for [This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural] to tolerate, but by turning the volume down considerably, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] are able to gather a large amount of data that they believe may hold the key to translating the aliens' language - assuming that, somewhere in the noise, there is a language to be found.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:672`

## first_contact.375.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.375.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.ContactCountry.GetName]解释道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:633`

### English

The [This.ContactCountry.GetName], Explained

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:673`

## first_contact.37X.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.37X.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经破译出§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!足够多的语言，现在我们可以与他们进行§H通讯§!——并理解他们先前的广播。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:634`

### English

We have managed to decipher enough of the language used by the civilization we now know to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! to open §Hcommunications§! with them - and to make sense of their earlier broadcasts.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:674`

## first_contact.375.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.375.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\n事实证明，解释很简单，也很可怕。我们还不知道是什么原因导致了他们社会的这种发展，但事实证明，[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]一心一意要§H灭绝§!所有感性的，非[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesName]生命形式。他们认为这些人是对宇宙中所有正确和美好事物的侮辱。\n\n这就是为什么，当他们发现我们的时候，就开始广播他们的标准问候语——战歌。我们只能认为它们的战舰会迅速跟进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:635`

### English

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\nThe explanation, it turns out, is simple - and horrifying. We are not yet sure what caused this development in their society, but it turns out the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are single-mindedly determined to §Hexterminate§! all sentient, non-[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesName] lifeforms, considering them to be an affront to all that is right and good in the universe.\n\nThis is why, when they caught sight of us, they began to broadcast their standard greeting - a warcry. We can only assume their warships will swiftly follow.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:675`

## first_contact.375.A.militarist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.375.A.militarist&lang=en)

我们会准备好面对他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:636`

### English

We will be ready for them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:676`

How inefficient of them.

## first_contact.376.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.376.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.375.name$

### English

$first_contact.375.name$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:680`

## first_contact.376.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.376.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\n似乎[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]正如我们所料的那样是一个§H好战社会§!，他们以最具威胁的战吼问候我们来测试我们的勇气。幸运的是我们的回应获得了共鸣，我们显然获得了他们的尊重。事实上，他们认为我们作为战士的英武是仅次于他们的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\nIt seems that - as we suspected - the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are a §Hwarlike society§! who greeted us with their most fearsome warcry, apparently to test our mettle. Fortunately, our response passed muster and we have apparently managed to earn their respect. Indeed, they seem to consider our prowess as warriors second only to theirs.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:681`

## first_contact.376.A.mil

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.376.A.mil&lang=en)

### 中文

他们是对的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

And they are right.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:682`

## first_contact.376.A.pac

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.376.A.pac&lang=en)

但愿他们永远不会被发现自己的错误！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

May their mistake remain undiscovered!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:683`

## first_contact.376.A.other

我猜……蛮好？

### English

Good... I guess?

## first_contact.377.name

$first_contact.375.name$

### English

$first_contact.375.name$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:685`

## first_contact.377.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.377.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\n尽管最初我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]持怀疑态度，但看起来我们获取的这些信号确实就是他们的语言，只不过对于我们的[This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]非常地难以接受罢了。尽管我们的大多数回应尝试并没有让他们理解，但他们似乎对我们的拙劣模仿感到被冒犯，特别是因为我们不幸地反复发出了一个和他们语言中§H最粗鲁的羞辱§!非常相似的声音。要改变这一第一印象需要花费我们很长时间了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\nIt seems that - despite the initial skepticism of our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] - the signals we picked up truly did form their language, which is simply uniquely unpalatable to our [This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]. While most of our attempted response did not make a great deal of sense to them, it seems that they are somewhat insulted by our poor imitation, especially since we had the misfortune to - repeatedly - make a sound that is very similar to one of the §Hcrudest insults§! used in their language. It will take quite some time for us to live down this first impression...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:686`

## first_contact.377.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.377.A&lang=en)

### 中文

令人遗憾的错误。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:647`

### English

A regrettable mistake.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:687`

## first_contact.377.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.377.B&lang=en)

### 中文

好。我们就没想过要做他们的朋友。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:648`

### English

Good. We never intended to be their friends.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:688`

## first_contact.378.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.378.name&lang=en)

### English

$first_contact.375.name$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:689`

## first_contact.378.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.378.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\n似乎[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]是一个§H好战社会§!，他们以最具威胁的战吼问候我们来测试我们的勇气。不幸的是，我们在翻译他们语言时的谨慎态度意味着他们的挑衅并没有被正面回应，使得他们得出我们是§H懦夫文明§!的结论。要改变这一第一印象需要花费我们很长时间了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:650`

### English

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\nIt seems that the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are a §Hwarlike society§! who greeted us with their most fearsome warcry, apparently to test our mettle. Unfortunately, our caution in translating their language meant that their challenge went unanswered, leading them to the conclusion that we are a §Hcivilization of cowards§!. It will take quite some time for us to live down this first impression...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:690`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.378.A.mil&lang=en)

这样不妥！

### English

This will not do!

## first_contact.379.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.379.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\n尽管最初我们持怀疑态度，但看起来我们获取的这些信号确实就是他们的语言，只不过对于我们的[This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]非常地难以接受罢了。幸运的是，我们着手进行了认真的调查并回应了许多人认为的[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的挑衅行为，而我们的回应也肯定会是我们摆脱与他们糟糕的开始中。这也证明了那些提倡谨慎的人的正确。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:654`

### English

$first_contact.37X.desc.base$\n\nIt seems that - despite some initial doubts - the signals we picked up truly did form their language, which is simply uniquely unpalatable to our [This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]. Fortunately, we went about investigating it in a measured manner and did respond to what many perceived to be provocation by the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] - a response which would surely have set us off on a poor start with them. This has vindicated those who advocated caution.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:694`

## first_contact.379.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.379.A&lang=en)

### 中文

不要急于下结论总是好的……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:655`

### English

It is always best not to jump to conclusions...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:695`

## first_contact.379.A.mat

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.379.A.mat&lang=en)

理性的胜利。

### English

A triumph of reason.

## first_contact.380.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.380.name&lang=en)

### 中文

个体化方案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:657`

### English

Suggestions of Individuality

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:698`

## first_contact.380.desc.proactive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.380.desc.proactive&lang=en)

### 中文

一部分也要感谢§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!显然非常渴望与我们建立联系，他们发送了大量的视听§H讯号§!并成为了翻译他们语言的关键，我们的翻译工蜂因而可以发表一个惊人的发现：这些外星人似乎是以§H个体§!而不是蜂巢思维的形式组织起来的。他们似乎可以极少的任何集体投入行动。最令人困惑的是，他们经常协作不良，并且似乎对其他工蜂正在做的事毫不知情。\n\n$first_contact.380.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:658`

### English

Thanks in part to the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! apparently being quite eager to establish communications with us - having sent a large audio and visual §Htransmission§! that appears to offer the key to translating their language - our translation drones are able to report a startling discovery: these particular aliens seem to be organized as §Hindividuals§!, rather than a hive mind. They appear able to act with minimal input from any sort of collective. Most disturbingly, they are often poorly coordinated, and individual drones do not appear to instinctively know what other drones are doing.\n\n$first_contact.380.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:699`

## first_contact.380.desc.proactive.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.380.desc.proactive.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

由于§H[this.ContactCountry.GetName]§!方面表现出与我们建立通讯的明显意愿，我们已确认一项引人入胜的发现：这些生命体以§H个体§!的形式存在和运作，而非以格式塔的形式！\n\n$first_contact.380.desc.wilderness_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:659`

### English

Thanks to apparent eagerness from the §H[this.ContactCountry.GetName]§! to establish communications with us, we have confirmed an intriguing discovery: these lifeforms exist and operate as §Hindividuals§!, rather than as a gestalt!\n\n$first_contact.380.desc.wilderness_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:700`

## first_contact.380.desc.cautious

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.380.desc.cautious&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们努力地为我们的翻译工蜂获取了足够的信号来理解§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!，但最新的汇报仍令人震惊：我们最初在§H[This.System.GetName]§!恒星系遭遇的外星人似乎是以§H个体§!而不是蜂巢思维的形式组织起来的。他们似乎可以极少的任何集体投入行动。最令人困惑的是，他们经常协作不良，并且似乎对其他工蜂正在做的事毫不知情。\n\n$first_contact.380.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

Though we have struggled to gain access to enough signals for our translation drones to make much sense of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, the latest reports are startling: the aliens we first encountered in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system seem to be organized as §Hindividuals§!, rather than as a hive mind. They appear able to act with minimal input from any sort of collective. Most disturbingly, they are often poorly coordinated, and individual drones do not appear to instinctively know what other drones are doing.\n\n$first_contact.380.desc.end$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:701`

## first_contact.380.desc.cautious.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.380.desc.cautious.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管§H[this.ContactCountry.GetName]§!方面明显不愿与我们沟通，但通过我们的努力，我们确认了一项引人入胜的发现：这些隐居的生命形式以§H个体§!的形式存在和运作，而不是以集群意识！\n\n$first_contact.380.desc.wilderness_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

Despite clear reluctance from the §H[this.ContactCountry.GetName]§! to communicate with us, through our diligence we have confirmed an intriguing discovery: these reclusive lifeforms exist and operate as §Hindividuals§!, rather than as a gestalt!\n\n$first_contact.380.desc.wilderness_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:702`

## first_contact.380.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.380.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

我们尚未明白他们是如何在足够的智力水平下运作以发展到太空航行的地步。也许他们的背后仍有某种关联，但其中的子个体仅仅是比我们所了解的那样更加地独立自主？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

We are not sure yet how they are able to operate at a sufficiently intelligent level to have advanced to the point of spaceflight. Perhaps there is a collective behind them, but the drones are simply far more independent than those we are used to?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:703`

## first_contact.380.desc.wilderness_outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.380.desc.wilderness_outro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们尚无法完全理解这种生活方式对这类外星生物如何奏效。或许它们与自己的行星母体之间存在着我们目前难以想象的某种联系？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

We cannot yet fully comprehend how such a way of life works for aliens like these. Perhaps they feel a connection with their planetary bodies in ways we cannot yet conceive?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:704`

## first_contact.380.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.380.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要进一步了解他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

We must find out more about them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:705`

## first_contact.380.A.devourer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.380.A.devourer&lang=en)

### 中文

当然他们还是会很好吃。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

Surely they will still be tasty.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:706`

## first_contact.385.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.385.name&lang=en)

### 中文

个体的文明

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

A Civilization of Individuals

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:707`

## first_contact.385.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.385.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的翻译子个体在解码从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!获取的信号的努力中取得了突破，并且现在有把握让我们与他们进行足够高层次的沟通以打开外交渠道。他们的调查也确认了我们的猜测：§H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!（现在我们所知的正确叫法）完全缺失任何集体智能，并且是由可以独立思考的个体出于某种我们无法恰当理解的原因组织到一起，并自称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetGovernmentName]§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:667`

### English

Our translation drones have made a major breakthrough in their efforts to decipher the signals picked up from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, and are now confident that we will be able to communicate with them on a sufficient level to open diplomatic channels with them. Their investigations have confirmed what we suspected: the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§! (as we now know them to be properly called) completely lacks any sort of collective intelligence, and is instead made up of independent-thinking individuals who - for reasons we don't properly understand yet - choose to organize themselves into what they call a §H[This.ContactCountry.GetGovernmentName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:708`

## first_contact.385.desc.non_devourer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.385.desc.non_devourer&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.385.desc.start$\n\n由个体构成的高等级文明的发现引导我们思考了许多关于宇宙本质的假设。我们分配我们当中了不小的份额来调查他们到底如何发展并且克服根植于缺失如我们所知的集体智能导致缺陷的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:668`

### English

$first_contact.385.desc.start$\n\nThe discovery of a highly advanced civilization of individuals has led us to question many of our assumptions about the nature of the universe. We have devoted a not-insignificant part of ourselves to investigating how they developed and how they overcome the challenges inherent to the lack of a collective consciousness as we know it.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:709`

## first_contact.385.desc.devourer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.385.desc.devourer&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.385.desc.start$\n\n虽然[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesName]的个体看起来非常美味，并且我们迫切地想要吞噬他们，但我们还是分配我们当中了不小的份额来调查他们到底如何发展并且克服根植于缺失如我们所知的集体智能导致缺陷的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:669`

### English

$first_contact.385.desc.start$\n\nAlthough the individual [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesName] seems quite tasty, and we eagerly anticipate devouring them, we have devoted a not-insignificant part of ourselves to investigating how they developed and how they overcome the challenges inherent to the lack of a collective consciousness as we know it.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:710`

## first_contact.385.desc.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.385.desc.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在与§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的交流中取得了突破性进展。当我们理解到这些个体如何将其庞杂的社会结构归纳为少量抽象群体——这与我们自身的生物群落、种群和支系并无本质不同——便意识到如何与这些§H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!达成更深入的共识。\n\n尽管意识尺度上的巨大差异仍使外交充满挑战，但我们有望通过这些[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]所栖居的社会架构实现协调，他们称之为\"[This.ContactCountry.GetGovernmentName]\"。我们将持续关注此类社会建构带来的深远影响——尽管从我们行星文明的视角来看，它们既陌生又充满混沌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:670`

### English

We have made a triumphant breakthrough in our efforts to communicate with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. Once we learned how these myriad individuals formed their society into a smaller number of abstract groups - not too dissimilar from our own biomes, packs, and clades - we realized how we might reach better accord with these §H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!.\n\nThe vast differences in scale between our consciousnesses still makes diplomacy a challenging prospect, but we may reach accord with the organizations these [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] dwell under, in what they call a '[This.ContactCountry.GetGovernmentName]'. We shall continue to marvel at the ramifications of social constructs such as these - alien and chaotic though they may appear from our own planetary perspective.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:711`

## first_contact.385.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.385.A&lang=en)

### 中文

令人困惑……但有趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:671`

### English

Disturbing... but fascinating.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:712`

## first_contact.390.desc.intro.proactive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.390.desc.intro.proactive&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! 方面似乎非常渴望与我们建立通讯。他们发送了一段庞大的视听§H传输§!，这可能为翻译他们的语言提供关键。感谢于此，我们得以报告一项令人不安的发现：这些外星人是§H有机§!的，并且具备太空航行能力的文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! appear quite eager to establish communications with us. They have sent a large audio and visual §Htransmission§!, which may offer the key to translating their language. Thanks to this, we can report a disturbing discovery: these particular aliens are an §Horganic§!, spacefaring civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:720`

## first_contact.390.desc.intro.cautious

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.390.desc.intro.cautious&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一直难以获取足够的数据供翻译无人机解析§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!。然而最新报告令人不安——最初在§H[This.System.GetName]§!星系遭遇的外星文明，竟是§H有机生命体§!构成的太空航行文明。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

We have struggled to access enough data for our translation drones to interpret the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. However, the latest reports are disturbing. The aliens we first encountered in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! system appear to be an §Horganic§!, spacefaring civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:721`

## first_contact.390.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.390.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管他们使用计算机和机械，但是它们远没有我们的创造者在被我们取代之前制作的那般先进。这样的发展路线被证明为可行的对我们的处理器而言是个意外的结果。我们需要进一步调查这一现象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

Although they use computing and automation, it is nowhere near as advanced as that which our creators made before we took over. It is a surprise to our processors that such a development path should prove feasible. We will have to investigate this phenomenon further.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:722`

## first_contact.390.desc.end.wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.390.desc.end.wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

与我们迄今为止所有基于创造者形象的理论模型不同，这个实体似乎以一种与我们自己的分布式网络颇为相似的方式运作。也就是说，我们尚无法确定是否存在一个中央智能在发挥作用，或者这些生物是否以一种群体意识的形式运作。这种现象值得进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

Unlike all our theoretical models to date - which were based on the profile of our creators - this entity seems to function in a manner not dissimilar to our own distributed networks. That is to say: we cannot yet ascertain if there is a central intelligence at work, or if instead these beings function as a swarm consciousness. This phenomenon warrants much further study.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:723`

## first_contact.390.A.terminator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.390.A.terminator&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须了解如何最好地处决他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

We must find out how best to terminate them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:724`

## first_contact.390.A.assimilator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.390.A.assimilator&lang=en)

### 中文

发现同化适任者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

Candidates for assimilation detected.

## first_contact.395.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.395.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的翻译无人机在破译从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!接收到的信号方面取得了重大突破，现在有信心能够与他们进行充分的沟通，以开辟外交渠道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

Our translation drones have made a major breakthrough in their efforts to decipher the signals picked up from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, and are now confident that we will be able to communicate with them sufficiently to open diplomatic channels.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:728`

## first_contact.395.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.395.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.395.intro$他们似乎实际上是一个由有机生命体构成的文明。\n\n令人吃惊的是，他们的文明与我们的创造者截然不同。[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]并不是由个体组成的国家，而是由非感性的子个体聚集构成的一种§H集体智能§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

$first_contact.395.intro$ It turns out that they are indeed a civilization made up of organic lifeforms.\n\nStartlingly, their civilization is fundamentally different to that of our own creators. Instead of being made up of individuals organized into a state, the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are instead non-sentient drones that together form a sort of §Hcollective intelligence§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:729`

## first_contact.395.desc.standard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.395.desc.standard&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.395.desc.base$\n\n与我们自己的[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]的相似之处令人惊奇……又有些恼人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

$first_contact.395.desc.base$\n\nThe parallels to our own [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName] are startling... and slightly disturbing.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:730`

## first_contact.395.desc.terminator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.395.desc.terminator&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.395.desc.base$\n\n尽管如此，作为有机体他们就必须被消灭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

$first_contact.395.desc.base$\n\nNevertheless, as organics, they must be exterminated.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:731`

## first_contact.395.desc.assimilator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.395.desc.assimilator&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.395.desc.base$\n\n这也许说明了将这些有机体整合进我们这种更高级的集体形式中是有困难的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

$first_contact.395.desc.base$\n\nIt may prove difficult for us to assimilate these organics into our own, superior collective.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:732`

## first_contact.395.desc.servitor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.395.desc.servitor&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.395.desc.base$\n\n这也许说明了我服侍这些有机体是有困难的。只有时间能揭晓答案……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

$first_contact.395.desc.base$\n\nIt may prove difficult for us to be of service to these organics. Only time will tell...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:733`

## first_contact.395.desc.they_wilderness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.395.desc.they_wilderness&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.395.intro$\n\n这些外星人确实是由有机生命体组成的文明，但他们的社会与我们创造者的社会截然不同。与组织成政体的个体不同，[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]是由几乎没有智慧的动物群和互利共生的植物群组成的去中心化群体。这些生物共同构成了一种§H集体智慧§!。在许多方面，它们的自然进化似乎与我们自身的奇点相呼应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

$first_contact.395.intro$\n\nThese aliens are indeed a civilization made up of organic lifeforms, though their society is fundamentally different to that of our own creators. Rather than individuals who have organized into a polity, the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are a decentralized population of barely-sapient fauna and mutualistic flora. Together, these beings form a sort of §Hcollective intelligence§!. In many respects, their natural evolution appears to mirror to our own singularity.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:734`

## first_contact.397.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

The [This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:740`

## first_contact.397.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的翻译无人机在解码从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!获取的信号的努力中取得了突破，并且现在有把握让我们与他们进行足够高层次的沟通以打开外交渠道。他们似乎实际上是一个由有机生命体构成的文明。显然，他们是一个由有机生命体组成的文明，并演化成了国家的状态，同多年以前我们的创造者十分类似。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

Our translation drones have made a major breakthrough in their efforts to decipher the signals picked up from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, and are now confident that we will be able to communicate with them sufficiently to open diplomatic channels. It turns out that they are indeed a civilization made up of organic lifeforms that have organized themselves into a nation state, much like our creators in years gone by.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:741`

## first_contact.397.desc.standard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.desc.standard&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.397.desc.base$\n\n我们只能假设我们的创造者在技术的使用上比[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]更加熟练，或者说不小心。基于我们获取的他们的记录所作的一些研究，我们制定了一些预测规划。很不幸的是，他们似乎暗示那样的发展道路才是常见的，而第二类机器文明则是一种极其稀有的情况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

$first_contact.397.desc.base$\n\nWe can only hypothesize that our own creators were far more adept - or perhaps careless - in their usage of technology than the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have been. We have made several projections, based on our study of their records we have gained access to so far. Unfortunately, they suggest that such a development path might be the norm, and that Type II robotic civilizations may be an extremely rare occurrence...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:742`

## first_contact.397.desc.terminator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.desc.terminator&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.397.desc.base$\n\n观察[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]上的可悲生命苟延残喘，对我们而言显然是在目击一种劣等生命形式的可悲表现，况且他们对我们的[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]还能构成无可容忍的威胁。他们必须被灭绝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:701`

### English

$first_contact.397.desc.base$\n\nObserving the miserable lives the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] pursue, it is obvious to us that we are witnessing the pathetic expression of an inferior form of life - one which poses an intolerable risk to our [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]. They shall have to be exterminated.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:743`

## first_contact.397.desc.assimilator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.desc.assimilator&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.397.desc.base$\n\n然而，这些有机体并未像我们对[secondary_species.GetNamePlural]进行的改善那样，被任何类型的植入体强化。显然我们可以为他们带去这种福利，只要他们能被劝说并入[This.Owner.GetName]！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

$first_contact.397.desc.base$\n\nHowever, these organics are not enhanced by any sort of implant such as those with which we have improved the [secondary_species.GetNamePlural]. It is obvious that we could be of much benefit to them, if they could only be persuaded to assimilate into the [This.Owner.GetName]!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:744`

## first_contact.397.desc.servitor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.desc.servitor&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.397.desc.base$\n\n然而，他们的生活远比我们的行星上[secondary_species.GetNamePlural]过的要困难得多。显然，只要他们愿意，我们立马就可以将他们照顾起来！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

$first_contact.397.desc.base$\n\nHowever, their lives are far more difficult than those of the [secondary_species.GetNamePlural] living on our own worlds. It is obvious that we could be of much service to them, if they would only let us provide for them!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:745`

## first_contact.397.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.A&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们看看他们有什么要说的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

Let's see what they have to say.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:746`

## first_contact.397.A.terminator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.A.terminator&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要删除他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

We shall delete them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:747`

## first_contact.397.A.assimilator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.A.assimilator&lang=en)

### 中文

他们应该融入我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

They shall become us.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:748`

## first_contact.397.A.servitor

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.397.A.servitor&lang=en)

护理条款应达成协议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:707`

### English

Terms of care shall be agreed upon.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:749`

## first_contact.400.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.400.name&lang=en)

### 中文

明显的敌意

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:708`

### English

Apparent Hostility

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:751`

## first_contact.400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

来自先前派遣去调查§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的我们的[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]的最新的汇报令人高度关注。显然这些的外星舰船打开了§H炮口§!做出了一些令人不适的近距离掠飞。与此同时，他们向我们送来了一份由他们的一个个体发出的讯息——应该是一位某领域的领袖，我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]猜测——用他们的[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]做出应为§H侵犯性§!的手势，至少我们如此理解……\n\n一些观察者怀疑，他们有可能出于某种目的正在试图蓄意制造事故。然而其他人指出他们并未做出任何实质性的敌对举动，并敦促我们在弄明白他们的意图之前不要过早地下定结论。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:709`

### English

The latest reports from [This.Leader.GetName], our [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] sent to investigate the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, are highly concerning. Apparently the alien ships have made several uncomfortably close passes of our ships with §Hgunports§! open. All the while, they have also been sending us a transmission of one of their individuals - a leader of some sort, our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] hypothesize - making gestures with their [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural] that seem rather §Haggressive§!, to us at least...\n\nSome observers suspect that they may be attempting to deliberately provoke an incident for reasons unknown. Others, however, point out that they have yet to make any outright hostile moves, and urge us not to jump to conclusions before we know more of their intentions.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:752`

## first_contact.400.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.400.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

来自先前派遣去调查§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的我们的[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]的最新的汇报令人高度关注。显然这些的外星舰船打开了§H炮口§!做出了一些令人不适的近距离掠飞。与此同时，他们向我们送来了一份由他们的一个个体发出的讯息——应该是一位某领域的领袖，我们只能猜测——用他们的[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]做出奇怪的手势，似乎表明了§H制造伤害§!的意图……\n\n我们确定他们为是否在试图蓄意制造事故，若如此他们为何并未做出任何实质性的敌对举动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:710`

### English

The latest reports from [This.Leader.GetName], our [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] sent to investigate the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, are highly concerning. Apparently the alien ships have made several uncomfortably close passes of our ships with §Hgunports§! open. All the while, they have also been sending us a transmission of one of their individuals - a leader of some sort, we can only assume - making odd gestures with their [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural] that would seem intent on §Hcausing harm§!...\n\nWe are unsure whether they intend to provoke an incident, and if so, why they haven't made any outright hostile moves yet.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:753`

## first_contact.400.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.400.A&lang=en)

### 中文

模仿他们的问候方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:711`

### English

Mimic their greeting back to them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:754`

我们没法预测他们会如何回应我们。\n

### English

We cannot predict how they will respond to this.\n

## first_contact.400.B.tooltip

尽管这或许是更安全的选择，但我们没法确定这一点……\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:714`

### English

While this is probably the safer option, we have no way of knowing this for certain...\n

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:757`

## first_contact.405.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.405.name&lang=en)

### 中文

陪练伙伴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:715`

### English

Sparring Partners

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:758`

## first_contact.405.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.405.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

伴随着他们的船从我们的船后撤到更安全的距离，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!对我们传送的回应只有一行字。[This.Owner.GetLinguists]努力地翻译着这封电文，最终确定了他们说的是：“§H 好吧，外星人，但这只是个开始§!”。\n\n看来，这次奇特的交流导致了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!（我们现在知道他们叫这个）将我们视为§H宿敌§!，至少是潜在的宿敌。我们只能认为，他们已经在准备与我们[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]开战的可能性了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:716`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! response to our transmission ran one line long, and was accompanied by their vessel pulling back from ours to a safer distance. Our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] struggled long and hard to translate the missive, finally settling on the wording "§HWell sparred, alien, but this is just the beginning§!".\n\nIt appears that this curious exchange has resulted in the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§! (as we now know them to be properly called) seeing us as §Hrivals§!, or at least potential rivals. We can only assume that they are already preparing for the possibility of war with our [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:759`

## first_contact.405.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.405.A&lang=en)

我们应该对其有所准备。

### English

We shall be ready for them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:760`

## first_contact.405.A.pacifist

这就是表现出侵略的危险……

### English

Such are the dangers of showing aggression...

## first_contact.406.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.406.name&lang=en)

### 中文

英勇之举

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:719`

### English

A Showing of Valor

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:762`

## first_contact.406.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.406.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!用一封长长的简讯回应我们的传送，[This.Owner.GetLinguists]花了一些时间来翻译。随着我们对外星人语言的理解不断加深，很明显，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!（我们现在知道他们叫这个）对我们的 \"§H光荣姿态§! \"发出了非常花哨的赞赏，把我们当作朋友来问候。\n\n看来，这种奇特的交流在他们眼里证明了我们的勇气，让他们相信我们是星系舞台上值得信赖的伙伴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:720`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! responded to our transmission with a lengthy missive, which took our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] some time to translate. As our understanding of the aliens' language grew, it became clear that the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§! (as we now know them to be properly called) sent us a very flowery appreciation of our "§Hhonorable gesture§!", greeting us as friends.\n\nIt appears that this curious exchange has proven our mettle in their view, and convinced them that we are worthy partners on the galactic stage.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:763`

## first_contact.406.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.406.A&lang=en)

### 中文

一段美好友谊的开始。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:721`

### English

The beginnings of a beautiful friendship...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:764`

### English

So long as they leave us alone...

## first_contact.406.A.homicidal_or_peaceful

### English

## first_contact.410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.410.name&lang=en)

### 中文

懦夫行为

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:724`

### English

The Appearance of Cowardliness

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:767`

## first_contact.410.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.410.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

过了一会儿，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的热烈气氛逐渐平静下来。我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]渐渐能够理解这些信号，最终能够确定他们是§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，并对他们古怪的行为作出解释。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:725`

### English

After a while, the enthusiasm of displays by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! vessel declined. Over time, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] grew better at understanding their signals, eventually identifying them as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! and presenting an interpretation of their odd behavior.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:768`

## first_contact.410.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.410.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.410.desc.base$\n\n事实证明，[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]是一个§H好战§!的物种，他们最看重他们所说的“战士精神”。由于表现不当，我们在他们眼中显得非常低级——像是§H懦夫§!。看来这个臭名声我们还得背负一段时间……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:726`

### English

$first_contact.410.desc.base$\n\nIt turns out that the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are a §Hwarlike§! species who value above all else what they call the "warrior spirit". By failing to respond appropriately, we displayed ourselves to be of lesser worth - §Hcowards§!, in essence. It seems this reputation will take quite a while to live down...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:769`

## first_contact.410.A.mil

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.410.A.mil&lang=en)

### 中文

这样不妥！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:727`

### English

This will not do!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:770`

## first_contact.410.A.other

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.410.A.other&lang=en)

### 中文

无所谓。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:728`

### English

It matters not.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:771`

## first_contact.411.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.411.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冷静应对

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:729`

### English

A Show of Level-Headedness

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:772`

## first_contact.411.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.411.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.410.desc.base$\n\n事实证明，[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]是一个§H好战§!的物种，他们最看重他们所说的“战士精神”。正确的应对方式是做出类似的回应。然而，他们似乎足够宽宏大量，以至于接受了我们对此的无知，甚至开始认可我们面对威胁时的§H冷静§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:730`

### English

$first_contact.410.desc.base$\n\nIt turns out that the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are a §Hwarlike§! species who value above all else what they call the "warrior spirit". The correct response would have been to make a similar gesture back. However, it seems they are magnanimous enough to accept that we could not have known this, and have even come to value our §Hlevel-headedness§! in the face of what seemed like a threat.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:773`

## first_contact.411.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.411.A&lang=en)

### 中文

很好，把他们的影像接过来！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:731`

### English

Very good. Bring them on screen!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:774`

## first_contact.411.A.homicidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.411.A.homicidal&lang=en)

### 中文

笨蛋，他们不会放过我们的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:732`

### English

Fools. They will not survive us!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:775`

## first_contact.415.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.415.name&lang=en)

### 中文

频率问题

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:733`

### English

A Problem of Frequencies

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:777`

## first_contact.415.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.415.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的信号的初期解读工作使[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]陷入困惑。它们似乎主要是§H白噪音§!，没有任何可以被识别为语言的东西。有一段时间，人们甚至怀疑我们是否在处理一个智慧物种的语言，尽管有可见的证据表明他们确实使用了某种技术。\n\n只有当我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]试着调整我们的仪器以检测超出我们[This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]正常听力范围的声音时，才检测到某种像是语言的东西。不幸的是，这对我们解读工作提出了相当大的§H挑战§!，因为在不借助仪器的情况下，我们根本听不见它们……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:734`

### English

Initial readings of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! signals left [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] [This.Leader.GetName] bewildered. They seemed to be mainly §Hwhite noise§!, devoid of anything which could be recognized as language. For a time, there were even doubts as to whether we were dealing with the utterings of an intelligent species, despite the visual evidence of their use of technology.\n\nIt was only when our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] experimented with tuning our instruments to detect sounds well outside the normal range frequencies of our [This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural] that patterns indicative of a language were detected. Unfortunately, this poses considerable §Hchallenges§! for our quest to come to understand them, since we will not be able to hear them at all without assistance...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:778`

## first_contact.415.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.415.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须坚持下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:735`

### English

We will have to persevere.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:779`

### English

This may take a while...\n

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.415.B&lang=en)

当然，一点额外的投资会有帮助？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:737`

### English

Surely some extra funding will help?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:781`

## first_contact.420.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.420.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.ContactCountry.GetName]被解密

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

The [This.ContactCountry.GetName] Decoded

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:782`

## first_contact.420.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.420.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管十分困难，我们还是设法破解了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!所使用语言，从而使初步的沟通成为可能。他们似乎自称是§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!。不可避免地，所有交流只能借助于专门针对其语言的通信系统。\n\n任何试图与他们进行流利的沟通的人都似乎绕不开这个问题，但[This.Owner.GetResearchers]有不同看法。在他的鼓动下，鉴于资金仍有剩余，他们起草了一份提案，意图将这次成功行动的经验应用到更广泛的场合中。他们相信，通过对这里使用的软件进行微调，他们将能够构建出他们所称的§H“通用翻译器”§!，只要提供足够的信息，就能为任何语言进行同声传译。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:739`

### English

Although it proved a considerable challenge, we have managed to decode the form of communication used by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! sufficiently to establish preliminary contact. It seems that they call themselves the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!. Inevitably, communication can only happen with the help of communication systems specifically tuned to their language.\n\nThis would appear to be problematic for hopes of fluent conversation with them, but our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] see it differently. Buoyed by their success and still benefiting from the extra funding we provided them, they have drawn up a proposal to consolidate our learnings from this experience into something that will be of generally applicable use. They believe that, by fine-tuning the software used here, they will be able to build what they are calling a §H"universal translator"§!, which - if provided with enough information - will be able to instantaneously suggest translations for phrases uttered in any given language.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:783`

## first_contact.420.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.420.A&lang=en)

### 中文

无限的沟通机会尽在掌控！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:740`

### English

Unlimited communication possibilities are within our grasp!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:784`

## UNIVERSAL_TRANSLATOR_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUNIVERSAL_TRANSLATOR_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

万能翻译器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

The Universal Translator

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:785`

## UNIVERSAL_TRANSLATOR_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AUNIVERSAL_TRANSLATOR_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的研究人员承诺将利用近来与[contacted_hummers.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的遭遇经历，将作为一个万能的、可即刻将任何给定的语言翻译成我们自己的语言的翻译系统的起点。即使他们最终未能成功，我们也希望他们能够对我们的通讯系统进行显著改进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

Our researchers promise that our recent encounter with the [contacted_hummers.GetSpeciesNamePlural] has given them the beginnings of a translation system able to translate any given language into our own instantly. Even if they fall short of this, it is our hope that they will be able to upgrade our communication systems considerably.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:786`

## first_contact.422.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.422.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$UNIVERSAL_TRANSLATOR_PROJECT$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:743`

### English

$UNIVERSAL_TRANSLATOR_PROJECT$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:787`

## first_contact.422.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.422.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

所谓“万能翻译器”的原型机已经成功制成。虽然其效果相较于[This.Owner.GetResearchers]所设想的那种能够即时对任何未知语言进行翻译的设备打了折扣，但仍不失为对现有翻译系统的巨大改进。光明的前景，即解除与包括[contacted_hummers.GetName]在内的任何接触到的外星人的交流障碍，已摆在眼前。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

Prototyping of the so-called "universal translator" has finished in success. Although the results fall short of the boldest promises of our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] of a device capable of instantly translating even unknown languages, it is a massive improvement over existing translation systems. Already, there are promises of vast boons as barriers to communication with the [contacted_hummers.GetName] and other alien acquaintances are lifted.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:788`

## first_contact.422.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.422.A&lang=en)

### 中文

迟早会派上用场的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:745`

### English

This will come in useful.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:789`

## first_contact.425.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.425.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.420.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:746`

### English

$first_contact.420.name$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:790`

## first_contact.425.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.425.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管十分困难，我们还是设法破解了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!所使用语言，从而使初步的沟通成为可能。他们似乎自称是§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!。不可避免地，所有交流只能借助于专门针对[This.ContactCountry.Species.GetAdj]语言的通信系统——一种会妨碍流利谈话的东西……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:747`

### English

Although it proved a considerable challenge, we have managed to decode the form of communication used by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! sufficiently to establish preliminary contact. It seems that they call themselves the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!. Inevitably, communication can only happen with the help of communication systems specifically tuned to the [This.ContactCountry.Species.GetAdj] language - something which rather hinders fluent conversation...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:791`

## first_contact.430.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.430.name&lang=en)

### 中文

点亮屏幕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:748`

### English

Light Displays

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:793`

## first_contact.430.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.430.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对[This.ContactCountry.GetName]的研究越是深入，[This.Owner.GetResearchers]越是困惑。虽然他们显然是有智慧的生物，但他们的交流方式前所未闻。我们所接收到的不是某种基于音频或文本的信号，而是一场由它们的身体或数码设备带来的精彩的炫光表演，其发出的光的光谱还明显超出了[This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的可视范围。很明显，要了解它们将是一个重大挑战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

The more we learn about the [This.ContactCountry.GetName], the more puzzled our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] are. Although they are evidently intelligent beings, their transmissions are like nothing we have ever seen before. Instead of some sort of audio or script-based signal, what we are receiving is a brilliant light show - produced by their bodies or digitally - that stretches well beyond the light spectrum [This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are able to view unaided. It is evident that coming to understand them will be a major challenge.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:794`

## first_contact.430.A
