## first_contact.435.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.435.name&lang=en)

### 中文

挡光板的另一侧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:752`

### English

Beyond the Light Barrier

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:797`

## first_contact.435.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.435.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.440.desc$\n\n进一步的研究中，我们惊奇地发现他们的发光模式与进出超空间航道的漩涡相似。我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]困惑于这种相似行为的含义，并提议对此进行深入研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

$first_contact.440.desc$\n\nDuring our studies, we discovered to our surprise that their patterns of light bear an astounding similarity to the swirls of interstellar space that make hyperlane travel possible. Our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] are confused as to what this connection may mean and propose further study into the topic.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:798`

## OCTOPODES_LIGHT_PATTERNS_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AOCTOPODES_LIGHT_PATTERNS_PROJECT&lang=en)

### 中文

[encountered_country.GetSpeciesAdj]图案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:754`

### English

[encountered_country.GetSpeciesAdj] Patterns

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:799`

## OCTOPODES_LIGHT_PATTERNS_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AOCTOPODES_LIGHT_PATTERNS_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

在与[encountered_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的接触过程中，我们发现他们用来沟通的发光模式和进出超空间航道的漩涡之间有着惊人的相似之处。对这个问题的进一步研究可能会使我们对星系的基本组成部分有所了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

During our encounter with the [encountered_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural], we discovered some astounding similarities between the patterns of light they use to communicate and the swirls of interstellar space that make hyperlane travel possible. Investigating further into this topic may shed some light on the fundamental building blocks of the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:800`

## first_contact.437.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.437.name&lang=en)

### 中文

超空间洞见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:756`

### English

Hyperlane Insights

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:801`

## first_contact.437.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.437.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[encountered_country.GetSpeciesAdj]的漩涡状发光模式和星际空间之间的联系取得了令人惊讶的成果。似乎[encountered_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]的发光规则在其不知道的情况下，竟与超空间随着时间的推移而流动的规则暗合，只是规模差别很大。\n\n之后，通过研究[encountered_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，我们将能够更好地预测超空间的流动，这将极大地扩展我们的传感器的探测范围。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:757`

### English

Our study into connections between patterns observed in interstellar space and the [encountered_country.GetSpeciesAdj] swirls of light has unearthed surprising results. It seems that the rules by which the [encountered_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] make their light patterns are - unbeknownst to them, apparently - the same as those by which the flows of hyperlane space drift over time, just on a vastly different scale.\n\nIt follows that, through studying the [encountered_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural], we will be able to better predict the flows of hyperlane space - significantly boosting our potential sensor range.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:802`

## first_contact.437.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.437.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们面前打开了一个新世界。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:758`

### English

We are now open to new possibilities.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:803`

## first_contact.440.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.440.name&lang=en)

### 中文

光线组成的语言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:759`

### English

A Language of Lights

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:804`

## first_contact.440.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.440.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的努力得到了回报，现在我们基本能够理解[This.ContactCountry.GetName]的语言并与之沟通了。他们自称[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural]，并说自己从未使用过口语，取而代之的是光线交流。虽然他们过去曾经使用过文字，但其民众认为它远不如视觉语言优雅高效。他们的大脑似乎完全胜任从复杂的图案中快速得出意义的工作，因此在大多数情况下，他们更喜欢使用视觉语言的交流形式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:760`

### English

Our efforts have paid off, and we are now able to understand the light signals given off by the [This.ContactCountry.GetName] and communicate with them, at least on a rudimentary level. It appears that the [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePlural], as they call themselves, never developed a spoken language and instead used their light displays. Though they used to use a written language, it is considered far less elegant and efficient than their visual language. Their brains seem perfectly adapted to rapidly derive meanings from even complex displays, so that they favor recordings of the latter in most cases.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:805`

## first_contact.440.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.440.A&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们看看他们有什么要说的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:761`

### English

Let's see what they have to say.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:806`

## first_contact.450.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.name&lang=en)

### 中文

诗歌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:762`

### English

Poetry

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:808`

## first_contact.450.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!信号中读取的信息让我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]及其兴奋。尽管这其中没有一个音节是我们能够识别的，但不可否认的是，声音本身是有韵律的，而且听起来莫名地令人感到舒缓。虽然我们无法确定，但[Owner.GetLinguists]坚信这是一首诗歌，对方是在友善地回应我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:763`

### English

The readings from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! signals have caused our [Owner.GetLinguists] a great deal of excitement. While not a single syllable is intelligible, the sounds themselves are undeniably rhythmical, and indeed curiously soothing. Although we cannot be sure, our [Owner.GetLinguists] are convinced that it is a form of poetry. They propose we answer in kind.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:809`

## first_contact.450.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.A&lang=en)

### 中文

当然了。用战歌回应！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:764`

### English

Of course. Send back a battle chant!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:810`

## first_contact.450.A.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.A.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

一旦刀兵齐举，\n旌旗拥、百万貔貅。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:765`

### English

Hark! Let the banners fly -\nFor now to war we ride

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:811`

## first_contact.450.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.B&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以分享一些关于求索的诗句。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:766`

### English

We can share some fine poetry about discovery.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:812`

## first_contact.450.B.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.B.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

路漫漫其修远兮，吾将上下而求索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:767`

### English

To strive, to seek, to find... and not to yield.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:813`

## first_contact.450.C

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.C&lang=en)

### 中文

用一些象征友谊的诗句打动他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:768`

### English

Bedazzle them with poetry about friendship.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:814`

## first_contact.450.C.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.C.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

海内存知己，天涯若比邻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:769`

### English

Shall I compare thee to a solar wind...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:815`

## first_contact.450.D

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.D&lang=en)

### 中文

给他们整点稀奇古怪的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:770`

### English

Send them something enigmatic.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:816`

## first_contact.450.D.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.D.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

石室诗士施氏嗜狮，\n施氏时时适市视狮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

Jolifanto bambla o falli bambla\ngrossiga m'pfa habla horem

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:817`

## first_contact.450.E

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.450.E&lang=en)

### 中文

没兴趣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:772`

### English

We don't have time for this.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:818`

## first_contact.455.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.455.name&lang=en)

### 中文

引起反感

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:773`

### English

Offense Caused

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:819`

## first_contact.455.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.455.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们播报了战歌数个小时后，来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的通讯中断了。他们把舰船远远地开到了有效攻击距离以外。可以认为他们应该是理解了我们信息的主旨，并且对此可一点都不感到高兴。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:774`

### English

Several hours after we broadcast the battle chant, transmissions from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! ceased. Their ship has positioned itself well out of effective attack range. We can surmise that they understood the gist of our message, and were not gladdened by what they heard.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:820`

## first_contact.460.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.460.name&lang=en)

### 中文

交换战歌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:775`

### English

An Exchange of War Chants

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:821`

## first_contact.460.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.460.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

就在我们发出信息的数分钟后，来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的信息发生了变化。内容依旧抑扬顿挫，但不再是和风细雨，而如同激起了惊涛骇浪。听起来像是一首战争的颂歌。他们并没有做出什么敌对的行为，而只是被我们的战歌所打动了而已。我们又发送了起初那首诗的几个不同版本，并且也快速地在整理[This.ContactCountry.GetName]战歌的大纲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:776`

### English

Within minutes of our transmission, the signals from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! changed. Still rhythmically flawless, they were no longer soothing, but rather upbeat, exciting. Much like a battle chant in fact. No hostile movements were made - they seem contented rather than provoked by our poetry. We have since sent several variations of the first chant, and in return are rapidly collecting a compendium of [This.ContactCountry.GetName] war poetry.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:822`

## first_contact.460.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.460.A&lang=en)

### 中文

和这个物种真是相见恨晚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:777`

### English

A species after our own hearts.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:823`

## first_contact.462.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.462.name&lang=en)

### 中文

适得其反的战歌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:778`

### English

War Chant Backfires

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:824`

## first_contact.462.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.462.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

好像§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!听完我们的战歌以后生气了。他们的几乎瞬间回以暴力。他们的武器开始开火，我们的舰船很幸运地全身而退。看来一场大战是不可避免了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:779`

### English

It appears the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! took offense to our battle chant. Their response was instantaneous - and violent. They began firing, and our vessel was fortunate to escape unharmed. It seems we have a fight on our hands!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:825`

## first_contact.465.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.465.name&lang=en)

### 中文

交换诗歌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:780`

### English

An Exchange of Poetry

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:826`

## first_contact.465.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.465.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对方对我们诗歌热忱回应：他们发来了一串不间断的长时间通讯，我们可以迅速地构建§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!行文字典。尽管我们还未能辨识这些单词的含义，但这些外星人好像对对应我们诗歌中的平仄清浊得心应手，这也便于我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]理解他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:781`

### English

The response to our poem appears to have been enthusiastic: the stream of broadcasts continues unabated, and we are developing a considerable library of §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! compositions. Although we have yet to put meanings to the words, these aliens have proven remarkably adept at matching the pitch and tone of our poems, something which is sure to help our [Owner.GetLinguists] interpret them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:827`

## first_contact.465.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.465.A&lang=en)

### 中文

他们真是彬彬有礼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:782`

### English

How pleasant of them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:828`

## first_contact.470.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.470.name&lang=en)

### 中文

调戏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:783`

### English

Flirtations

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:829`

## first_contact.470.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.470.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在几分钟的沉默后，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的通讯恢复了。尽管还不能理解，但他们的态度发生了明显的转变：我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]称听到了俏皮而扭捏的旋律。虽然有点难以置信，似乎他们在与我们调情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:784`

### English

After several long minutes of silence, the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! transmissions resumed. Although still incomprehensible, they were markedly different: a melody described by our [Owner.GetLinguists] as playful, almost bashful. Startlingly, it appears that they are flirting with us!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:830`

## first_contact.470.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.470.A&lang=en)

### 中文

一整个尴尬了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:785`

### English

How embarrassing.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:831`

## first_contact.470.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.470.B&lang=en)

### 中文

也不是不能理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:786`

### English

A reasonable response.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:832`

## first_contact.472.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.472.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

焦急等待的过程分秒如年，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!依旧没有回应。尽管他们不太可能理解我们诗歌的具体含义，但他们好像对里面的情感表达吓住了，开始转用一种更谨慎的态度与我们接触。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:787`

### English

Minutes turned into hours, and still there was no response from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. Though they couldn't possibly have understood the exact meaning of our poetry, it seems they were taken aback by the emotions expressed in it, and are now approaching us in a far more guarded manner.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:833`

## first_contact.474.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.474.name&lang=en)

### 中文

随机噪音

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:788`

### English

Random Noises

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:834`

## first_contact.474.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.474.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!以相似的方式回应了：抑扬顿挫的诗歌变成了随机的噪音，模仿着我们的嗓音。很遗憾，我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]表示，对这些声音的解码“完全没有用”，因为现在的这些信号根本没有任何规律可言。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:789`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! are responding in kind: rhythmical chants have turned to random noise, mimicking our own incoherent sounds. Despairingly, our [Owner.GetLinguists] describe our efforts to decode these sounds as "completely useless", owing to the fact that it is now impossible to detect any sort of pattern.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:835`

## first_contact.474.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.474.A&lang=en)

### 中文

感觉要失败了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:790`

### English

This will set us back.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:836`

## first_contact.475.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.475.name&lang=en)

### 中文

偃旗息鼓

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:791`

### English

Clashes End

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:837`

## first_contact.475.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.475.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们互相理解的加深，[This.ContactCountry.GetName]或者说§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!对我们的敌意缓和了。尽管仍有侵略性，他们好像把我们视为了乐意进一步交流的“可敬的战士”。至于这会是一段友谊的开端或者是战争的序曲我们仍不得而知，但至少我们已经重温了一遍先祖流传的战争之音！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:792`

### English

As we come to understand each other, hostilities with the [This.ContactCountry.GetName] - or rather, the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! - have ceased. Despite their aggression, they appear to consider us "honorable warriors" who they would gladly spar with again. Whether this is the beginning of friendship or a prelude to war remains to be seen, but at least we've been able to brush up on our war chants!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:838`

## first_contact.475.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.475.A&lang=en)

### 中文

奇怪的交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:793`

### English

A curious exchange.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:839`

## first_contact.480.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.480.name&lang=en)

### 中文

破译诗歌

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:794`

### English

Poetry Successfully Interpreted

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:840`

## first_contact.480.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.480.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的努力有了回报，现在我们能够破译[This.ContactCountry.GetName]，或者准确来说叫§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!向我们发送来的信息了。基于他们的构句方法得知，他们对诗词艺术有着难以想象的执着。这种儒雅风流的沟通方式不仅使我们获得了他们的尊重，同时也让我们充分地了解了他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:795`

### English

Our efforts have paid off, allowing us to interpret the missives sent by the [This.ContactCountry.GetName], who we now know to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!. Based on their compositional style, they place an incredible importance in the poetic arts. By participating in this lyrical exchange, we have not only earned their admiration, but also learned a considerable amount about them during the process.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:841`

## first_contact.485.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.485.name&lang=en)

### 中文

解密密语

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:796`

### English

Enigma Decoded

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:842`

## first_contact.485.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.485.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在冗长而困难的第一次接触流程后，我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]终于认出[This.ContactCountry.GetName]应该被称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!。他们祝贺我们解开了他们的字谜，但看起来对我们花费如此长的时间颇有不满。最后的最后，我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]得以研发出数种新的模式辨识方法，让我们在未来可以更容易破译语言。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:797`

### English

After a long and arduous first contact process, our [Owner.GetLinguists] can now identify the [This.ContactCountry.GetName] as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!. They congratulate us for solving their linguistic riddles, although they seem a little put out with us that it took this long. At the very least, our [Owner.GetLinguists] were able to pioneer several new methods of pattern recognition which should stand us in good stead in the future.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:843`

## first_contact.485.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.485.A&lang=en)

### 中文

行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:798`

### English

Phew.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:844`

## first_contact.490.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.490.name&lang=en)

### 中文

翻译奉承

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:799`

### English

Flattery Translated

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:845`

## first_contact.490.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.490.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的努力有了回报，现在我们能够破译[This.ContactCountry.GetName]，或者准确来说叫§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!向我们发送来的信息了：\n\n“啊，巧夺天工的谄媚。遇到另一个珍视诗歌的物种已是三生有幸，谁曾想到，亲爱的[Owner.GetSpeciesName]，你们竟是如此热情洋溢、乖唇蜜舌。祝福你们的[Owner.GetSpeciesOrganName]。”\n\n信息后面还有许多诸如此类的词句。我们也没法完全确定是该受宠若惊还是直接大吃一惊，但也许我们的关系是有了个不错的开端。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:800`

### English

Our efforts have paid off, and we can now interpret the missives sent by the [This.ContactCountry.GetName], who we now know to be the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!:\n\n"Ah, the fine art of flattery. It is nice indeed to meet another species that appreciates poetry, and especially one as exuberant and adorable as you, my sweet [Owner.GetSpeciesName] - bless your [Owner.GetSpeciesOrganName]."\n\nThe message continues at some length in a similar vein. We are not entirely sure whether to be flattered or taken aback, but it seems our relationship is off to a good start.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:846`

## first_contact.490.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.490.A&lang=en)

### 中文

他们还真不错。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:801`

### English

How nice of them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:847`

## first_contact.500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第一次接触展现敌意

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:802`

### English

Hostilities at First Contact

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:849`

## first_contact.500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

显然，我们的回应不合时宜：§Y[contact_country.GetName]§!的反应快速而充满敌意，他们开始向我们的舰船开火。似乎我们必须依靠自己的[Owner.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]来回敬他们了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:803`

### English

Clearly, our response was not the appropriate one: the response of the §Y[contact_country.GetName]§! was swift and hostile, as they began firing on our ships. It seems we have a fight on our [Owner.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:850`

## first_contact.500.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.500.A&lang=en)

### 中文

给他们点颜色瞧瞧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:804`

### English

To arms!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:851`

## first_contact.500.A.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.500.A.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[contact_country.GetName]§!将与我们敌对。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:805`

### English

The §Y[contact_country.GetName]§! are now hostile to us.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:852`

## first_contact.505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们实在不知道搞错了什么，§Y[contact_country.GetName]§!突然就向我们发起了攻击！看来我们必须靠自己的[Owner.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]回敬他们了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:806`

### English

It is not clear what went wrong, but the §Y[contact_country.GetName]§! are suddenly attacking us! It seems we have a fight on our [Owner.GetSpeciesHandNamePlural]!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:853`

## first_contact.5000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现清晰信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:808`

### English

Clear Signal Found

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:856`

## first_contact.5000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的传感器检测到§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!正在发送不断重复的清晰信号，似乎为我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]编码他们语言的工作提供了绝佳的材料。有了这份天降的幸事，语言学家们心态十分乐观，坚信很快就能有重大的进展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:809`

### English

Our sensors have detected a particularly clear repeating signal from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! that seems to offer our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] excellent material for codifying their language. They are optimistic that we will be able to make some significant progress on the back of this stroke of luck.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:857`

## first_contact.5000.desc2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5000.desc2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功地获取了数艘§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!舰船间对话的清晰音频讯号。在我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]还不能准确认识他们所记录下的单词的含义，但他们对于这段新的信号将会对突破带来重大进展抱有信心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:810`

### English

We have managed to pick up a clear audio feed of conversations between several §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! ships. While our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] are still none the wiser as to the meanings of any of the words they are recording, they are optimistic that this new signal will help them make significant progress towards a breakthrough.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:858`

## first_contact.5005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

信号匮乏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:811`

### English

A Lack of Signal

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:859`

## first_contact.5005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]日渐沮丧，因为我们没能提供给他们充分的材料用于解码§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!使用的语言，他们的进度十分有限。似乎这些外星人正使出浑身解数，防止不怀好意的[This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]窃听到他们的信号！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:812`

### English

Our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] are becoming increasingly frustrated at our failure to provide them with sufficient material to make progress on decoding the language used by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. It seems these particular aliens are doing their utmost to prevent their signals from being heard by the wrong [This.Owner.GetSpeciesEarNamePlural]!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:860`

## first_contact.5005.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5005.A&lang=en)

### 中文

继续尝试！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:813`

### English

Keep trying!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:861`

## first_contact.5010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

错误方向

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:814`

### English

Mistaken Identification

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:862`

## first_contact.5010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]在翻译§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!所使用的语言时遇到了重大§H挫折§!。不幸的是，他们认为的语言根本就不是一种语言。[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap][This.Leader.GetName]在[This.Leader.GetHerHis]的报告中指出，在几天内他们从多个角度的探讨，[This.Owner.GetLinguists]最终得到结论，这可能只是种试作音乐……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:815`

### English

It seems our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have suffered a major §Hsetback§! in their attempts at codifying the language used by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. Unfortunately, what they had taken to be their language quite simply was not a language. In [This.Leader.GetHerHis] report, [This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap] [This.Leader.GetName] states that, after days of investigating the matter from every angle, the [This.Owner.GetLinguists] finally concluded that it was in fact probably an attempt at music...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:863`

## first_contact.5010.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5010.A&lang=en)

### 中文

一个不幸的失误。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:816`

### English

An unfortunate error.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:864`

## first_contact.5015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.ContactCountry.GetName]扫描[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:817`

### English

[This.ContactCountry.GetName] Scan [This.Owner.Capital.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:865`

## first_contact.5015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

令许多[This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]大吃一惊的是，似乎一艘属于§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的飞船已经扫描了§H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!。我们对该行为出现了不同意见的分歧：我们是应该把这次冲击视为与外星文明建立更紧密的联系的跳板，或者是被认为一种警告，我们必须确保舰队准备万全，以免为时已晚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:818`

### English

In what comes as a profound shock to many [This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural], it has emerged that a vessel belonging to the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! has scanned §H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. Opinion is divided on whether we should treat this shock as a springboard to boost efforts to establish closer ties with alien civilizations, or whether it should be taken as a warning and that we must make sure our fleet is in good order now, before it is too late.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:866`

## first_contact.5015.desc.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5015.desc.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

令许多[This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]大吃一惊的是，似乎一艘属于[This.ContactCountry.GetName]的飞船已经扫描了§H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!。虽然有些人担心这些外星人可能不像我们这么友好，但人们已经达成共识，对于这种情况，我们应该加倍努力与星系其他文明建立更紧密的联系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:819`

### English

In what comes as a welcome surprise to many [This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural], it has emerged that a vessel belonging to the [This.ContactCountry.GetName] has scanned §H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. Although some worry that these aliens may not be as welcoming as we are, consensus has already been established that we should respond to this occurrence by redoubling our efforts to establish closer ties with the other civilizations of the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:867`

## first_contact.5015.desc.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5015.desc.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

令许多[This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]大吃一惊的是，似乎一艘属于§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的飞船已经扫描了§H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!。人们已经达成广泛共识，我们应该对这种入侵做出回应，以确保我们的舰队准备万全且为时未晚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:820`

### English

In what comes as a profound shock to many [This.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural], it has emerged that a vessel belonging to the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! has scanned §H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. Widespread consensus has emerged that we should respond to this trespass by making sure our fleet is in good order with all haste, before it is too late.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:868`

## first_contact.5015.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5015.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

令人不安的是，似乎一艘属于§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的飞船已经扫描了§H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!。我们不确定该如何应对此事：我们应当选择继续专注于与外星文明和平友好地共存，还是应该把这看做一种警醒并提高我们的舰队规模，以防万一可能的威胁呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:821`

### English

In a disturbing development, it seems that a vessel belonging to the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! has scanned §H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. We are unsure how to respond to this: should we choose to redouble our focus on peaceful and friendly coexistence with alien civilizations, or should we treat this as a warning and increase the size of our navy in case future hostilities are necessary?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:869`

## first_contact.5015.desc.gestalt.homicidal

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5015.desc.gestalt.homicidal&lang=en)

### 中文

令人不安的是，似乎一艘属于§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的飞船已经扫描了§H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!。我们应当扩充我们的舰队以保证这帮外星人不会有什么非分之想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:822`

### English

In a disturbing development, it seems that a vessel belonging to the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! has scanned §H[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. We should increase the size of our navy so that we may set about putting these aliens in their place.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:870`

发现[This.ContactCountry.GetName]首都

### English

[This.ContactCountry.GetName] Capital Discovered

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:873`

## first_contact.5020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经辨识出了先前我们在§H[This.ContactCountry.Capital.System.GetName]§!恒星系扫描的殖民地其实是§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的首都。我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]通过调查从扫描中获取的资料发现许多关于他们的有价值的线索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:826`

### English

We have identified the colony we recently scanned in the §H[This.ContactCountry.Capital.System.GetName]§! system to be the capital of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. Our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] have found many valuable clues about them by investigating the data we gathered from the scan.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:874`

## first_contact.5025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

前途渺茫

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:827`

### English

Trail Goes Cold

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:875`

## first_contact.5025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在已经有一段时间没有获取到来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的视听资料了。我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]发现他们似乎没有什么新的信息值得获取，并担心这条路似乎行不通了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:828`

### English

We have caught neither sight nor sound of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! for some time now. Our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] find they have little information to go on and worry that the trail may have gone cold.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:876`

## first_contact.5025.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5025.A&lang=en)

继续搜索！

### English

Keep searching!

## first_contact.5030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

未知的美好

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:830`

### English

The Delight of the Unknown

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:878`

## first_contact.5030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们获取到越来越多被称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的外星人的图像，它们不可避免地逐渐被泄露到公众视野当中。他们的外表令[This.Owner.GetAdj]的公众感到兴奋，大家已经迫不及待想要和这些外星人进一步接触了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:831`

### English

As we began to pick up more and more visual images of the aliens known as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, it was inevitable that these would eventually be leaked to the wider public. Their appearance has proven delightful to the [This.Owner.GetAdj] public, who are now impatient to make closer acquaintance with these aliens.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:879`

## first_contact.5030.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5030.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们获取到越来越多被称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的外星人的图像，我们很快意识到我们正在与一个机器人社会打交道。这些消息被泄露到公众视野当中只是时间的问题。这些发现引起了[This.Owner.GetAdj]公众的遐想，大家惊叹于机器居然可以自行建立一个太空文明，并且迫不及待地想进一步熟识它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:832`

### English

As we began to pick up more and more visual images of the aliens known as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, it quickly became clear that we are dealing with a society of robots. It was only a matter of time before this knowledge was leaked to the wider public. The revelation has provoked wonder among the [This.Owner.GetAdj] public, who marvel that machines could build their own spacefaring civilization, and are impatient to make closer acquaintance with them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:880`

## first_contact.5035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5035.name&lang=en)

可怖一幕

### English

## first_contact.5035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们获取到越来越多被称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的外星人的图像，它们不可避免地逐渐被泄露到公众视野当中。我们中的许多人对它们的外表感到惊悚，并称他们为§H“令人作呕的[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]§!”。并且，已有许多人要求我们采取措施以应对新的“[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesClass]”威胁……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:836`

### English

As we began to pick up more and more visual images of the aliens known as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, it was inevitable that these would eventually be leaked to the wider public. Their appearance has provoked widespread dismay amongst our populace, who have taken to describing them as §H"repulsive [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]"§!. Already, many are demanding that we take action to deal with the new "[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesClass] threat"...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:884`

## first_contact.5035.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5035.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们获取到越来越多被称为[This.ContactCountry.GetName]的外星人的图像，我们再也无法忽视这一事实：我们正要面对的是一个机器人社会。这些消息被泄露到公众视野当中只是时间的问题。我们中的许多人对它们的外表感到惊悚，并称他们为§H“令人作呕的[This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]§!”。并且，已有许多人要求我们采取措施以应对新的“机械威胁”……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:837`

### English

As we began to pick up more and more visual images of the aliens known as the [This.ContactCountry.GetName], we could no longer deny it: we are dealing with a society of robots. It was only a matter of time before this knowledge was leaked to the wider public. The revelation has provoked widespread horror amongst our populace, who have taken to describing them as §H"repulsive [This.ContactCountry.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]"§!. Already, many are demanding that we take action to deal with the new "mechanical threat"...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:885`

### English

## first_contact.5035.B

他们不会那么糟糕的。

### English

## first_contact.5040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迷惑的信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:840`

### English

Confusing Signals

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:888`

## first_contact.5040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]已经在大量收集来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的信号取得了稳步的进展。然而，随着我们对他们语言的知识储备越来越丰富，我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]却越来越感到迷惑。很明显他们不得不对先前工作的翻译进行数次重新评估，因为他们发现先前认定文字的含义事实上§H极度不准确§!。似乎[This.ContactCountry.GetName]的整体面貌和我们极度相异——比我们能够想象的差的更多！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:841`

### English

Our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] have been making steady progress gathering together a large collection of signals from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. However, as our knowledge of their language has grown, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have become ever more confused. Apparently, they have had to reassess their previous working translations several times after finding out that the meanings they had ascribed to words were, in fact, §Hextremely inaccurate§!. It seems that the whole outlook of the [This.ContactCountry.GetName] is very different to ours - far more than we could possibly have imagined!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:889`

## first_contact.5040.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5040.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们在大量收集来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的信号方面取得了稳步的进展，而我们在真正理解这些信号的方面的进展……被彻底压倒了。我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]已经被迫对他们先前的翻译工作进行了数次的重新评估，因为发现他们之前认定文字的含义事实上§H非常不准确§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:842`

### English

While we have made steady progress on gathering together a large collection of signals from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, our progress on coming to actually understand these signals has been... underwhelming. Already, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have been forced to reassess their previous working translations several times after finding out that the meanings they had ascribed to words were, in fact, §Hextremely inaccurate§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:890`

## first_contact.5040.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5040.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.5040.desc.gestalt$似乎[This.ContactCountry.GetName]的思维模式和我们相异甚远——比我们之前认为的差的更多，即使是对于外星人而言！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:843`

### English

$first_contact.5040.desc.gestalt$ It seems that the thought patterns of the [This.ContactCountry.GetName] are very different to our own - far more than we had considered possible, even among aliens!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:891`

## first_contact.5040.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5040.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.5040.desc.gestalt$似乎[This.ContactCountry.GetName]的执行逻辑并不是基于任何一种我们所知的理性。这将会带给我们理解他们更多的挑战！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:844`

### English

$first_contact.5040.desc.gestalt$ It seems that the [This.ContactCountry.GetName] does not base its execution logic on any sort of rationality known to us. This will make understanding them a severe challenge!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:892`

## first_contact.5040.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5040.A&lang=en)

### 中文

这最终会变的明朗的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:845`

### English

It will become clear, eventually.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:893`

## first_contact.5045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

快速翻译

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:846`

### English

Swift Translations

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:894`

## first_contact.5045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们收集到更多有关§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的资料，我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]非常惊讶于进展速度是如此之快。似乎——如果他们是对的话——这些外星人和我们对于整个星系具有大量相同的看法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

As we have gathered more material on the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have been surprised at the fast pace of their progress. It seems that - if they are right - these aliens share much of our outlook on the galaxy as a whole.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:895`

## first_contact.5045.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5045.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们在§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!取得更多的材料，我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]非常惊讶于进展速度是如此之快。似乎——如果他们是对的话——这些外星人与我们具有非常相似的思维模式。或许他们的意识与我们的[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]是相似的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:848`

### English

As we have gathered more material on the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have been surprised at the fast pace of their progress. It seems that - if they are right - these aliens share very similar thought patterns to our own. Perhaps their consciousness is alike to our own [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:896`

## first_contact.5045.desc.machine

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5045.desc.machine&lang=en)

### 中文

随着我们在§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!取得更多的材料，我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]非常惊讶于进展速度是如此之快。似乎——如果他们是对的话——这些外星人和我们具有非常相似的执行逻辑。或许在这些信号之后的人工智能与我们的[This.Owner.GetGovernmentName]非常相似？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:849`

### English

As we have gathered more material on the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have been surprised at the fast pace of their progress. It seems that - if they are right - these aliens share a very similar execution logic to our own. Perhaps an AI much like our own [This.Owner.GetGovernmentName] lies behind these signals?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:897`

## first_contact.5045.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5045.A&lang=en)

这对我们之间未来的关系是一个好的预示。

### English

This bodes well for our future relations with them.

## first_contact.5045.B

### English

Of course. Why would they not abhor the xeno?

## first_contact.5045.C

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5045.C&lang=en)

遇到与我们相似的文明是件好事。

### English

It will be good to meet those who are like ourselves.

## first_contact.5050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

错误翻译

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

Mistaken Translations

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:901`

## first_contact.5050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!信号的语言解析工作遇到了挫折。似乎我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]对翻译中的关键假设已经被§H证伪§!。尽管这意味着我们许多的进展不得不被推倒重来，但至少我们未来的努力很可能会被建立在更可靠的基础之上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:854`

### English

Our attempts to decode the language used in the signals from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!  have hit a setback. It seems that a key assumption our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] had been relying on in their translations has been §Hconclusively disproven§!. While this means much of our progress will have to be revised, at least our future efforts will hopefully be built upon a more reliable basis.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:902`

## first_contact.5055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

稳步进展

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:855`

### English

Steady Progress

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:903`

## first_contact.5055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]最近在解析来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!信号所用的语言取得了明显的进展。很明显他们现在坚信自己已经对该语言的基础有了坚实的把控，并且承诺他们能够很快报道进一步的发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:856`

### English

Our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have recently made significant progress towards decoding the language used in the signals from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. Apparently they now believe themselves to have a solid grasp of its fundamentals and promise that they will be able to report further findings soon.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:904`

## first_contact.5055.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5055.A&lang=en)

### 中文

继续干吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:857`

### English

Keep at it!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:905`

## first_contact.5065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

探测到清晰信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:858`

### English

Clear Signal Detected

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:906`

## first_contact.5065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们历经艰辛以在§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!足够的信息从而让我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]推进进程，但是现在我们的努力终于获得了一个极大的推动：我们的传感器收集到了他们数艘船只之间颇具长度的交流。我们只能猜测是他们的戒备突然松懈了一下，或者是他们的科技并没有我们怀疑的那么先进，他们只是自己觉得通信通道是安全的……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:859`

### English

We had struggled to gather enough information on the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! for our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] to have much to go, but now our efforts have received a major boost: our sensors have picked up a lengthy exchange between several of their ships. We can only assume their guard slipped for a moment, or perhaps their technology is not as advanced as we had suspected and they thought their channel to be secure...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:907`

## first_contact.5070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

色彩语言学

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:860`

### English

Colorful Linguistics

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:908`

## first_contact.5070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]在解码§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!语言的过程中取得了一项突破性进展。他们使用的文字系统里包含了将近一千七百万种字符，每个字符恰好可以和红-绿-蓝色值一一对应。尽管颇为费解，我们的语言学家们认为他们是可以破译这种外星语言的，只是还需要再花上一些时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:861`

### English

Our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have made a breakthrough deciphering the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! language. They use an alphabet consisting of almost seventeen million unique characters, which in turn correspond with a color on the red-green-blue spectrum. Although convoluted, our linguists are convinced they will be able to decipher the alien language. They just need some more time.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:909`

## first_contact.5070.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5070.A&lang=en)

### 中文

一路绿灯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:862`

### English

Green lights all the way.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:910`

## first_contact.5071.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5071.name&lang=en)

### 中文

解码彩色语言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:863`

### English

Deciphering Colorful Language

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:911`

## first_contact.5071.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5071.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过运用十六进制数位系统，我们的数学家和[This.Owner.GetLinguists]几乎就快要破译§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的色彩语言了。官方也正在起草第一次接触协议。\n\n通过对§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!语言的研究，我们发现他们的文化包罗万象，而且沟通上产生误解的可能性非常高。现在我们必须决定沟通的方针究竟是倾向于他们的逻辑型还是偏向他们的情感表达……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:864`

### English

By utilizing a hexadecimal positional numeral system, our mathematicians and [This.Owner.GetLinguists] are on the verge of a colorful language breakthrough with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. Officials are preparing first contact protocols.\n\nThrough studying the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! language, we know their culture is vast. The potential risk for misunderstanding is high. Now we must decide if we should appeal to their sense of logic, or to their emotions...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:912`

## first_contact.5071.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5071.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

通过运用十六进制数位系统，我们几乎就要破译§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的色彩语言了。第一次接触协议也正在起草中。\n\n我们现在可以选择通过富有文化特质的问候来取悦他们的情感或是尝试进行一种更复杂的但高效的表达方式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:865`

### English

By utilizing a hexadecimal positional numeral system, the collective is on the verge of a colorful language breakthrough with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. We are preparing first contact protocols.\n\nWe can now either pander to their emotions with a culturally significant greeting, or we can attempt a more complex protocol to increase potential efficiency.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:913`

## first_contact.5071.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5071.A&lang=en)

### 中文

用音乐与他们打招呼吧！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:866`

### English

Contact them in song!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:914`

## first_contact.5071.A.tooltip

我们会尝试与[This.ContactCountry.GetName]建立情感联系。

### English

We will attempt to forge an emotional connection with the [This.ContactCountry.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:915`

## first_contact.5071.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5071.B&lang=en)

### 中文

在交流中避免情绪化。

### English

Keep the emotion out of it.

## first_contact.5072.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5072.name&lang=en)

### 中文

情感连接!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:869`

### English

Emotional Connection!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:917`

## first_contact.5072.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5072.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试与§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!进行性感层面上的沟通取得了不错的收获。\n\n我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]展示了对他们文化的理解，结果他们对我们表示了暖心的欢迎。任务“漂亮”地完成了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:870`

### English

Efforts at connecting on an emotional level with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have proven fruitful.\n\nOur [This.Owner.GetLinguists] demonstrated an understanding of their culture, and as a result, we have been warmly received. Mission accomplished, with great panache!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:918`

## first_contact.5072.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5072.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

以情感为杠杆，我们获得了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的青睐。\n\n我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]展现了对他们文化的理解，结果他们对我们表示了暖心的欢迎。任务完成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:871`

### English

Leveraging emotion, we have gained favor with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.\n\nOur [This.Owner.GetLinguists] demonstrated an understanding of their culture, and as a result, we have been warmly received. Mission accomplished.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:919`

## first_contact.5073.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5073.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灾难性羞辱！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:872`

### English

Catastrophically Insulting!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:920`

## first_contact.5073.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5073.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尝试与§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!进行情感层面的沟通产生了灾难性的后果。\n\n我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]没能展示对他们文化的良好理解，反倒是严重地冒犯了他们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:873`

### English

Efforts at connecting on an emotional level with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! have proven catastrophic.\n\nOur [This.Owner.GetLinguists] failed to demonstrate an understanding of their culture, and as a result, we have greatly offended them!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:921`

## first_contact.5073.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5073.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管借助了情感，我们仍没能获得§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的青睐\n\n我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]没能展示对他们文化的良好理解，反倒是严重地冒犯了他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:874`

### English

Leveraging emotion, we have failed to gain favor with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!\n\nOur [This.Owner.GetLinguists] were unable to demonstrate an understanding of their culture, and as a result, they are deeply offended.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:922`

## first_contact.5075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无效沟通

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:875`

### English

Communication Obfuscation

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:923`

## first_contact.5075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的语言看似包含了数百万看似冗余的音节，似乎仅仅只是他们古代语言残留下来约定俗成的东西。在翻译的时候，这些不必要的填充性词汇唯一的作用看起来只是用来补充语法的完整性，或者最多有一些微不足道的上下文提示作用。\n\n幸好，我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]已经缩减了他们语言中这些过时的部分，制定出了简化版辞典，这样一来我们就能与§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!进行高效的沟通。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:876`

### English

The language of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! contains millions of seemingly redundant sounds, apparently dialectic hangovers from their precursor civilizations. When translated, these apparently superfluous filler words seem to serve no purpose other than to add linguistic flavor - or at most have only minor contextual implications.\n\nThankfully, our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] have narrowed down the obsolete aspects of their language, and are now compiling an abridged lexicon which should prove very useful for efficient communication with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:924`

## first_contact.5080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

穿梭机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:878`

### English

The Shuttle

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:927`

## first_contact.5080.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5080.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!在太空中的一艘太空梭靠近了我们。由于没有发生其他事情，我们假设他们希望在远离他们主舰船的地方会面。由于船只尺寸较小，只有我们的[Owner.GetEnvoy][Leader.GetName]被派去，其他外交团队成员留在后方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:879`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! positioned a shuttle close to us in space. Since nothing further happened, we assumed that they wished to meet away from their main ship. The small size of the vessel meant that only our [Owner.GetEnvoy] [Leader.GetName] could be sent, with the rest of the diplomatic team staying behind.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:928`

## first_contact.5080.desc.weapon

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5080.desc.weapon&lang=en)

### 中文

穿梭机配备了精妙的武器系统，外星人已经准备好进行武力对抗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:880`

### English

The shuttle is outfitted with elaborate weapon systems and the aliens are prepared for a violent confrontation.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:929`

## first_contact.5080.desc.functional

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5080.desc.functional&lang=en)

### 中文

太空梭讲究实用，体积很大。显然功能比外观的优先级更高。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:881`

### English

The shuttle is utilitarian and bulky. Function has clearly been prioritized over form.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:930`

## first_contact.5080.desc.bling

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5080.desc.bling&lang=en)

### 中文

太空梭上装饰着精美的装饰品和金色的铆钉。显然，它的设计旨在通过奢华的展示给人留下深刻的印象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:882`

### English

The shuttle is covered in elaborate ornaments and golden rivets. It is clearly designed to impress with a display of opulence.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:931`

## first_contact.5080.desc.standard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5080.desc.standard&lang=en)

### 中文

外星人的太空梭和我们的设计款式很类似，这在某种程度上让人舒心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:883`

### English

The alien shuttle is similar to our own designs, which is reassuring in a way.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:932`

## first_contact.5085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

等待之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:884`

### English

The Wait

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:934`

## first_contact.5085.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5085.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]返回报告说，没有来自§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的人接见[Leader.GetHimHer]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:885`

### English

[Leader.GetName] returned to report that no one from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! met [Leader.GetHimHer].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:935`

## first_contact.5085.desc.meditation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5085.desc.meditation&lang=en)

### 中文

无人驾驶的太空梭内设有一个中央会议室。地面和墙壁覆盖着柔软的材料，而来自外星世界的平静自然声响则从隐藏的扬声器中播放出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:886`

### English

The pilotless shuttle contained a central meeting room. The floor and walls were covered in a soft material, while calming natural sounds from an alien world played from hidden speakers.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:936`

## first_contact.5085.desc.pool

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5085.desc.pool&lang=en)

### 中文

无人驾驶的太空梭内设有一个中央会议室，其中一端有一个水池。外星符号环绕着水池周围的地板。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:887`

### English

The pilotless shuttle contained a central meeting room with a pool of water in one end. Alien symbols surrounded the floor around the pool.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:937`

## first_contact.5085.desc.imposing

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5085.desc.imposing&lang=en)

### 中文

无人驾驶的太空梭内设有一个中央会议室，其装修风格既低调又奇异地给人一种庄严的感觉。墙壁的角度和装饰设计旨在将观者的注意力引向一个高高的讲台。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:888`

### English

The pilotless shuttle contained a central meeting room with an architectural style that was at the same time understated and oddly imposing. The angles of the walls and their ornamentation were designed to draw the viewer's attention towards a raised dais.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:938`

## first_contact.5085.desc.standard

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5085.desc.standard&lang=en)

### 中文

无人驾驶的太空梭内设有一个中央会议室，令人惊讶的是，它非常朴素，缺乏任何装饰或装饰品。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:889`

### English

The pilotless shuttle contained a central meeting room that was surprisingly nondescript and lacking in decoration or adornment of any kind.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:939`

## first_contact.5085.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5085.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

有一面墙上装满了记录设备和传感器。站在冷漠的机器前，[Leader.GetName]概述了我们物种的历史和文化。演示没有得到任何回应。[Leader.GetName]感到十分沮丧，只能留下一个视频平板离开，平板中包含了大量历史记录和我们语言使用的入门指南。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:890`

### English

One wall was covered in recording equipment and sensors. Standing before the impassive machines, [Leader.GetName] outlined our species' history and culture. The presentation was met with silence. Frustrated, [Leader.GetName] left behind a video tablet with a vast collection of historical records and primers in our language.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:940`

## first_contact.5085.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5085.a&lang=en)

### 中文

拭目以待吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:891`

### English

Now we wait.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:941`

## first_contact.5090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

手段

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:892`

### English

The Method

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:943`

## first_contact.5090.desc.proactive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5090.desc.proactive&lang=en)

### 中文

单方面的沟通让外交团队遭受巨大的挫折。他们渴望成功的第一次接触，为此他们在没有任何事实基础的情况下，制定了分支预测策略来指导剩余的接触流程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:893`

### English

The one-sided communication greatly frustrated the diplomatic team. Eager for a successful first contact, they created branching prediction strategies for the remaining procedure without any factual information to inform the approach.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:944`

## first_contact.5090.desc.cautious

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5090.desc.cautious&lang=en)

### 中文

在登陆太空梭进行展示之后，紧接着是一阵焦虑，连[Leader.GetName]都公开怀疑[Leader.GetHeShe]是否成功地进行了介绍汇报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:894`

### English

An anxious wait followed the presentation aboard the shuttle, with [Leader.GetName] openly doubting if [Leader.GetHeShe] had made a successful introduction.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:945`

## first_contact.5090.desc.aggressive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5090.desc.aggressive&lang=en)

### 中文

幸亏来自[Leader.GetName]的直接命令阻止了第一次接触流程升级演变为暴力事件。对于团队来说，外星人持续的沉默无疑是一种侮辱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:895`

### English

Only the direct orders of [Leader.GetName] prevented the first contact protocol from escalating to violence. To the team, the obstinate silence of the aliens was nothing short of insulting.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:946`

## first_contact.5090.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5090.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

几个小时后，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!用我们的语言传达了一条简短的信息：\n\n“我们对你们的文化没有产生深刻印象。就这些吗？[Owner.GetEnvoy]有最后一次机会在你们选择的领域中给我们留下深刻印象。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:896`

### English

After several hours, the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! transmitted a short message in our language:\n\n"We are not impressed with your culture. Is this all? The [Owner.GetEnvoy] has a final chance to impress us in a field of your choosing."

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:947`

判决

### English

The Verdict
