## first_contact.5095.desc.combat_good

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5095.desc.combat_good&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!返回了！尽管伤痕累累，但却载誉而归。\n\n从一个隐藏的门中，一名外星战士突然现身并开始同我们的[Owner.GetEnvoy]展开搏斗，直到筋疲力尽。在最后一丝力量的召唤下，[Leader.GetName]设法制服了[Leader.GetHisHer]的对手。\n\n不到一个小时，[This.ContactCountry.GetName]发送了一条信息：\n\n“[Leader.GetName]将被记录在我们的史册中，作为一位伟大的战士。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:904`

### English

[Leader.GetName] returned from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! shuttle injured, but victorious.\n\nFrom a hidden door, an alien fighter emerged and proceeded to batter our [Owner.GetEnvoy] to the point of exhaustion. Summoning a last surge of strength, [Leader.GetName] managed to subdue [Leader.GetHisHer] opponent.\n\nWithin the hour, the [This.ContactCountry.GetName] sent a message:\n\n"[Leader.GetName] will be entered into our annals as a great warrior."

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:956`

## first_contact.5095.desc.combat_bad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5095.desc.combat_bad&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]迷惑地从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的太空梭返回，没有找到对手。在一个小时内，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!发送了一条消息：\n\n“你们野蛮的做法让我们感到失望。对于一个开化的文明来说，进行肉体上的战斗是非常不合适的。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

[Leader.GetName] returned from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! shuttle confused, having found no opponent. Within the hour, the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! sent a message:\n\n"Your barbaric ways disappoint us. A physical fight would be most unbecoming for a civilized culture."

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:957`

## first_contact.5095.desc.speech_good

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5095.desc.speech_good&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]满怀信心地从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的太空梭返回。[Leader.GetHeSheCap]在感应墙前为我们的情况辩论时，忘却了时间的概念，全面地解释了历史决策的各种原因以及我们作为一个文化如何学习和成长。\n\n不到一个小时，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!发来了一条消息：\n\n“尽管你们过去经历了困难，但你们取得了巨大的成长。你们的文化是一个鼓舞人心的榜样。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:906`

### English

[Leader.GetName] returned from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! shuttle brimming with conviction. [Leader.GetHeSheCap] had lost track of time arguing our case in front of the sensor wall, explaining the reasons for historical decisions and how we've learned and grown as a culture.\n\nWithin the hour, the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! sent a message:\n\n"Despite your troubled past, you have grown spectacularly. Your culture is an inspiration."

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:958`

## first_contact.5095.desc.speech_bad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5095.desc.speech_bad&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]失望地从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的太空梭返回。[Leader.GetHeSheCap]在感应墙前花了一个多小时，事无巨细地介绍我们的文化，并辩论我们历史决策的正确性。\n\n不久之后，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!发来了一条信息：\n\n“你所叙述的历史展示的是你的文化的贫瘠。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:907`

### English

[Leader.GetName] returned from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! shuttle disappointed. [Leader.GetHeSheCap] spent over an hour at the sensor wall presenting our culture in greater detail and arguing for the rightness of our historical decisions.\n\nShortly thereafter, the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! sent a message:\n\n"The history you describe paints a poor picture of your culture."

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:959`

## first_contact.5095.desc.song_good

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5095.desc.song_good&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的穿梭机上兴高采烈地回来了。描述我们过去和未来的即兴歌词自由地传唱着，这是由我们的[Owner.GetEnvoy]诗人在两者之间创造出了令人惊讶由具有启发的联系。\n\n不到一小时，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!发来了一条消息：\n\n“你们的文化非常辉煌，我们对这场表演表示赞赏。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:908`

### English

[Leader.GetName] returned from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! shuttle in a great mood. The improvised lyrics describing our past and future had flown freely, with our [Owner.GetEnvoy]-poet making associations both surprising and illuminating.\n\nWithin the hour, the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! sent a message:\n\n"Your culture is most splendid and we applaud the performance."

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:960`

## first_contact.5095.desc.song_bad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.5095.desc.song_bad&lang=en)

### 中文

[Leader.GetName]从§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的太空梭返回，[Leader.GetHisHer]的音乐表演让自己感到很不满。我们过去的行为与我们当前的自我认知之间的不和谐使得歌词的联想变得平淡无奇。\n\n不到一小时，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!发来了一条消息：\n\n“我们对一次重要的思想交流会被平庸的韵律破坏感到失望。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:909`

### English

[Leader.GetName] returned from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! shuttle dissatisfied with [Leader.GetHisHer] musical performance. The dissonance between our past actions and our current self-conception made the lyrical associations fall flat.\n\nWithin the hour, the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! sent a message:\n\n"We are disappointed that a momentous meeting of minds was spoiled by middling rhymes."

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:961`

## fcsum_monstrous_noises_silent_end_bad

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afcsum_monstrous_noises_silent_end_bad&lang=en)

第一次接触协议

### English

First Contact Protocols

## first_contact.1060.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1060.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近与外星生命形式的遭遇重新燃起了关于我们到底应该如何与潜在智慧外星文明发起接触的古老的争议，并且这个争议突然变得迫在眉睫了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:972`

### English

Our recent encounter with alien lifeforms has reignited - and made suddenly more urgent - the old debate on how we should approach contacting any potentially intelligent alien civilizations we may meet.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1026`

## first_contact.1060.desc.payback.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1060.desc.payback.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与外星生命体首次相遇了，这让我们对其他生命体保持警惕。太空中有生命，在探索星系的过程中，我们无疑会再次遇到智慧生命。但我们打招呼的方式仍有待商榷。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:973`

### English

Our first encounter with alien lifeforms left us wary of others. There's life out there, and while exploring the galaxy, we will doubtlessly meet intelligent life again. But the manner of our greeting is still up for debate.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1027`

## first_contact.1060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.1060.desc.base$\n\n尽管一些人提倡着力于通过发送和平的信息尽快建立友好关系，另一些声音则建议采取谨慎的态度并指出我们无从得知这些外星文明是否对我们心怀不轨，因而过早让他们过多了解我们是不明智的。\n\n还有一种更激进的团体敦促对其发起先发制人的行动。“为避免[This.GetSpeciesNamePlural]置身危险”他们说，“我们应该毫不犹豫、不择手段地在任何潜在的外星威胁前获取主动权。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:974`

### English

$first_contact.1060.desc.base$\n\nWhile some advocate focusing on establishing friendly relations as quickly as possible by contacting them with a message of peace, others advise caution, pointing out that we cannot know whether alien minds bear ill intent towards us and that it would be unwise to let them know too much about us before it is necessary.\n\nA third, more radical group, push for pre-emptive action against them: "With the security of all [This.GetSpeciesNamePlural] at risk," they say, "we dare not hesitate to take whatever measures necessary to gain the upper hand against any potential xeno threat."

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1028`

## first_contact.1060.desc.payback

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1060.desc.payback&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.1060.desc.payback.base$\n\n尽管一些人提倡尽快建立友好关系，另一些声音则建议小心谨慎，并指出我们的友好天性恰恰导致了我们遭到囚禁和背叛。\n\n还有一种更激进的团体敦促先发制人。“避免重蹈覆辙的最好方法就是先发制人，”他们说，“绝不再向异种人卑躬屈膝！”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:975`

### English

$first_contact.1060.desc.payback.base$\n\nWhile some advocate establishing friendly relations as quickly as possible, others advise caution, pointing out that our friendly nature is exactly what led us to imprisonment and betrayal.\n\nA third, more radical group, push for pre-emptive action: "the best way to avoid a second invasion is to hit them first," they say. "No more will we bow to the will of xenos!"

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1029`

## first_contact.1060.desc.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1060.desc.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.1060.desc.base$\n\n尽管一些人提倡着力于通过发送和平的信息尽快建立友好关系，另一些声音则建议采取谨慎的态度并指出我们无从得知这些外星文明是否对我们心怀不轨，因而过早让他们过多了解我们是不明智的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:976`

### English

$first_contact.1060.desc.base$\n\nWhile some advocate focusing on establishing friendly relations as quickly as possible by contacting them with a message of peace, others advise caution, pointing out that we cannot know whether alien minds bear ill intent towards us and that it would be unwise to let them know too much about us before it is necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1030`

## first_contact.1060.desc.payback.xenophile

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1060.desc.payback.xenophile&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.1060.desc.base.payback$\n\n虽然我们大多数人都想相信外太空有善良的文明，但其他人却建议谨慎行事，并指出我们的友好天性恰恰导致了我们遭到囚禁和背叛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:977`

### English

$first_contact.1060.desc.base.payback$\n\nWhile most of us want to believe there are kind civilizations out there, others advise caution, pointing out our friendly nature is exactly what led us to imprisonment and betrayal.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1031`

## first_contact.1060.desc.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1060.desc.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.1060.desc.base$\n\n尽管一些人提倡对其发起先发制人的行动，另一些声音则建议采取谨慎的态度并指出我们在与之进入敌对状态之前了解更多关于这些外星人的能力是更明智之举。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:978`

### English

$first_contact.1060.desc.base$\n\nWhile some advocate taking hostile pre-emptive action against them, others advise caution, pointing out that it would be wise to learn more about the aliens' capabilities before we risk antagonizing them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1032`

## first_contact.1060.desc.payback.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1060.desc.payback.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact.1060.desc.payback.base$\n\n大家的反应是一致的：避免重蹈覆辙的最好办法就是在他们能够伤害我们之前先发制人。我们不会再向异种卑躬屈膝了!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:979`

### English

$first_contact.1060.desc.payback.base$\n\nThe response is unanimous: the best way to avoid a second invasion is to assert dominance over others before they can harm us. No more will we bow to the will of xenos!

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1033`

## first_contact.1060.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1060.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近与外星生命形式的遭遇对我们如何与遭遇到的任何潜在外星智慧文明提出了需求。\n\n我们的其中一种可行方式是通过发送必要的信息使其便于翻译我们的通讯，以求尽快与之建立和平的接触。然而这也可能在对方对我们不怀好意时显得过于轻率。\n\n最安全的策略是小心行事并试图在不暴露过多我们的信息的前提下与之沟通。另一方面，我们也可以采取更激进的举措以进一步了解他们并且将其封锁在我们国境之外，然而这样会与其陷入对立。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:980`

### English

Our recent encounter with alien lifeforms has brought forward the need for us to decide how we should act towards any potentially intelligent alien civilizations we may meet.\n\nOne possibility would be for us to seek to establish peaceful contact with them as soon as possible by sending them the information necessary for them to translate our communications. This may, however, prove imprudent should they have hostile intentions towards us.\n\nThe safest option would be to proceed with caution and attempt to communicate with them without giving them too much information on ourselves. On the other hand, we could make more aggressive moves to both find out more about them and ward them off from our territories; however, this would be sure to antagonize them.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1034`

## opinion_friendly_first_comms_greeting

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aopinion_friendly_first_comms_greeting&lang=en)

### 中文

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1018`

### English

Our encounter with a particularly attractive alien species has caused a surge in volunteers for our Interstellar Exploration department.

Our encounter with a civilization of robots has left many skeptical about the influence of automation on our society.

我们遇到了一个相当令人作呕的外星种族，使得人们纷纷自闭只关注本族，不再愿意去关注外星生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1024`

### English

Our encounter with a particularly repulsive alien species has persuaded many to look inwards to our own rather than outwards towards others.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1079`

## repulsive_aliens_uncondemned_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Arepulsive_aliens_uncondemned_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能对我们遭遇到的新威胁表现出反抗态度，民众内部开始出现反对意见。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1028`

### English

Our failure to express opposition to the worrying new threat we have encountered has sparked opposition among our populace.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1083`

## first_contact_challenge_not_returned

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_challenge_not_returned&lang=en)

### 中文

挑衅未回应

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1029`

### English

Challenge Not Returned

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1084`

## first_contact_challenge_not_returned_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_challenge_not_returned_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然有些人担心，我们可能会因为在与恶意文明交流时因不先发制人而使自己处于危险之中，但我们仍坚决致力于以科学合理的方式处理第一次接触，因为这对我们的探索部门而言有明显的好处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1030`

### English

Our steadfast dedication to handling first contact in a rational and scientific manner is a major boon to our exploration department, although some worry that we may be putting ourselves at risk by not preempting conflict with apparently hostile civilizations.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1085`

## first_contact_defeated_dadaism

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_defeated_dadaism&lang=en)

### 中文

挫败荒唐

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1031`

### English

Defeated Absurdism

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1086`

## first_contact_defeated_dadaism_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_defeated_dadaism_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个特别值得纪念的第一次接触事件，我们被迫掌握了一种荒诞不经的回应方式。现在对我们的语言学家们来说再也没有什么是难以破译的了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1032`

### English

A particularly memorable first contact exchange forced us to come to grips with absurdist responses. There is now no challenge too great for our linguists to decode.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1087`

## first_contact_war_preparations

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_war_preparations&lang=en)

### 中文

战争准备

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1033`

### English

War Preparations

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1088`

## first_contact_war_preparations_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_war_preparations_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与显然敌意的文明的遭遇激起了广泛的战争准备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1034`

### English

Our encounter with an apparently hostile civilization has sparked widespread preparations for war.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1089`

## first_contact_warlike_warriors_recognised

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_warlike_warriors_recognised&lang=en)

### 中文

英武得到认可

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1035`

### English

Warlike Prowess Recognized

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1090`

## first_contact_warlike_warriors_recognised_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_warlike_warriors_recognised_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近遭遇的文明对我们的英武印象深刻，因此他们宣称我们是全星系里最强的战士。这对我们的声誉没有什么影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1036`

### English

A civilization we recently encountered was so impressed by our prowess as warriors that they declared us to be among the finest fighters of the galaxy. This has done our reputation no harm whatsoever.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1091`

## first_contact_warlike_warriors_presumed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_warlike_warriors_presumed&lang=en)

### 中文

被误以为英武

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1037`

### English

Warlike Prowess Presumed

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1092`

## first_contact_warlike_warriors_presumed_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_warlike_warriors_presumed_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近遭遇的文明误会我们是一个由强壮且聪慧的武士所构建的社会。尽管我们并非如此，但我们仍会在一段时间内占有这一误会的好处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1038`

### English

A civilization we recently encountered has mistaken us for a society of strong and brave warriors. While we are nothing of the sort, we can benefit from this mistaken impression, for a time.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1093`

## first_contact_warlike_warriors_recognised_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_warlike_warriors_recognised_2&lang=en)

### 中文

战士间的宿敌关系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1039`

### English

Rivalry Between Warriors

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1094`

## first_contact_warlike_warriors_recognised_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_warlike_warriors_recognised_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近遭遇的一个文明对我们的英武印象深刻，因此他们发誓要努力与我们匹敌，并在星系舞台上宣称我们为宿敌。这对我们的声誉没有什么影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1040`

### English

A civilization we recently encountered was so impressed by our prowess as warriors that they vowed to strive to match our valor, declaring us their rivals on the galactic stage. This has done our reputation no harm whatsoever.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1095`

## first_contact_warlike_warriors_presumed_2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_warlike_warriors_presumed_2&lang=en)

### 中文

（虚假的）战士间的宿敌关系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1041`

### English

Rivalry Between (Presumed) Warriors

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1096`

## first_contact_warlike_warriors_presumed_2_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_warlike_warriors_presumed_2_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近遭遇的文明误会我们是一个由强壮且聪慧的武士所构建的社会。不幸的是，他们发誓要努力与我们相匹敌，并在星系舞台上宣称我们为宿敌。我们并非如此……但我们仍会在一段时间内占有这一误会的好处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1042`

### English

A civilization we recently encountered has mistaken us for a society of strong and brave warriors. Unfortunately, they claim they will strive to match our valor, declaring us their rivals on the galactic stage. Also, we are nothing of the sort... but at least we can benefit from this mistaken impression, for a time.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1097`

## first_contact_level_headedness

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_level_headedness&lang=en)

### 中文

万众瞩目

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1043`

### English

Good in a Tight Spot

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1098`

## first_contact_level_headedness_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_level_headedness_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在面对困境时的冷静沉着在星系舞台上获得广泛钦佩。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1044`

### English

Our level-headedness in the face of adversity is widely admired on the galactic stage.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1099`

## first_comms_hostility_preparations

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_comms_hostility_preparations&lang=en)

为敌对行为做准备

### English

Preparations for Hostile Action

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_comms_defensive&lang=en)

警觉的反间谍行动

### English

Watchful Counterespionage

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1101`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_comms_defensive_desc&lang=en)

在首次接触后，目标就对我们持有相当的戒心。

Our espionage targets view us with a great deal of suspicion, having only recently first met us.

语言相近

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1048`

### English

Speak Like Us

## first_contact_speak_like_us_country

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_speak_like_us_country&lang=en)

### 中文

解谜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1049`

### English

Riddles Decoded

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1104`

## first_contact_speak_like_us_country_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_speak_like_us_country_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在破译由一个特别令人困惑的文明发给我们的谜语方面的经验，改善了我们应对初次接触的方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1050`

### English

Our experience in decoding riddles sent to us by a particularly confusing civilization has allowed us to strengthen our protocols for first encounters.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1105`

## universal_translator

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auniversal_translator&lang=en)

### 中文

万能翻译器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1051`

### English

Universal Translator

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1106`

## universal_translator_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Auniversal_translator_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然所谓的“万能翻译器”没有达到我们研究人员最初承诺的水准，即立即达成与任何智能物种的交流，但它仍然不失为一种非常有用的交流工具，其现已成为驻各国特使和社会科学家所采用的标准设备。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1052`

### English

Although the so-called "Universal Translator" falls short of the initial promises of our researchers to instantly facilitate communication with any and all intelligent species, it is nevertheless a highly useful communication tool that now forms part of the standard equipment of our envoys and social scientists.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1107`

## space_lights_insights

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspace_lights_insights&lang=en)

### 中文

空间流动预测

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1053`

### English

Interstellar Flow Prediction

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1108`

## space_lights_insights_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aspace_lights_insights_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一种我们之前遇到的物种的发光皮肤上的图案给了我们对于新知识的关键启示，使我们对超空间旅行的理解有了“光年级别”的进步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1054`

### English

Our understanding of hyperlane travel has been advanced by light years following the revelation that a species we met held some keys to new knowledge in the patterns they made on their skins.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1109`

## first_contact_hive_drones_study_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_hive_drones_study_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们正研究蜂巢思维如何控制他们的子个体以探寻我们可以用于增加我们工人生产效率的办法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1056`

### English

We are studying the ways a hive mind controls its drones in the search for methods we can use to increase the efficiency of our own workers.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1111`

## first_contact_critters.1.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.1.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有规律的信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1081`

### English

Structured Signals

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1139`

## first_contact_critters.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了来源不明的读数。这些信号似乎遵循着某种重复的句法，证明其来源应为智慧生物。我们应进一步研究它。它们似乎集中在一个空间站附近，但尚不清楚这个空间站是不是一个更大的太空文明的一部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1082`

### English

We have picked up readings of unknown nature. The signals seem to follow some kind of repeating syntax which could indicate sapient origin. It should be investigated further. They seem to be concentrated around a single space station, though it is unclear whether this forms part of a larger spacefaring civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1140`

## first_contact_critters.1.desc.cfl

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.1.desc.cfl&lang=en)

### 中文

我们探测到了性质不明的读数。这些信号似乎遵循某种重复的句法，这可能表明其源自智慧生命。我们应对此进行进一步调查。信号似乎集中在一艘大型舰船周围，但目前尚不清楚这是否属于某个更庞大的星际文明的一部分。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1083`

### English

We have picked up readings of unknown nature. The signals seem to follow some kind of repeating syntax which could indicate sapient origin. It should be investigated further. They seem to be concentrated around a single large vessel, though it is unclear whether this forms part of a larger spacefaring civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1141`

## first_contact_critters.5.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.5.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在成功的翻译了他们的语言之后，我们与§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!的独立空间站建立了通讯。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1086`

### English

After successfully translating their language, we have established communications with the independent space station of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1144`

## first_contact_critters.5.A

### English

## first_contact_critters.10.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.10.name&lang=en)

### 中文

怪异热源

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1089`

### English

Weird Heat Blobs

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1147`

## first_contact_critters.10.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.10.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

长程扫描仪获取了[This.System.GetName]恒星系中的数据，对其的解析几乎说明它是一个巨大热源。这非常值得研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1090`

### English

Long range scanners have picked up readings in the [This.System.GetName] system that could best be described as giant heat blobs. It's worth investigating.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1148`

## first_contact_critters.10.A

### English

## first_contact_critters.15.name

### 中文

$story.501.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1092`

### English

$story.501.name$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1150`

## first_contact_critters.15.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.15.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]的社会学研究领袖称之为§H缇扬奇§!”的太空生物十分温顺。它们可以进入低维度的亚空间，因此可以轻易地在星系之间遨游。\n\n它们以气态巨行星外层常见的气体为食。这不太可能是它们唯一的食物来源，但到目前为止尚未观察到其他的进食方式。\n\n它们不会主动进攻，因此可以安全地无视它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1093`

### English

The spaceborne lifeforms - which the [Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] Head of Society Research has come to refer to as "§HTiyanki§!" - are docile creatures. Capable of accessing some lower dimension of subspace, they roam from system to system with remarkable ease.\n\nThey graze on gases common to the upper layers of many gas giants. It is highly unlikely, to say the least, that this is their only food source, but intake of other nutrients has yet to be observed.\n\nThey will rarely, if ever, attack, even when provoked. They can safely be ignored.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1151`

## first_contact_critters.20.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.20.name&lang=en)

### 中文

大而美味

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1097`

### English

Big and Testy

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1155`

## first_contact_critters.20.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.20.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们进入[This.System.GetName]时，传感器获取了一个奇怪的数据。一开始把它们当成发光的小行星而忽视了，但后来当发现它们会对太空中的移动物体哪怕距离很远都会做出一定的进攻性反应，事情就变得有趣了起来。\n\n建议最好从安全的距离上观察这些东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1098`

### English

Upon entering [This.System.GetName] our sensors picked up strange readings. Dismissed at first as glorified asteroids, things quickly changed when it was discovered that these entities reacted aggressively to movement in space - even at great distance!\n\nObservation of these entities - preferably from a safe distance - is advised.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1156`

## first_contact_critters.20.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.20.A&lang=en)

### 中文

最好从远处观察，继续吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1099`

### English

Best viewed from afar. Keep it up.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1157`

## first_contact_critters.25.name

$story.101.name$

### English

$story.101.name$

## first_contact_critters.25.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.25.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队此前遭遇到的实体是一种新发现的太空生命形式。\n\n由于最初对传感器数据的粗略误读，我们很快就给它们起昵称为§H“太空变形虫”§!，实际上这种生物比[Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]护卫舰的平均尺寸还要大。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1101`

### English

The entities encountered by our fleets some time ago are new, spaceborne lifeforms.\n\nQuickly nicknamed §H"Space Amoeba"§! following a gross misreading of initial sensor output, the creature is in fact larger than the average [Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] corvette.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1159`

## first_contact_critters.25.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.25.A&lang=en)

### 中文

明白了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1102`

### English

Understood.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1160`

## first_contact_critters.30.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.30.name&lang=en)

### 中文

活着的晶体？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1104`

### English

Living Crystals?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1162`

## first_contact_critters.30.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.30.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们之前用§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!指称的不规则物体看起来一定程度上由晶态物质组成。有趣的是，它们可以在虚空中推进，并能在必要的时候保护自己，说明他们应该是具有生命的。我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]正在做进一步的调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1105`

### English

The irregularity we had designated as §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! appears to be made of some sort of crystalline material. Curiously, it seems to be able to propel itself across the void and protect itself when necessary, indicating that it may be alive. Our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] are working hard to find out more.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1163`

## first_contact_critters.30.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.30.A&lang=en)

### 中文

继续调查。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1106`

### English

Keep at it.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1164`

## first_contact_critters.40.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.40.name&lang=en)

### 中文

太空自动设施

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1108`

### English

Space Automata

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1166`

## first_contact_critters.40.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.40.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[This.System.GetName]发现了一种新的生命形式，虽然称之为“生命”可能不太准确。这些实体看起来完全是一种完全出于未知的工业用途的全自动无人机器人。\n\n我们会继续观察，但由于这些机器有自我保护的编码，所以最好谨慎行事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1109`

### English

We have discovered a new lifeform in [This.System.GetName], although calling it a "lifeform" may be incorrect. The entities appear to be fully automated, mechanical drones built exclusively for some unknown industrial purpose.\n\nObservations will continue, but since it's likely that these machines are programmed with defensive protocols, it would be prudent to tread lightly.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1167`

## first_contact_critters.40.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.40.A&lang=en)

### 中文

继续观察，小心行事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1110`

### English

Keep looking, but use caution.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1168`

## first_contact_critters.45.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.45.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$story.301.name$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1111`

### English

$story.301.name$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1169`

## first_contact_critters.45.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.45.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对关于此前遭遇的深空无人机的报告进行了进一步的调查。这些无人机似乎是数百万年前建造的自动轨道采矿作业的工人和监工。这些采矿作业似乎开始于数千年前，然后很快被抛弃，只留下了无人机继续运作。\n\n这些无人机拥有强力的采矿激光，并且在[Owner.GetSpeciesAdj]舰船试图靠近时表现出了明显具有威胁性，甚至是侵略性的举动。或许它们只是些老旧的无人机，但[Owner.GetName]仍应该密切观察它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1112`

### English

Reports of the deep-space drones previously encountered have been investigated closer. The drones appear to be the workers and custodians of an autonomous orbital mining operation, established millennia ago and then soon abandoned by all but the drones themselves, judging by the state of the processing equipment.\n\nThe drones possess powerful mining lasers and make obviously threatening, if not outright hostile, overtures toward [Owner.GetSpeciesAdj] vessels when approached. They may only be old drones, but they should be kept under close watch by the [Owner.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1170`

## first_contact_critters.45.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.45.A&lang=en)

### 中文

明白了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1113`

### English

Understood.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1171`

## first_contact_critters.50.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.50.name&lang=en)

### 中文

野蛮迹象

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1115`

### English

Signs of Barbarism

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1173`

## first_contact_critters.50.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.50.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管他们生活在居住站上的事实暗示了他们很可能是一个先进的社会，然而[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]不能肯定我们是不是真的在和一种智慧生命形式打交道：他们似乎无时不刻在展现敌意，这对于一个稳定的社会而言是不可想象的：即便在我们短暂的调查期间也展现了数次纯粹的野蛮行为。\n\n他们看起来具有语言，但我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]仍在努力解析当中，以便我们在未来能够理解他们的行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1116`

### English

Although their dwellings on habitat stations would suggest an advanced society, [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] [This.Leader.GetName] is unsure whether we are indeed dealing with an intelligent lifeform: they seem unremittingly hostile, so much so that a stable society seems almost unthinkable: even during our brief period of investigation, we have observed several displays of pure barbarism.\n\nThey seem to have a language, but our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] are still working on deciphering it so we can make sense of this display.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1174`

## first_contact_critters.50.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.50.A&lang=en)

### 中文

真令人不舒服。我们可得小心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1117`

### English

Disturbing. We must be watchful.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1175`

## first_contact_critters.60.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.60.name&lang=en)

### 中文

敌对舰船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1118`

### English

Hostile Vessels

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1176`

## first_contact_critters.60.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.60.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[This.System.GetName]遇到的舰船似乎有点过时，但仍然武装到了牙齿。这些舰船应该是有人驾驶的，但对任何靠近的物体都展现了敌意。有必要谨慎应对……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1119`

### English

The vessels we encountered in the [This.System.GetName] system appear to be somewhat outmoded, but nevertheless armed to the teeth. They seem to be piloted by sentient crews but respond with hostility to any approach. We will have to treat them with caution.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1177`

## first_contact_critters.65.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.65.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们仍然无法与他们建立联络，主要是因为他们并不想理我们，但我们已经可以确认§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!是海盗。视觉指示器表明他们的文化很大程度上是生长于太空的，我们观察到的一些表现形式不符合任何一种居住于行星的文明。\n\n不幸的是，他们的船员似乎对民事交流没有任何兴趣。我们从截获的舰见通讯也表明他们主要以海盗活动为生，偶尔也会招募部分文明边缘的元素到他们的贼船上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1120`

### English

Although we have been unable to establish communications with them - mainly because they are unwilling to communicate with us - we have been able to identify the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! as pirates. Visual indicators point to their culture being largely spaceborne, and some phenotypes observed among them match no known planet-dwelling civilizations.\n\nUnfortunately, their crews are completely uninterested in civil discourse. What little we have been able to glean from intercepting intra-fleet communications indicate that they primarily subsist on piracy, occasionally recruiting fringe elements of spacefaring civilizations to their cause.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1178`

## first_contact_critters.70.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.name&lang=en)

### 中文

未知舰船

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1121`

### English

Unknown Vessels

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1179`

## first_contact_critters.70.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[This.System.GetName]恒星系中侦测到的舰队似乎是某种目的不明的跨星系探险队。他们没有采取任何有敌意的举动，也没有任何显而易见的重型武器，反而他们似乎在反复但无目的地向我们的大致方向广播某种具有实际内容的信号。我们的[This.Owner.GetEnvoy]，[This.Leader.GetName]很困惑，但希望那些广播能帮助我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]翻译他们的语言。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1122`

### English

The vessels detected in the [This.System.GetName] system seem to be part of some sort of expedition across the galaxy, with purposes unknown. They have not made any hostile moves, nor is there any heavy weaponry in evidence - instead, they seem to be broadcasting repetitive but not particularly content-filled signals in our general direction. Our [This.Owner.GetEnvoy], [This.Leader.GetName], is confused, but hopes that the broadcasts will help our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] translate their language.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1180`

## first_contact_critters.70.desc.home

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.desc.home&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.System.GetName]恒星系似乎是某种未知起源的先进社会的家园。居住在恒星系的一系列空间站中，[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]留意到还有不少飞船在其间穿梭，同时还有一些目的不明的更大的远航舰队离开恒星系。他们似乎在向我们大致方向不断传播着重复的但没有实际内容的信号，这一进展令我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]感到困惑，但这有助于翻译他们的语言。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1123`

### English

The [This.System.GetName] system appears to be home to an advanced society of unknown origins. Dwelling in a series of space stations across the system, [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] [This.Leader.GetName] has observed multiple crafts flitting between them, as well as several larger expeditions leaving the system for unknown purposes. They seem to be broadcasting repetitive but not particularly content-filled signals in our general direction, a development which has confused our [This.Owner.GetLinguists], but may help them to translate their language.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1181`

## first_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic.base&lang=en)

### 中文

在[This.System.GetName]星系中探测到的舰船似乎正在进行某种横跨星系的远征，这种生活方式对我们自己的族群而言并不陌生。他们没有表现出任何敌意，而是不断向我们方向重复广播信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1124`

### English

The vessels detected in the [This.System.GetName] system appear to be on some kind of expedition across the galaxy, a way of life our own people know well. They have made no hostile moves, broadcasting repetitive signals in our direction instead.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1182`

## first_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic.base$\n\n看到一个族群将家园安于星海之间而非任何星球之上，这种景象令人感到几分莫名的亲切。我们的[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]迫切希望能够进一步了解这些同为旅人的族群。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1125`

### English

$first_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic.base$\n\nThere is something almost familiar about the sight of a people making their home among the stars rather than on any world. Our [This.Owner.GetEnvoy], [This.Leader.GetName], is eager to learn more about these fellow travelers.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1183`

## first_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic.unfriendly

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic.unfriendly&lang=en)

### 中文

$first_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic.base$\n\n一群流浪者，和我们没什么不一样，但却不太受人欢迎。[This.Owner.GetEnvoy]，[This.Leader.GetName]对此不太感兴趣，但也愿意听听他们要说什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1126`

### English

$first_contact_critters.70.desc.nomadic.base$\n\nWanderers, not unlike ourselves, though the comparison is an unwelcome one. Our [This.Owner.GetEnvoy], [This.Leader.GetName], is unimpressed, but will hear what they have to say.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1184`

## first_contact_critters.70.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.A&lang=en)

### 中文

令人好奇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1127`

### English

Curious.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1185`

## first_contact_critters.70.B

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.B&lang=en)

### 中文

令人恼火。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1128`

### English

Troubling.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1186`

## first_contact_critters.70.C

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.C&lang=en)

### 中文

蜂巢外的这些东西的行为确实很奇怪。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1129`

### English

The actions of those outside the hive are indeed strange.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1187`

## first_contact_critters.70.D

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.D&lang=en)

### 中文

我们怎么才能彻底了解有机体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1130`

### English

How can we fathom the ways of organic life?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1188`

## first_contact_critters.70.E

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.70.E&lang=en)

### 中文

他们已经声明了自己在这片区域的航路吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1131`

### English

Have they already claimed this region's Wayline routes?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1189`

## first_contact_critters.75.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.75.name&lang=en)

### 中文

行商

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1132`

### English

The Caravaneers

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1190`

## first_contact_critters.75.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.75.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功地破译了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!向我们发送的令人困惑的重复信息，我们发现这些信息是一些……商业广告！显然他们自称为§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!，是一个以向偶遇的任何文明提供最优惠的交易谋生的星际商团的一部分，至少他们自称是最优惠的。一如既往，他们似乎正在尝试联系我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1133`

### English

We have managed to decipher the confusing, repetitive messages the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! were sending us, and it turns out that they were... commercial advertisements! Apparently they call themselves §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! and are part of a group of spacefaring traders that make a living by offering the best deals - or what they claim to be the best deals - to whichever civilization they happen to be visiting at the time. True to form, it appears they are attempting to contact us.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1191`

## first_contact_critters.75.desc.home

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.75.desc.home&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经成功地破译了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!向我们发送的，一遍又一遍的，令人困惑的信息，我们发现这些信息竟是一些……商业广告！似乎[This.System.GetName]恒星系是一个自称§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!的星际商团的母星系。显然，他们以向偶遇的任何文明提供最优惠的交易谋生，至少他们自称是最优惠的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1134`

### English

We have managed to decipher the confusing, repetitive messages the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! were sending us, and it turns out that they were... commercial advertisements! It appears that the [This.System.GetName] system is the home system of a group of spacefaring traders that call themselves the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!. They apparently make a living by offering the best deals - or what they claim to be the best deals - to whichever civilization they happen to be visiting at the time.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1192`

## first_contact_critters.75.B

### English

## first_contact_critters.80.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.80.name&lang=en)

### 中文

活的星际碎片？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1137`

### English

Living Space Debris?

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1195`

## first_contact_critters.80.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.80.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们之前用§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!指称的不规则物体可能是一种有机生物，[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap][This.Leader.GetName]仍在努力验证这一假说。尽管它看起来就是一些随机的星际物质组成的团簇，但毋庸置疑地它们可以在虚空中推进，并展现出了一定程度的自主智能。我们的[This.Owner.GetResearchers]为这种现象困惑，并正在努力验证获得的数据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1138`

### English

[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap] [This.Leader.GetName] is still working to confirm the hypothesis that the irregularity we had designated as §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! is in fact a living organism. While it seems to be made up of a cluster of random star dust, it is demonstrably able to propel itself across the void and shows basic signs of independent agency. Our [This.Owner.GetResearchers] are perplexed by the phenomenon, and are working hard to verify our readings.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1196`

## first_contact_critters.80.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.80.A&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一定搞错了什么……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1139`

### English

We must be missing something...

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1197`

## first_contact_critters.90.name

### 中文

$first_contact_critters.70.name$

### English

$first_contact_critters.70.name$

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1198`

## first_contact_critters.90.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.90.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[This.System.GetName]恒星系中侦测到的舰队似乎是某种目的不明的跨星系远航舰队的一部分。他们没有采取任何有敌意的举动，也没有任何显而易见的重型武器。然而他们似乎完全缄默不语，我们无法从他们当中获取任何信号。我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]相信他们可以从我们拾获的只言片语中翻译他们的语言，当然这可能要花好长时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1141`

### English

The vessels detected in the [This.System.GetName] system seem to be part of some sort of expedition across the galaxy, with purposes unknown. They have not made any hostile moves, nor is there any heavy weaponry in evidence. However, they have been fairly closed-mouthed, and we have been unable to pick up many signals from them. Our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] are confident that they will be able to translate their language eventually from the scraps we pick up. However, this may take quite some time.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1199`

## first_contact_critters.95.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.95.name&lang=en)

### 中文

游牧民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1142`

### English

The Nomads

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1200`

## first_contact_critters.95.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.95.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们成功地破解了神秘的§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!语言。我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]被难住了好长时间，直到一次他们开始向我们的大致方向上广播时才取得了突破。广播的内容是一条欢迎讯息，其中他们自称为§H“那马里安”§!。看来他们现在愿意与我们交流了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1143`

### English

We have managed to decipher the language of the mysterious §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. Our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] remained stumped for quite some time, and a breakthrough was only made once they began broadcasting what turns out to be a welcome message identifying them as the §H"Namarians"§! in our general direction. It appears that they are now eager to talk to us.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1201`

## first_contact_critters.100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[This.System.GetName]星系遭遇的舰队与我们此前在此区域见到的任何东西都不一样。它没有表现出攻击性，但我们眼下只能猜测它的起源或目的。我们最有把握的猜测是，该舰队代表的派别与任何地方当局或文明都没有正式联系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1144`

### English

The fleet we encountered in the [This.System.GetName] system is unlike any we've seen in this region of space before. It does not appear to be aggressive, but we can only speculate as to its origin or purpose here. Our best guess is that the fleet represents a faction without formal ties to any local authority or civilization.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1202`

## first_contact_critters.100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谨慎行事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1145`

### English

Proceed with caution.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1203`

## first_contact_critters.105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

中立舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1146`

### English

Neutral Fleet

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1204`

## first_contact_critters.105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact_critters.105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与这支自称是§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!的舰队陷入了僵局。这个不寻常的来客对我们大部分的外交姿态置之不理，但我们可以推断它代表一个独立于星系文明的中立实体。\n\n我们推测，若舰队有传统意义上的所有者，则需要采取非常措施才能接近他们。考虑到这一点，我们总结了目前的发现，供未来的[this.owner.GetScientistPlural]团队使用（如有必要）。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/nemesis_intel_l_simp_chinese.yml:1147`

### English

We have reached an impasse with the fleet identifying itself as the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!. This unusual presence ignored most of our diplomatic overtures, but we can infer that it represents a neutral entity which stands apart from galactic civilization.\n\nWe suspect that if the fleet possess any owners in the conventional sense, it would take extraordinary measures to reach them. With this in mind, we have summarized our findings for future teams of [this.owner.GetScientistPlural] to make use of - in case such an opportunity does arise.

Source: `localisation/english/nemesis_intel_l_english.yml:1205`
